  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/config.js?v=12"></script>
  <script src="./assets/auth.js?v=13"></script>
  <script src="./assets/profile.js?v=18"></script>
  <script src="./assets/avatar.js?v=7"></script>
  <script src="./assets/nav.js?v=6"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = $('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }

    function fmtPrice(coin, n){
      if (n == null || !isFinite(n)) return '–';
      if (coin === 'bobai') return '$' + n.toFixed(10).replace(/0+$/,'').replace(/\.$/,'');
      if (coin === 'btc')   return '$' + n.toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
      return '$' + n.toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 4 });
    }

    let saved = { btc: null, bnb: null, bobai: null };
    let pending = { btc: null, bnb: null, bobai: null };
    let live = { btc: null, bnb: null, bobai: null };
    let locked = false;

    function isLocked(){
      // One-time MD2 extension — picks editable until 15 min before the first
      // matchday-2 match (CZ–ZA, 2026-06-18 16:00 UTC). DB-enforced via the
      // hardcoded timestamp in wc_crypto policies. Picks reveal at that moment.
      const lockMs = new Date(window.WC_CONFIG.BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC).getTime();
      return Date.now() >= lockMs;
    }

    function updateSaveBar(){
      const filled = ['btc','bnb','bobai'].filter(k => pending[k] != null).length;
      $('#answered-count').textContent = `${filled}/3`;
      const dirty = ['btc','bnb','bobai'].some(k => (pending[k] || null) !== (saved[k] || null));
      $('#btn-save').disabled = !dirty || locked;
      $('#save-status').textContent =
        locked ? '🔒 Locked — final lock has passed.'
        : dirty ? 'Unsaved changes.'
        : filled === 0 ? 'Pick at least one.'
        : '✅ Up to date.';
    }

    function readInputs(){
      ['btc','bnb','bobai'].forEach(k => {
        const el = $(`[data-input="${k}"]`);
        const v = el.value.trim();
        if (v === '') { pending[k] = null; return; }
        const n = parseFloat(v);
        pending[k] = isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? n : null;
      });
    }

    async function refreshLive(){
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.fetchLivePrices();
      live = r;
      ['btc','bnb','bobai'].forEach(k => {
        const el = $(`[data-live="${k}"]`);
        const next = fmtPrice(k, live[k]);
        if (el.textContent !== next) {
          el.textContent = next;
          el.classList.remove('pulse'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('pulse');
        }
      });
    }

    // Helper: format a numeric price for the input field at coin-appropriate precision
    function formatForInput(coin, n){
      if (coin === 'bobai') return n.toFixed(10).replace(/0+$/,'').replace(/\.$/,'');
      return n.toFixed(coin === 'btc' ? 2 : 4);
    }

    // wire preset buttons (use live + ±% deltas)
    $$('[data-preset]').forEach(btn => btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const k = btn.dataset.preset;
      const pct = parseFloat(btn.dataset.pct);
      if (live[k] == null) return;
      const target = live[k] * (1 + pct / 100);
      if (!isFinite(target) || target < 0) return;
      const inp = $(`[data-input="${k}"]`);
      inp.value = formatForInput(k, target);
      readInputs(); updateSaveBar();
    }));

    // input listeners
    $$('[data-input]').forEach(inp => inp.addEventListener('input', () => { readInputs(); updateSaveBar(); }));

    $('#btn-save').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const btn = $('#btn-save');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving…';
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.saveCrypto(pending);
      btn.textContent = 'Save predictions';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); btn.disabled = false; return; }
      saved = { ...pending };
      showAlert('ok', 'Predictions saved.');
      updateSaveBar();
      setTimeout(() => showAlert(), 2500);
    });

    (async () => {
      const me = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      if (!me) { window.location.href = '/worldcup/'; return; }

      locked = isLocked();
      if (locked) $('#locked').classList.remove('hidden');
      if (locked) $$('[data-input], [data-preset]').forEach(el => el.disabled = true);

      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.getCrypto();
      if (r.crypto) {
        ['btc','bnb','bobai'].forEach(k => {
          const dbKey = k + '_price';
          if (r.crypto[dbKey] != null) {
            saved[k] = parseFloat(r.crypto[dbKey]);
            pending[k] = saved[k];
            $(`[data-input="${k}"]`).value = saved[k];
          }
        });
      }
      updateSaveBar();
      await refreshLive();
      setInterval(refreshLive, 20000); // refresh live every 20s
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    (function(){
      document.documentElement.classList.add('wc-gate');
      function hasSession(){
        try {
          return Object.keys(localStorage).some(function(k){
            return k.indexOf('sb-')===0 && k.lastIndexOf('-auth-token')===k.length-11 && !!localStorage.getItem(k);
          });
        } catch(e){ return true; }
      }
      if (!hasSession()){ window.location.replace('/worldcup/'); return; }
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('wc-gate');
      window.addEventListener('pageshow', function(e){
        if (e.persisted && !hasSession()) window.location.replace('/worldcup/');
      });
    })();
  </script>

              <label for="w-addr-in" style="display:block;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);letter-spacing:.5px;margin-bottom:6px">Your BNB Smart Chain (BEP-20) address</label>
              <input id="w-addr-in" type="text" placeholder="0x…" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" style="width:100%;padding:12px 14px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.4);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;color:var(--text);font-size:13px;font-family:'Space Grotesk','Courier New',monospace">
              <button id="w-link" type="button" class="btn sm" style="margin-top:10px" disabled>Link wallet…</button>
              <div class="lock-note">
                <span class="ic">⚠</span>
                <span>Double-check the address before confirming. Once linked it cannot be changed or removed — only the owner of this account can use this wallet for payouts.</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- LINKED -->
            <div id="wallet-set" class="hidden">
              <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:6px">
                <span style="color:var(--green);font-size:16px">✓</span>
                <strong style="font-size:14px">Wallet linked</strong>
              </div>
              <div class="wallet-addr" id="w-addr">—</div>
              <p class="hint" style="margin:4px 0 0">Permanent link — this wallet now belongs to your account for all payouts.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Confirm modal -->
        <div id="confirm-modal" class="modal-back" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="confirm-title">
          <div class="modal">
            <h4 id="confirm-title">Confirm wallet link</h4>
            <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text);line-height:1.5;margin:0 0 4px">You're about to permanently link this address to your account:</p>
            <div class="confirm-addr" id="confirm-addr">—</div>
            <label class="check-row"><input type="checkbox" id="confirm-c1"><span>The address above is correct and I control this wallet.</span></label>
            <label class="check-row"><input type="checkbox" id="confirm-c2"><span>I understand the link is <b>permanent</b> — I cannot switch or remove this wallet later.</span></label>
            <label class="check-row"><input type="checkbox" id="confirm-c3"><span>I am not using this wallet on another BOBAI Worldcup account.</span></label>
            <div class="actions">
              <button id="confirm-cancel" type="button" class="btn sm ghost">Cancel</button>
              <button id="confirm-go" type="button" class="btn sm" disabled>Link wallet permanently</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </main>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/config.js?v=12"></script>
  <script src="./assets/countries.js?v=11"></script>
  <script src="./assets/auth.js?v=13"></script>
  <script src="./assets/profile.js?v=18"></script>
  <script src="./assets/projection.js?v=5"></script>
  <script src="./assets/avatar.js?v=7"></script>
  <script src="./assets/nav.js?v=6"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

    function fmtPts(p){
      if (p == null) return '0';
      const n = Number(p);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0';
      return Number.isInteger(n) ? String(n) : (+n.toFixed(2)).toString();
    }

    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = $('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }
    function clearAlertSoon(){ setTimeout(()=>showAlert(), 2500); }

    function findCountry(code){
      return window.WC_COUNTRIES.find(c => c.code === code) || null;
    }

    let me = null;
    let pendingCountry = null;

    function renderProfile(){
      $('#me-name').textContent = me.username;
      $('#p-username').textContent = me.username;
      const c = findCountry(me.avatar_country);
      $('#ava-icon').innerHTML = window.WC_AVATAR.avatarHtml(me.avatar_country, 'lg');
      $('#p-country').textContent = c ? c.name : 'no country picked yet';
      $('#link-pub-profile').href = './user.html?u=' + encodeURIComponent(me.username);

      // Wallet badge (top-right of wallet card)
      const wb = $('#p-wallet-badge');
      if (me.wallet) {
        $('#wallet-empty').classList.add('hidden');
        $('#wallet-set').classList.remove('hidden');
        $('#w-addr').textContent = me.wallet;
        wb.innerHTML = '<span class="badge gold">⚽ linked</span>';
      } else {
        $('#wallet-empty').classList.remove('hidden');
        $('#wallet-set').classList.add('hidden');
        if ($('#w-addr-in')) $('#w-addr-in').value = '';
        if ($('#w-link')) $('#w-link').disabled = true;
        wb.innerHTML = '<span class="badge muted">not linked</span>';
      }

      // Stats card — wallet cell
      const sw = $('#s-wallet');
      const sws = $('#s-wallet-sub');
      if (me.wallet) {
        sw.textContent = '✓ linked';
        sw.style.color = 'var(--green)';
        sws.textContent = me.wallet.slice(0,6) + '…' + me.wallet.slice(-4);
      } else {
        sw.textContent = '— missing';
        sw.style.color = 'var(--muted)';
        sws.textContent = 'required for payouts';
      }
    }

    async function loadMyStats(){
      if (!me) return;
      const sb = window.WC_SB;
      try {
        // Leaderboard row + total players
        const [{ data: lb }, { count: totalUsers }, { count: tipCount }, { data: bonusRow }, { data: cryptoRow }] = await Promise.all([
          sb.from('wc_leaderboard').select('user_id,total_points').order('total_points', { ascending: false }).limit(500),
          sb.from('wc_users').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
          sb.from('wc_tips').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('user_id', me.id),
          sb.from('wc_bonus').select('champion,most_goals_team,fewest_goals_team,red_cards_bracket,topscorer_country').eq('user_id', me.id).maybeSingle(),
          sb.from('wc_crypto').select('btc_price,bnb_price,bobai_price').eq('user_id', me.id).maybeSingle(),
        ]);
        let myPoints = 0, myRank = '—';
        if (lb && lb.length) {
          const idx = lb.findIndex(r => r.user_id === me.id);
          if (idx >= 0) {
            myPoints = lb[idx].total_points || 0;
            myRank = idx + 1;
          }
        }
        $('#s-total').textContent = fmtPts(myPoints);
        $('#s-rank').textContent = myRank;
        $('#s-rank-sub').textContent = 'of ' + (totalUsers || 1) + ' player' + ((totalUsers || 1) === 1 ? '' : 's');

        const tipsPlaced = tipCount || 0;
        const tipsMissing = 104 - tipsPlaced;
        $('#s-tips').textContent = tipsPlaced;
        $('#s-tips-sub').innerHTML = tipsMissing > 0
          ? `${tipsMissing} missing — <a href="./tips.html" style="color:var(--gold);font-weight:600">place your tips →</a>`
          : 'All 104 placed ✓';

        const VALID_BRACKETS = new Set(['<5','5-9','10-14','15-19','>19']);
        const isValidBonus = (k, v) => v && (k !== 'red_cards_bracket' || VALID_BRACKETS.has(v));
        const bonusFilled = bonusRow
          ? ['champion','most_goals_team','fewest_goals_team','red_cards_bracket','topscorer_country']
              .filter(k => isValidBonus(k, bonusRow[k])).length
          : 0;
        const bonusMissing = 5 - bonusFilled;
        $('#s-bonus').textContent = bonusFilled;
        $('#s-bonus-sub').innerHTML = bonusMissing > 0
          ? `${bonusMissing} missing — <a href="./bonus.html" style="color:var(--gold);font-weight:600">answer them →</a>`
          : 'All 5 answered ✓';

        const cryptoFilled = cryptoRow ? ['btc_price','bnb_price','bobai_price'].filter(k => cryptoRow[k] != null).length : 0;
        const cryptoMissing = 3 - cryptoFilled;
        $('#s-crypto').textContent = cryptoFilled;
        $('#s-crypto-sub').innerHTML = cryptoMissing > 0
          ? `${cryptoMissing} missing — <a href="./crypto.html" style="color:var(--gold);font-weight:600">predict prices →</a>`
          : 'All 3 set ✓';
      } catch (e) {
        $('#stats-meta').textContent = 'stats unavailable';
      }
    }

    async function loadMyProjection(){
      if (!me || !window.WC_PROJECTION) return;
      window.WC_PROJECTION.injectStyles();
      const box = $('#proj-content');
      try {
        const proj = await window.WC_PROJECTION.loadAndCompute(me.id, me.wallet);
        box.innerHTML = window.WC_PROJECTION.renderHtml(proj);
      } catch (e) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="proj-empty">Projection unavailable.</div>';
      }
    }

    function renderGrid(){
      const grid = $('#country-grid');
      grid.innerHTML = '';
      window.WC_COUNTRIES.forEach(c => {
        const b = document.createElement('button');
        b.type = 'button';
        b.className = 'country-cell' + (me.avatar_country === c.code ? ' sel' : '');
        b.dataset.code = c.code;
        b.innerHTML = `<span class="fl">${c.flag}</span><span class="nm">${c.name}</span>`;
        b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
          $$('.country-cell').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));
          b.classList.add('sel');
          pendingCountry = c.code;
          $('#save-country').disabled = (pendingCountry === me.avatar_country);
        });
        grid.appendChild(b);
      });
    }

    /* Avatar-edit toggle */
    $('#btn-edit-avatar').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      const block = $('#avatar-edit');
      const opening = block.classList.contains('hidden');
      block.classList.toggle('hidden');
      if (opening) {
        pendingCountry = me.avatar_country;
        renderGrid();
        $('#save-country').disabled = true;
        block.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      }
    });
    $('#btn-cancel-avatar').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      $('#avatar-edit').classList.add('hidden');
      pendingCountry = me.avatar_country;
    });

    $('#save-country').addEventListener('click', async ()=>{
      if (pendingCountry === me.avatar_country) return;
      const btn = $('#save-country');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving…';
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.updateAvatar(pendingCountry);
      btn.textContent = 'Save avatar';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); btn.disabled = false; return; }
      me.avatar_country = pendingCountry;
      renderProfile();
      $('#avatar-edit').classList.add('hidden');
      showAlert('ok', 'Avatar updated.');
      clearAlertSoon();
    });

    /* Wallet link: enter address → confirm-modal with 3 acknowledgements → save */
    const ADDR_RE = /^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/;
    let pendingAddr = null;

    function validateAddrInput(){
      const raw = ($('#w-addr-in').value || '').trim();
      $('#w-link').disabled = !ADDR_RE.test(raw);
    }
    $('#w-addr-in').addEventListener('input', validateAddrInput);

    function openConfirm(addr){
      pendingAddr = addr.toLowerCase();
      $('#confirm-addr').textContent = pendingAddr;
      ['confirm-c1','confirm-c2','confirm-c3'].forEach(id => { $('#'+id).checked = false; });
      $('#confirm-go').disabled = true;
      $('#confirm-modal').classList.add('open');
    }
    function closeConfirm(){
      $('#confirm-modal').classList.remove('open');
      pendingAddr = null;
    }

    $('#w-link').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      const raw = ($('#w-addr-in').value || '').trim();
      if (!ADDR_RE.test(raw)) {
        showAlert('error', 'Enter a valid BEP-20 address (0x + 40 hex characters).');
        return;
      }
      showAlert();
      openConfirm(raw);
    });

    ['confirm-c1','confirm-c2','confirm-c3'].forEach(id => {
      $('#'+id).addEventListener('change', ()=>{
        $('#confirm-go').disabled = !($('#confirm-c1').checked && $('#confirm-c2').checked && $('#confirm-c3').checked);
      });
    });

    $('#confirm-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeConfirm);
    // Close on backdrop click (but not when clicking inside the modal)
    $('#confirm-modal').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ if (e.target === $('#confirm-modal')) closeConfirm(); });

    $('#confirm-go').addEventListener('click', async ()=>{
      if (!pendingAddr) return;
      const btn = $('#confirm-go');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Linking…';
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.saveWallet(pendingAddr);
      btn.textContent = 'Link wallet permanently';
      if (r.error) {
        showAlert('error', r.error);
        closeConfirm();
        return;
      }
      closeConfirm();
      me = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      renderProfile();
      loadMyProjection();  // refresh projection so wallet-warning disappears
      showAlert('ok', 'Wallet linked. This wallet is now permanently tied to your account.');
      clearAlertSoon();
    });

    /* logout handler is wired by nav.js */

    (async ()=>{
      me = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      if (!me) { window.location.href = '/worldcup/'; return; }
      renderProfile();
      loadMyStats();         // async, populates the stats card
      loadMyProjection();    // async, populates the projected-payout card
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

    // Designer mode toggle button
    $('#dm-state').textContent = window.WC_AVATAR.designOn ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
    function toggleDesign(){
      const nowOn = !window.WC_AVATAR.designOn;
      if (nowOn) localStorage.setItem('wc_design', '1');
      else       localStorage.removeItem('wc_design');
      window.location.href = window.location.pathname + (nowOn ? '?design=1' : '');
    }

    // Inventory all illustration slots, then HEAD-check each in parallel
    const inv = window.WC_AVATAR.inventory();
    const avatars = inv.filter(x => x.kind === 'avatar');
    const illus   = inv.filter(x => x.kind === 'illus');

    function escapeHtml(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({ '&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;' })[m]); }
    async function copyToClipboard(text, btn){
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
        const orig = btn.textContent;
        btn.textContent = '✓ Copied';
        setTimeout(()=>{ btn.textContent = orig; }, 1200);
      } catch (e) { /* noop */ }
    }

    function renderAvatars(filter){
      const list = $('#avatar-list');
      const rows = avatars
        .filter(a => filter === 'all' || a._state === filter)
        .map(a => {
          const stateCls = a._state || 'pending';
          const stateLabel = stateCls === 'done' ? `✓ delivered (.${a._ext})`
                          : stateCls === 'missing' ? 'pending'
                          : 'checking…';
          const preview = stateCls === 'done'
            ? `<img src="${a.basePath}.${a._ext}" alt="">`
            : `<div class="ph">${a.flag}</div>`;
          const justFile = a.filename.split('/').pop();
          const fileLabel = stateCls === 'done'
            ? `${justFile}.${a._ext}`
            : `${justFile}.{webp | png | jpg}`;
          return `<div class="item ${stateCls}">
            <button class="copy-btn" data-copy="${justFile}.webp">📋</button>
            <div class="preview">${preview}</div>
            <div class="info">
              <div class="name">${escapeHtml(a.name)}</div>
              <div class="file">${fileLabel}</div>
              <div class="status">${stateLabel}</div>
            </div>
          </div>`;
        })
        .join('');
      list.innerHTML = rows || '<p style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:30px">No avatars match this filter.</p>';
      $$('#avatar-list .copy-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.addEventListener('click', e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          copyToClipboard(b.dataset.copy, b);
        });
      });
    }

    function renderIllus(){
      const list = $('#illus-list');
      list.innerHTML = illus.map(i => {
        const cls = i._state === 'done' ? 'done' : '';
        const stateLabel = i._state === 'done' ? `✓ DELIVERED (.${i._ext})`
                        : i._state === 'missing' ? 'PENDING'
                        : 'checking…';
        const justFile = i.filename.split('/').pop();
        const fileLabel = i._state === 'done'
          ? `${i.filename}.${i._ext}`
          : `${i.filename}.{webp | png | jpg}`;
        return `<div class="illus-card ${cls}" data-file="${i.filename}">
          <div class="badge-row">
            <span class="lbl">${stateLabel}</span>
            <button class="copy-btn" data-copy="${justFile}.webp" style="position:static">📋 Copy filename</button>
          </div>
          <div class="ttl">${escapeHtml(i.name)}</div>
          <div class="file">${fileLabel}</div>
          <div class="specs">${escapeHtml(i.specs)}</div>
          <div class="used">Used on: ${escapeHtml(i.usedOn)}</div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('');
      $$('#illus-list .copy-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.addEventListener('click', e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          copyToClipboard(b.dataset.copy, b);
        });
      });
    }

    function updateStats(){
      const all = inv.filter(x => x._state); // only counted once HEAD-checked
      const done = all.filter(x => x._state === 'done').length;
      const miss = all.filter(x => x._state === 'missing').length;
      $('#st-done').textContent = done;
      $('#st-missing').textContent = miss;
      $('#st-total').textContent = inv.length;
      const pct = inv.length ? Math.round((done / inv.length) * 100) : 0;
      $('#st-pct').textContent = pct + '%';
    }

    // Run HEAD checks. findFormat() probes all formats in parallel per slot,
    // so each slot only takes one round-trip. We still limit concurrency so we
    // don't open hundreds of sockets at once.
    async function checkAll(){
      const queue = [...inv];
      const CONC = 16;
      const workers = Array.from({ length: CONC }, async () => {
        while (queue.length) {
          const it = queue.shift();
          const ext = await window.WC_AVATAR.findFormat(it.basePath);
          if (ext) { it._state = 'done'; it._ext = ext; }
          else     { it._state = 'missing'; it._ext = null; }
        }
      });
      const renderInterval = setInterval(()=>{
        renderAvatars(currentFilter);
        renderIllus();
        updateStats();
      }, 250);
      await Promise.all(workers);
      clearInterval(renderInterval);
      renderAvatars(currentFilter);
      renderIllus();
      updateStats();
    }

    let currentFilter = 'all';
    $$('.filter-bar button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
      $$('.filter-bar button').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('active', x === b));
      currentFilter = b.dataset.filter;
      renderAvatars(currentFilter);
    }));

    // Initial render (all pending) then start checking
    renderAvatars(currentFilter);
    renderIllus();
    updateStats();
    checkAll();
    // Click-to-zoom handled centrally by avatar.js (matches .item.done .preview img + .wc-ava-img)
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <style>
    .country-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(72px,1fr));gap:6px;max-height:240px;overflow-y:auto;padding:8px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.3);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px}
    .country-cell{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:2px;padding:8px 4px;border-radius:8px;background:transparent;border:1px solid transparent;cursor:pointer;transition:all .15s;color:var(--text)}

  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = $('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }

    // Render country grid
    const grid = $('#country-grid');
    const cInput = $('#country-input');
    window.WC_COUNTRIES.forEach(c => {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.type = 'button';
      b.className = 'country-cell';
      b.dataset.code = c.code;
      b.innerHTML = `<span class="fl">${c.flag}</span><span class="nm">${c.name}</span>`;
      b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
        $$('.country-cell').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));
        b.classList.add('sel');
        cInput.value = c.code;
      });
      grid.appendChild(b);
    });

    // Tab switching (login/register only; forgot is a separate pane)
    function showPane(which){
      $$('.tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active', x.dataset.tab===which));
      $('#form-login').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='login');
      $('#form-register').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='register');
      $('#form-forgot').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='forgot');
      showAlert();
    }
    $$('.tab').forEach(t=>t.addEventListener('click',()=>showPane(t.dataset.tab)));
    $('#link-forgot').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ e.preventDefault(); showPane('forgot'); });
    $('#link-back-login').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ e.preventDefault(); showPane('login'); });

    // Login
    $('#form-login').addEventListener('submit', async (e)=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Signing in…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.login({
        identifier: fd.get('identifier'),
        password: fd.get('password'),
      });
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Sign in';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); return; }
      window.location.href = './dashboard.html';
    });

    // Register
    $('#form-register').addEventListener('submit', async (e)=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      if (!fd.get('country')) {
        showAlert('error', 'Please pick your country flag below.');
        document.getElementById('country-grid').scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' });
        return;
      }
      const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Creating…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.register({
        username: fd.get('username'),
        email: fd.get('email'),
        password: fd.get('password'),
        passwordConfirm: fd.get('password_confirm'),
        country: fd.get('country'),
      });
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Create account';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); return; }
      if (r.needsConfirmation) {
        const email = fd.get('email') || '';
        const safeEmail = email.replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
        showAlert('ok', `📧 <b>Check your email</b> — we sent a confirmation link to <b style="word-break:break-all">${safeEmail}</b>. Click the link to activate your account, then sign in below.<br><br>⚠ <b>Don't see it?</b> Check your <b>spam / junk</b> folder — confirmation mails sometimes land there.`);
        // Scroll alert just under the top nav with minimal gap.
        // requestAnimationFrame waits for the alert HTML to render so we
        // compute its real position, not the empty-div position.
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          const alertEl = document.getElementById('alert');
          if (!alertEl) return;
          // Measure entire <nav> in case it has additional rows (.tabbar etc.)
          const navEl = document.querySelector('nav');
          const navH = navEl ? navEl.offsetHeight : 58;
          const top = alertEl.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - navH - 16;
          window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, top), behavior: 'smooth' });
        });
        return;
      }
      showAlert('ok', 'Account created — redirecting…');
      setTimeout(()=>{ window.location.href = './dashboard.html'; }, 700);
    });

    // Forgot password
    $('#form-forgot').addEventListener('submit', async (e)=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.resetPasswordRequest(fd.get('email'));
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Send reset link';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); return; }
      showAlert('ok', 'If an account exists for that email, a reset link has been sent. Check your inbox.');
    });

    // If already logged in, jump to dashboard
    (async ()=>{
      const p = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      if (p) window.location.href = './dashboard.html';
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
