    // BTC/BNB = Binance 1s-candle open @19:00:00.000Z, BOBAI = GeckoTerminal pool price
    // (last trade 10:33 UTC, unchanged through kickoff) — same sources as the live display.
    const FINAL_KICKOFF_PRICES = { btc: 64420.00, bnb: 567.47, bobai: 0.0000553231784899214 };
    // walletOnly: when true, non-linked players are hidden across every view (Overall, Groups,
    // Bracket projection, Crypto) and ranks re-number against the linked-only set — this is
    // the literal payout ranking the admin uses at settlement. Default OFF so the leaderboard
    // still shows bragging-rights positions for non-linked players. Persisted in localStorage.
    let walletOnly = false;
    try { walletOnly = localStorage.getItem('wc_wallet_filter') === '1'; } catch(e){}
    function isEligible(r){ return !!(r && r.has_wallet); }
    function applyWalletFilter(rows){ return walletOnly ? rows.filter(isEligible) : rows; }

    // ============== FORMATTERS ==============
    function countryFlag(code){ const c = window.WC_COUNTRIES.find(x => x.code === code); return c ? c.flag : '⚪'; }
    function countryName(code){ const c = window.WC_COUNTRIES.find(x => x.code === code); return c ? c.name : '—'; }
    function fmtPts(p){
      if (p == null) return '0';
      const n = Number(p);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0';
      return Number.isInteger(n) ? String(n) : (+n.toFixed(2)).toString();
    }
    function fmtBobai(n){
      if (n == null) return '0';
      const v = Number(n);
      if (v >= 1_000_000) return (v/1_000_000).toFixed(2) + 'M';
      if (v >= 1_000)     return (v/1_000).toFixed(1) + 'K';
      return v.toFixed(0);
    }
    function fmtUsd(n){
      if (n == null || !isFinite(n)) return '$0.00';
      if (n >= 1_000_000) return '$' + (n/1_000_000).toFixed(2) + 'M';
      if (n >= 1_000)     return '$' + (n/1_000).toFixed(1) + 'K';
      return '$' + n.toFixed(2);
    }
    function fmtCryptoPrice(coin, n){
      if (n == null) return '—';
      const v = Number(n);
      if (coin === 'bobai') return '$' + v.toFixed(10).replace(/0+$/,'').replace(/\.$/,'');
      if (coin === 'btc')   return '$' + v.toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
      return '$' + v.toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 4 });
    }
    function fmtPct(p){
      if (p == null || !isFinite(p)) return '—';
      if (p === 0) return '0%';
      if (p < 0.01) return '<0.01%';
      if (p < 0.1)  return p.toFixed(2) + '%';
      if (p < 10)   return p.toFixed(1)  + '%';
      return p.toFixed(0) + '%';
    }
    function fmtKickoffShort(iso){
      const d = new Date(iso);
      return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month:'short', day:'numeric' }) + ' · ' +
             d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' });
    }
    function escapeHtml(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({ '&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;' })[m]); }
    function coinDisplay(c){ return c === 'bobai' ? 'BOBAI' : c.toUpperCase(); }
    function coinIconPath(c){ const m = COINS.find(x => x.code === c); return m ? m.icon : ''; }

    // ============== PRIZE MATH ==============
    // End-pool: fixed tiered weights (50 / 25 / 15 / 10 %) for top 4 by total_points.
    // Skip+promote: non-linked players are skipped — the next wallet-linked player takes the
    // slot (e.g. if leaderboard #1 has no wallet, the wallet-linked #2 becomes payout #1).
    function computeEndPoolShares(rows){
      const shares = new Map();
      const eligible = rows.filter(isEligible);
      const top = eligible.slice(0, END_TOP_N);
      top.forEach((r, i) => {
        const tier = POOL_SPLIT.endTiers[i] || 0;
        shares.set(r.user_id, tier * pool.end);
      });
      return shares;
    }

    // Group pot: 55/30/15 across {top1, top2, best-3rd}. Per-player share = (fraction *
    // group_pot) / FIXED slots (12/12/8) — NOT divided by actually-filled slots. Empty
    // slots don't inflate the remaining players' projections; any unallocated share is
    // resolved at payout time within the SAME pot (cap-cascade first; if residual remains
    // after every winner is capped, leftover → permanent BOBAI/BNB liquidity + LP burn).
    // Keyed by `${user_id}|${letter}`.
    function computeGroupShares(allGroups){
      const shares = new Map();
      if (pool.group <= 0) return shares;
      const top1s = [], top2s = [], best3candidates = [];
      allGroups.forEach(g => {
        // Skip+promote within each group: drop non-linked players first, then pick top1/top2/best3.
        const r = g.rows.filter(isEligible);
        if (r[0]) top1s.push({ ...r[0], _letter: g.letter });
        if (r[1]) top2s.push({ ...r[1], _letter: g.letter });
        if (r[2]) best3candidates.push({ ...r[2], _letter: g.letter });
      });
      const best3 = best3candidates
        .sort((a,b) => (b.group_points||0) - (a.group_points||0))
        .slice(0, GROUP_SLOTS_BEST3);
      function distribute(bracket, fraction, slots){
        if (!bracket.length || slots <= 0) return;
        const each = (pool.group * fraction) / slots;
        bracket.forEach(r => shares.set(`${r.user_id}|${r._letter}`, each));
      }
      distribute(top1s, POOL_SPLIT.groupTop1,  GROUP_SLOTS_TOP1);
      distribute(top2s, POOL_SPLIT.groupTop2,  GROUP_SLOTS_TOP2);
      distribute(best3, POOL_SPLIT.groupBest3, GROUP_SLOTS_BEST3);
      return shares;
    }

    // Crypto: each coin pot ≈ 3.3% of total (= crypto_pot / 3); winner-take-all
    function computeCryptoWinnerShare(){ return pool.crypto / 3; }

    // ============== UI HELPERS ==============
    // contextPot: the pot this row is paid out of (end / group / crypto). The percentage
    // is the share of THAT pot — so Overall 1st reads 50% (of end pool), Crypto winner 33%
    // (of crypto pot), Group top1 ≈ 4.6% (of group pot, since 12 winners share the 55% bracket).
    function prizeCellHtml(bobai, contextPot){
      const pot = contextPot || 0;
      const pct = pot > 0 ? (bobai / pot * 100) : 0;
      const hasPrice = (pool.price || 0) > 0;
      const usdStr = !(bobai > 0) ? '$0' : (hasPrice ? fmtUsd(bobai * pool.price) : 'n/a');
      const isZero = !(bobai > 0);
      return `<div class="prize-cell ${isZero ? 'zero' : ''}">
        <div class="pz-pct">${isZero ? '—' : fmtPct(pct)}</div>
        <div class="pz-bobai">${isZero ? '0 BOBAI' : fmtBobai(bobai) + ' BOBAI'}</div>
        <div class="pz-usd">${usdStr}</div>
      </div>`;
    }

    function rowClass(rank, userId, extra){
      let c = 'row';
      if (rank === 1) c += ' top1';
      else if (rank === 2) c += ' top2';
      else if (rank === 3) c += ' top3';
      if (me && me.id === userId) c += ' me';
      if (extra) c += ' ' + extra;
      return c;
    }

    function rankLabel(rank){
      if (rank === 1) return '🥇';
      if (rank === 2) return '🥈';
      if (rank === 3) return '🥉';
      return '#' + rank;
    }

    function userRow(r, rank, opts){
      const country = countryName(r.avatar_country);
      const ball = r.has_wallet ? '<span class="ball">⚽</span>' : '';
      const href = './user.html?u=' + encodeURIComponent(r.username);
      const ava = window.WC_AVATAR
        ? window.WC_AVATAR.avatarHtml(r.avatar_country, 'sm')
        : `<span style="font-size:22px">${countryFlag(r.avatar_country)}</span>`;
      const prize = (opts.prizeBobai != null)
        ? prizeCellHtml(opts.prizeBobai, opts.contextPot)
        : `<div class="prize-cell zero"><div class="pz-pct">—</div><div class="pz-bobai">0 BOBAI</div><div class="pz-usd">$0</div></div>`;
      const ptsLabel = opts.ptsLabel ? `<span class="lbl">${opts.ptsLabel}</span>` : '';
      const pts = `<div class="pts ${opts.ptsClass || ''}">${opts.ptsValue}${ptsLabel}</div>`;
      // Tie-breaker badge — only when this row genuinely beat a tied-on-points
      // neighbor on BOBAI balance (server-side flag, no amounts exposed). Limited
      // to paid positions in this view: Groups top 3 (55/30/15), Overall/Bracket
      // top 4 (50/25/15/10). Non-payout ties stay quiet.
      const medalRank = opts.medalRank || 3;
      // Past tense ("held") for settled/frozen views, present ("holds") for live ones.
      const tieVerb = opts.tiePast ? 'held' : 'holds';
      const tiePill = (r.bobai_beats_below && rank <= medalRank)
        ? ` · <span class="tie-pill" title="Tied on points; ranks higher because this wallet ${tieVerb} more $BOBAI (rules §7).">${tieVerb} more $BOBAI</span>`
        : '';
      // Cap-not-reached badge — wallet linked but 26× holder cap = 0 (i.e. holds
      // too little $BOBAI), so the row's share cascades to the other group winners.
      // Shown only after the group pot is paid (opts.forfeit = wc_payouts amount === 0).
      const forfeitPill = opts.forfeit
        ? ` · <span class="forfeit-pill" title="Wallet didn't meet the 26× holder cap — share cascaded to the remaining winners / next eligible player (rules §5).">didn't reach 26× cap</span>`
        : '';
      const paidPill = opts.paidPill ? ` · <span class="paid-pill-inline">✓ paid</span>` : '';
      return `<a href="${href}" class="${rowClass(rank, r.user_id, opts.extraClass)}" style="text-decoration:none;color:inherit">
        <div class="rank">${rankLabel(rank)}</div>
        <div class="ava-sm">${ava}</div>
        <div class="uinfo">
          <div class="uname"><span class="uname-text">${escapeHtml(r.username)}</span>${ball}</div>
          <div class="umeta">${country}${tiePill}${forfeitPill}${paidPill}</div>
        </div>
        ${prize}
        ${pts}
      </a>`;
    }

    function meFooterHtml({ rank, totalRows, qualifierRank, qualifierPts, myPts, qualifiedCopy, needCopy }){
      if (!me) {
        return `<div class="me-footer"><div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-status">Sign in to see your own rank here.</span></div></div>`;
      }
      if (rank == null) {
        return `<div class="me-footer"><div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-status">You don't have entries in this view yet.</span></div></div>`;
      }
      const qualified = rank <= qualifierRank;
      const need = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((qualifierPts || 0) - (myPts || 0) + 0.01));
      const statusHtml = qualified
        ? `<span class="mf-status qualified">${qualifiedCopy}</span>`
        : `<span class="mf-status need">${needCopy.replace('{n}', need)}</span>`;
      return `<div class="me-footer">
        <div class="mf-left">
          <span class="mf-rank">#${rank}</span>
          <span class="mf-status">of ${totalRows} player${totalRows===1?'':'s'}</span>
        </div>
        <div>${statusHtml}</div>
      </div>`;
    }

    // ============== POOL ==============
    async function loadPool(){
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadPool();
      if (r.error) return;
      const p = r.pool || {};
      pool = {
        total:        parseFloat(p.total_bobai)     || 0,
        group:        parseFloat(p.group_pot)       || 0,
        end:          parseFloat(p.endpool)         || 0,
        crypto:       parseFloat(p.crypto_pot)      || 0,
        price:        parseFloat(p.bobai_price_usd) || 0,
        groupPaidAt:  p.group_paid_at || null,
      };
    }

    // ============== MATCHES + FIFA STANDINGS ==============
    async function loadAllMatches(){
      if (allMatches) return allMatches;
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadMatches();
      allMatches = r.matches || [];
      return allMatches;
    }

    // --- Knockout result helpers (extra time / penalties) ---
    // goals_home/away = 90-min result (what tips are scored on).
    // final_home/away (+ decided_by 'aet'|'pens') = full result that decides who
    // advances. Winner = direction of final when present, else of goals.
    function matchWinnerSide(m){
      const hasFinal = m.final_home != null && m.final_away != null;
      const h = hasFinal ? m.final_home : m.goals_home;
      const a = hasFinal ? m.final_away : m.goals_away;
      if (h == null || a == null || h === a) return null;
      return h > a ? 'home' : 'away';
    }
    // Goals counted toward scorer tallies: extra-time goals count, penalties never.
    function statGoals(m){
      if (m.decided_by === 'aet' && m.final_home != null)
        return { h: m.final_home, a: m.final_away };
      return { h: m.goals_home, a: m.goals_away };
    }
    // Scoreline suffix: "(3:4 pen)" or "(2:1 a.e.t.)". Empty for regular results.
    function resultSuffix(m){
      if (m.decided_by === 'pens' && m.final_home != null)
        return ` <span class="br-pens">(${m.final_home - m.goals_home}:${m.final_away - m.goals_away} pen)</span>`;
      if (m.decided_by === 'aet' && m.final_home != null)
        return ` <span class="br-pens">(${m.final_home}:${m.final_away} a.e.t.)</span>`;
      return '';
    }

    function computeStandings(matches, groupLetter){
      const teams = {};
      const gm = matches.filter(m => m.phase === 'group' && m.group_letter === groupLetter);
      gm.forEach(m => {
        [m.team_home, m.team_away].forEach(t => {
          if (t && t !== 'TBD' && !teams[t]) teams[t] = { code:t, P:0, W:0, D:0, L:0, GF:0, GA:0, GD:0, Pts:0 };
        });
        if (!m.played || m.goals_home == null || m.goals_away == null) return;
        const H = teams[m.team_home], A = teams[m.team_away];
        if (!H || !A) return;
        H.P++; A.P++;
        H.GF += m.goals_home; H.GA += m.goals_away;
        A.GF += m.goals_away; A.GA += m.goals_home;
        H.GD = H.GF - H.GA; A.GD = A.GF - A.GA;
        if (m.goals_home > m.goals_away)      { H.W++; H.Pts += 3; A.L++; }
        else if (m.goals_home < m.goals_away) { A.W++; A.Pts += 3; H.L++; }
        else                                  { H.D++; A.D++; H.Pts++; A.Pts++; }
      });
      return Object.values(teams).sort((a,b) =>
        b.Pts - a.Pts || b.GD - a.GD || b.GF - a.GF || a.code.localeCompare(b.code)
      );
    }

    function renderStandingsTable(rows, letter){
      if (!rows.length) return `<div class="empty">Group ${letter} teams not seeded yet.</div>`;
      const head = `<tr><th class="pos">#</th><th class="team-cell" style="text-align:left">Team</th><th>P</th><th>W</th><th>D</th><th>L</th><th>GF:GA</th><th>GD</th><th>Pts</th></tr>`;
      const body = rows.map((t, i) => {
        const rank = i + 1;
        const cls = rank <= 2 ? 'q'+rank : (rank === 3 ? 'q3' : '');
        const gdCls = t.GD > 0 ? 'gd-pos' : (t.GD < 0 ? 'gd-neg' : '');
        const gdTxt = t.GD > 0 ? '+'+t.GD : t.GD;
        return `<tr class="${cls}"><td class="pos">${rank}</td>
          <td><div class="team-cell"><span class="fl">${countryFlag(t.code)}</span><span class="nm">${countryName(t.code)}</span></div></td>
          <td>${t.P}</td><td>${t.W}</td><td>${t.D}</td><td>${t.L}</td><td>${t.GF}:${t.GA}</td>
          <td class="${gdCls}">${gdTxt}</td><td class="pts">${t.Pts}</td></tr>`;
      }).join('');
      return `<div class="standings"><table><thead>${head}</thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table>
        <div class="q-note">Top 2 advance to Round of 32. Best 3rd-placed teams may also qualify per FIFA seeding rules.</div></div>`;
    }

    // ============== OVERALL ==============
    async function renderOverall(){
      $('#sub-selector').classList.add('hidden');
      $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading…</div>';
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadOverallLeaderboard();
      if (r.error) { $('#list').innerHTML = `<div class="empty">${r.error}</div>`; return; }
      if (!r.rows.length) { $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No players yet.</div>'; return; }
      // Shares are computed from the unfiltered set (skip+promote handled inside computeEndPoolShares),
      // so a non-linked leader still doesn't get a share even with the toggle off. The toggle only
      // affects which rows are *shown* — and when shown, ranks re-number to match payout positions.
      const shares = computeEndPoolShares(r.rows);
      // Settled overlay (Final paid 2026-07-20): wc_payouts pot=end amounts are the
      // only truth — incl. Dgn's 26×-cap forfeit (0) and the proportional cascade
      // bonus on the remaining three. Same pattern as the settled groups.
      const endForfeits = new Set();
      let endPaid = false;
      {
        const pr = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadPayouts('end');
        if (!pr.error && (pr.rows || []).length) {
          endPaid = true;
          shares.clear();
          for (const row of pr.rows) {
            shares.set(row.user_id, Number(row.bobai_amount) || 0);
            if (Number(row.bobai_amount) === 0) endForfeits.add(row.user_id);
          }
        }
      }
      const rows = applyWalletFilter(r.rows);
      if (!rows.length) { $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No wallet-linked players yet — toggle "All players" to see the full leaderboard.</div>'; return; }
      const html = rows.map((row, i) => userRow(row, i+1, {
        ptsLabel: 'pts',
        ptsValue: fmtPts(row.total_points || 0),
        prizeBobai: shares.get(row.user_id) || 0,
        contextPot: pool.end,
        medalRank: END_TOP_N,
        extraClass: endPaid ? (endForfeits.has(row.user_id) ? 'forfeit' : ((shares.get(row.user_id) || 0) > 0 ? 'paid' : '')) : '',
        forfeit: endForfeits.has(row.user_id),
      })).join('');
      const scrollCls = rows.length > 10 ? 'list scroll-cap' : 'list';
      const myIdx = me ? rows.findIndex(r0 => r0.user_id === me.id) : -1;
      const cutoff = rows[END_TOP_N - 1];
      const footer = meFooterHtml({
        rank: myIdx >= 0 ? myIdx + 1 : null,
        totalRows: rows.length,
        qualifierRank: END_TOP_N,
        qualifierPts: cutoff ? (cutoff.total_points || 0) : 0,
        myPts: myIdx >= 0 ? (rows[myIdx].total_points || 0) : 0,
        qualifiedCopy: `✓ You're in the End-Pool top ${END_TOP_N}`,
        needCopy: `+{n} pts to enter the End-Pool top ${END_TOP_N}`,
      });
      $('#list').innerHTML = `<div class="${scrollCls}">${html}</div>${footer}`;
    }

    // ============== GROUPS ==============
    async function loadAllGroupData(){
      if (allGroupData) return allGroupData;
      const arr = await Promise.all(GROUP_LETTERS.map(async L => {
        const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadGroupLeaderboard(L);
        return { letter: L, rows: r.rows || [] };
      }));
      allGroupData = arr;
      return arr;
    }

    async function renderGroup(letter){
      $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading…</div>';
      const [allG, matches] = await Promise.all([ loadAllGroupData(), loadAllMatches() ]);
      const standings = computeStandings(matches, letter);
      const standingsHtml =
        `<div class="section-h"><span>Group ${letter} — FIFA standings</span><span class="right">${standings.reduce((a,t)=>a+t.P,0) ? 'live results' : 'matches not played yet'}</span></div>` +
        renderStandingsTable(standings, letter);

      const myGroup = allG.find(g => g.letter === letter);
      const rows = applyWalletFilter(myGroup ? myGroup.rows : []);

      // Build shares Map. Default = raw cascade-naive shares from leaderboard math.
      // Once the group pot is paid, overlay actual post-cascade amounts from
      // wc_payouts (which include 0-balance forfeits and redistributed bonuses).
      const shares = computeGroupShares(allG);
      const forfeits = new Set();
      let payoutRows = [];
      if (pool.groupPaidAt) {
        const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadPayouts('group');
        if (!r.error) {
          payoutRows = r.rows || [];
          for (const row of payoutRows) {
            shares.set(`${row.user_id}|${row.group_letter}`, Number(row.bobai_amount) || 0);
            if (Number(row.bobai_amount) === 0) forfeits.add(`${row.user_id}|${row.group_letter}`);
          }
        }
      }
      const best3InThisGroup = rows[2];
      const best3HasShare = best3InThisGroup
        && shares.has(`${best3InThisGroup.user_id}|${letter}`)
        && (shares.get(`${best3InThisGroup.user_id}|${letter}`) > 0);

      const paidDateStr = pool.groupPaidAt
        ? new Date(pool.groupPaidAt).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })
        : '';
      const playerSection = pool.groupPaidAt
        ? `<div class="section-h"><span>Group ${letter} — payout <span class="settled-pill">✓ settled</span></span><span class="right">frozen ${paidDateStr}</span></div>`
          + `<div class="settled-note">Paid out <b>${paidDateStr}</b> and <b>frozen</b>. The three winners below received the amounts shown on-chain.</div>`
        : `<div class="section-h"><span>Group ${letter} — players (top 5)</span><span class="right">55 / 30 / 15 % split of group pot</span></div>`;
      let playerListHtml;
      let footer = '';
      if (!rows.length) {
        playerListHtml = `<div class="empty">No tips in Group ${letter} yet.</div>`;
      } else if (pool.groupPaidAt) {
        // ===== SETTLED VIEW =====
        // The group pot is paid out and frozen. The ONLY source of truth is
        // wc_payouts — never the live re-projection. We render the actual
        // winners first (in their paid 1st/2nd/best-3rd order, with their
        // real on-chain amounts), then the remaining players by live points
        // at 0. This stops a wallet that drifted into a top-3 live slot after
        // the freeze (daily $BOBAI tie-breaker shuffle) from showing a phantom
        // share it never received.
        // The group stage is over → group_points are final. The only thing that
        // still moves is the live $BOBAI tie-breaker, which must NOT re-order a
        // settled group. So: (a) pin the actual winners in their paid order,
        // (b) order everyone else by frozen group_points. The "holds more $BOBAI"
        // pill stays on genuine point-ties (userRow only shows it when
        // bobai_beats_below && a tie exists) — it explains which wallet won the
        // tiebreak that set the frozen payout order.
        const frozen = r => r;
        const POS_RANK = { '1st': 1, '2nd': 2, 'best_3rd': 3 };
        const winRows = payoutRows
          .filter(p => p.group_letter === letter)
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => (POS_RANK[a.position] || 9) - (POS_RANK[b.position] || 9));
        const winnerIds = new Set(winRows.map(w => w.user_id));
        const byId = new Map(rows.map(r => [r.user_id, r]));
        const meIdx = me ? rows.findIndex(r0 => r0.user_id === me.id) : -1;

        // Everyone outside the paid top-3, ordered by frozen group_points.
        const others = rows
          .filter(r => !winnerIds.has(r.user_id))
          .sort((a, b) => (b.group_points || 0) - (a.group_points || 0));

        // "held more $BOBAI" pill for a SETTLED group must come from the FROZEN
        // payout order, NOT the live RPC flag (r.bobai_beats_below): the daily
        // $BOBAI snapshot drifts after the freeze (and wallet swaps), which would
        // otherwise move the badge onto the lower-paid tied player. A paid winner
        // held more iff it's tied on points with the row directly below it in the
        // frozen order (next paid winner, else the top non-winner) and that row
        // has a linked wallet. Forfeits (0 paid) never claim "held more".
        const winnerHtml = winRows.map((w, i) => {
          const base = byId.get(w.user_id) || {
            user_id: w.user_id, username: w.username,
            avatar_country: w.country_code, has_wallet: true, group_points: w.group_points,
          };
          const amt = Number(w.bobai_amount) || 0;
          const isForfeit = amt === 0;
          const below = (i + 1 < winRows.length) ? byId.get(winRows[i + 1].user_id) : others[0];
          const heldMore = !isForfeit && !!below && below.has_wallet !== false
            && (below.group_points || 0) === (base.group_points || 0);
          return userRow({ ...base, bobai_beats_below: heldMore }, i + 1, {
            ptsLabel: 'pts',
            ptsValue: fmtPts(base.group_points || 0),
            prizeBobai: amt,
            contextPot: pool.group,
            extraClass: isForfeit ? 'forfeit' : (amt > 0 ? 'paid' : ''),
            forfeit: isForfeit,
            tiePast: true,
          });
        }).join('');

        const othersShown = others.slice(0, Math.max(0, 5 - winRows.length));
        // Unpaid "live standings" rows never claim the frozen held-more pill.
        const othersHtml = othersShown.map((r0, j) => userRow({ ...frozen(r0), bobai_beats_below: false }, winRows.length + j + 1, {
          ptsLabel: 'pts',
          ptsValue: fmtPts(r0.group_points || 0),
          prizeBobai: 0,
          contextPot: pool.group,
          tiePast: true,
        })).join('');

        const shownIds = new Set([...winnerIds, ...othersShown.map(r => r.user_id)]);
        let meHtml = '';
        if (me && meIdx >= 0 && !shownIds.has(me.id)) {
          const meRow = rows[meIdx];
          const meFrozenRank = others.findIndex(r => r.user_id === me.id);
          meHtml = `<div class="rank-gap">···</div>` + userRow({ ...frozen(meRow), bobai_beats_below: false }, winRows.length + (meFrozenRank >= 0 ? meFrozenRank + 1 : meIdx + 1), {
            ptsLabel: 'pts',
            ptsValue: fmtPts(meRow.group_points || 0),
            prizeBobai: 0,
            contextPot: pool.group,
            tiePast: true,
          });
        }
        const divider = othersHtml
          ? `<div class="settled-divider">— paid winners above · live standings below (no further payout) —</div>`
          : '';
        playerListHtml = `<div class="list">${winnerHtml}${divider}${othersHtml}${meHtml}</div>`;

        // Settled footer: tell me exactly what I won here (if anything).
        if (me) {
          const myWin = winRows.find(w => w.user_id === me.id);
          let statusHtml;
          if (myWin && Number(myWin.bobai_amount) > 0) {
            const pos = myWin.position === '1st' ? '1st' : myWin.position === '2nd' ? '2nd' : 'best 3rd';
            statusHtml = `<span class="mf-status qualified">✓ You won Group ${letter} ${pos} — ${fmtBobai(Number(myWin.bobai_amount))} BOBAI paid on-chain</span>`;
          } else if (myWin) {
            statusHtml = `<span class="mf-status need">Group ${letter} ${myWin.position === 'best_3rd' ? 'best 3rd' : myWin.position} — share cascaded (26× cap not reached)</span>`;
          } else {
            const myRank = meIdx >= 0 ? meIdx + 1 : null;
            statusHtml = `<span class="mf-status">Group ${letter} settled — you finished ${myRank ? '#' + myRank : 'unranked'}, outside the paid positions</span>`;
          }
          footer = `<div class="me-footer"><div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-status">Group ${letter} payout ✓ frozen</span></div><div>${statusHtml}</div></div>`;
        }
      } else {
        const top5 = rows.slice(0, 5);
        const meIdx = me ? rows.findIndex(r0 => r0.user_id === me.id) : -1;
        const meRowData = (meIdx >= 5) ? rows[meIdx] : null;
        const listHtml = top5.map((r0, i) => userRow(r0, i+1, {
          ptsLabel: 'pts',
          ptsValue: fmtPts(r0.group_points || 0),
          prizeBobai: shares.get(`${r0.user_id}|${letter}`) || 0,
          contextPot: pool.group,
        })).join('');
        const meHtml = meRowData
          ? `<div class="rank-gap">···</div>` + userRow(meRowData, meIdx + 1, {
              ptsLabel: 'pts',
              ptsValue: fmtPts(meRowData.group_points || 0),
              prizeBobai: shares.get(`${meRowData.user_id}|${letter}`) || 0,
              contextPot: pool.group,
            })
          : '';
        playerListHtml = `<div class="list">${listHtml}${meHtml}</div>`;

        const cutoffPlayer = rows[1] || rows[0];
        const cutoffPts = cutoffPlayer ? (cutoffPlayer.group_points || 0) : 0;
        const myPts = (meIdx >= 0) ? (rows[meIdx].group_points || 0) : 0;
        const myRank = (meIdx >= 0) ? meIdx + 1 : null;
        const qualifiedCopy = myRank === 1
          ? `✓ You're 1st in Group ${letter} — top-1 share`
          : `✓ You're 2nd in Group ${letter} — top-2 share`;
        const needCopy = (myRank === 3 && best3HasShare)
          ? `Currently qualifies as best-3rd · +{n} pts to lock top 2`
          : `+{n} pts to enter Group ${letter} top 2`;
        footer = meFooterHtml({
          rank: myRank, totalRows: rows.length,
          qualifierRank: 2, qualifierPts: cutoffPts, myPts,
          qualifiedCopy, needCopy,
        });
      }

      $('#list').innerHTML = standingsHtml + playerSection + playerListHtml + footer;
      if (window.twemoji) try { window.twemoji.parse($('#list'), { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    // ============== BRACKET ==============
    function brTeam(code, align){
      const tbd = !code || code === 'TBD';
      const cls = ['br-team']; if (tbd) cls.push('tbd'); if (align === 'right') cls.push('away');
      const flag = tbd ? '⚪' : countryFlag(code);
      const name = tbd ? 'TBD' : countryName(code);
      if (align === 'right') return `<div class="${cls.join(' ')}"><span class="nm">${name}</span><span class="fl">${flag}</span></div>`;
      return `<div class="${cls.join(' ')}"><span class="fl">${flag}</span><span class="nm">${name}</span></div>`;
    }
    function brTeamWithWin(code, align, isWin){
      const html = brTeam(code, align);
      if (!isWin) return html;
      return html.replace('br-team', 'br-team win');
    }
    function renderBracketMatchRow(m){
      const played = m.played && m.goals_home != null && m.goals_away != null;
      let hWin = false, aWin = false;
      if (played) { const s = matchWinnerSide(m); hWin = s === 'home'; aWin = s === 'away'; }
      const isFinal = m.phase === 'final';
      const time = m.kickoff_utc ? fmtKickoffShort(m.kickoff_utc) : '';
      const center = played
        ? `<div class="br-score played">${m.goals_home} : ${m.goals_away}${resultSuffix(m)}</div><div class="br-time">${time}</div>`
        : `<div class="br-vs">VS</div><div class="br-time">${time}</div>`;
      return `<div class="br-match ${played?'played':''} ${isFinal?'final':''}">
        ${brTeamWithWin(m.team_home, 'left',  hWin)}
        <div class="br-center">${center}</div>
        ${brTeamWithWin(m.team_away, 'right', aWin)}
      </div>`;
    }

    async function renderBracket(){
      showPhaseSelector();
      $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading bracket…</div>';
      const matches = await loadAllMatches();
      const phase = KO_PHASES.find(p => p.key === subView) || KO_PHASES[0];
      const phaseMatches = matches.filter(m => m.phase === phase.key)
        .sort((a,b) => new Date(a.kickoff_utc) - new Date(b.kickoff_utc));
      const matchListHtml = phaseMatches.length
        ? `<div class="br-list">${phaseMatches.map(renderBracketMatchRow).join('')}</div>`
        : `<div class="empty">No ${phase.label} matches scheduled yet.</div>`;

      // Overall standings below — from KO onwards only End Pool matters.
      // Same skip+promote logic + toggle filter as the Overall view.
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadOverallLeaderboard();
      const allRows = r.rows || [];
      const shares = computeEndPoolShares(allRows);
      const overallRows = applyWalletFilter(allRows);
      const overallHtml = overallRows.length
        ? overallRows.map((row, i) => userRow(row, i+1, {
            ptsLabel: 'pts',
            ptsValue: fmtPts(row.total_points || 0),
            prizeBobai: shares.get(row.user_id) || 0,
            contextPot: pool.end,
            medalRank: END_TOP_N,
          })).join('')
        : '<div class="empty">No players yet.</div>';
      const scrollCls = overallRows.length > 10 ? 'list scroll-cap' : 'list';
      const myIdx = me ? overallRows.findIndex(r0 => r0.user_id === me.id) : -1;
      const cutoff = overallRows[END_TOP_N - 1];
      const footer = meFooterHtml({
        rank: myIdx >= 0 ? myIdx + 1 : null,
        totalRows: overallRows.length,
        qualifierRank: END_TOP_N,
        qualifierPts: cutoff ? (cutoff.total_points || 0) : 0,
        myPts: myIdx >= 0 ? (overallRows[myIdx].total_points || 0) : 0,
        qualifiedCopy: `✓ You're in the End-Pool top ${END_TOP_N}`,
        needCopy: `+{n} pts to enter the End-Pool top ${END_TOP_N}`,
      });

      $('#list').innerHTML = `
        <div class="section-h"><span>${phase.label}</span><span class="right">${phaseMatches.length} match${phaseMatches.length===1?'':'es'}</span></div>
        ${matchListHtml}
        <div class="section-h" style="margin-top:26px"><span>End-Pool projection — overall standings</span><span class="right">share of end pool</span></div>
        <div class="${scrollCls}">${overallHtml}</div>
        ${footer}`;
      if (window.twemoji) try { window.twemoji.parse($('#list'), { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    // ============== BONUS ==============
    // State cache so switching between bonus sub-tabs doesn't re-fetch every time.
    let bonusData = null;

    async function loadBonusData(){
      if (bonusData) return bonusData;
      const [ba, sc, st, mt] = await Promise.all([
        window.WC_PROFILE.loadAllBonusAnswers(),
        window.WC_PROFILE.loadScorers(),
        window.WC_PROFILE.loadTournamentStats(),
        loadAllMatches(),
      ]);
      bonusData = {
        answers: ba.rows || [],
        scorers: sc.scorers || [],
        stats:   st.stats || { red_cards_total: 0 },
        matches: mt || [],
      };
      return bonusData;
    }

    // Compute goals-scored-per-team from played group matches AND played KO matches.
    // Returns { code: { gf, played, gamesLeft, eliminated } }. `gamesLeft` is
    // unplayed matches the team is still scheduled to play, which we need to
    // know whether a "currently leading" team is mathematically uncatchable.
    function computeTeamGoalStats(matches){
      const byTeam = {};
      const ensure = code => {
        if (!code || code === 'TBD') return null;
        if (!byTeam[code]) byTeam[code] = { code, gf: 0, played: 0, gamesLeft: 0, eliminated: false };
        return byTeam[code];
      };
      matches.forEach(m => {
        const H = ensure(m.team_home), A = ensure(m.team_away);
        if (m.played && m.goals_home != null && m.goals_away != null) {
          const sg = statGoals(m);  // ET goals count, penalties don't
          if (H) { H.gf += sg.h; H.played++; }
          if (A) { A.gf += sg.a; A.played++; }
        } else {
          if (H) H.gamesLeft++;
          if (A) A.gamesLeft++;
        }
      });
      // Eliminated detection — two paths, neither speculative:
      //   1) Team lost a KO match (definitive exit).
      //   2) Group stage fully complete AND team has no future scheduled match.
      //      During the group phase we can't tell yet — the team's R32 slot may
      //      still be TBD pending the cross-group 3rd-place comparison, and the
      //      football-data.org sync only fills KO assignments after group-end.
      //      Without this guard, advancing teams like Netherlands (10 group goals)
      //      get flagged eliminated just because their TBD home slot hasn't
      //      received a team code yet.
      const groupTotal = matches.filter(m => m.phase === 'group').length;
      const groupPlayed = matches.filter(m => m.phase === 'group' && m.played).length;
      const groupDone = groupTotal > 0 && groupPlayed === groupTotal;
      // KO losers
      matches.forEach(m => {
        if (m.phase === 'group') return;
        if (!m.played || m.goals_home == null || m.goals_away == null) return;
        const side = matchWinnerSide(m);  // resolves ET/penalty winner too
        if (!side) return;
        const loserCode = side === 'home' ? m.team_away : m.team_home;
        if (loserCode && byTeam[loserCode]) byTeam[loserCode].eliminated = true;
      });
      // Group-phase exits — only safe to call after all 72 group matches played.
      if (groupDone) {
        Object.values(byTeam).forEach(t => {
          if (t.played > 0 && t.gamesLeft === 0) t.eliminated = true;
        });
      }
      return byTeam;
    }

    // Render one card per "winning entry" (a team / player / bracket) followed
    // by the chips of the users who tipped it. The award badge on each chip is
    // GREY when the question is open and GREEN once the answer is locked in.
    function bonusCardHtml({ avaHtml, primary, secondaryHtml, eliminated, tipperRows, locked, isWinner }){
      const chips = tipperRows.length
        ? tipperRows.map(r => {
            const href = './user.html?u=' + encodeURIComponent(r.username);
            const isMe = !!(me && r.user_id === me.id);
            const award = locked
              ? `<span class="pts-tag awarded">+20</span>`
              : `<span class="pts-tag pending">20</span>`;
            const ball = r.has_wallet ? '<span class="ball">⚽</span>' : '';
            return `<a class="tip-chip${isMe?' me':''}" href="${href}">${escapeHtml(r.username)}${ball}${award}</a>`;
          }).join('')
        : `<div class="empty">No one tipped this one.</div>`;
      const cls = ['bonus-card'];
      if (eliminated) cls.push('eliminated');
      if (isWinner)   cls.push('winner');
      return `<div class="${cls.join(' ')}">
        <div class="bc-head">
          <div class="bc-ava">${avaHtml}</div>
          <div class="bc-info">
            <div class="bc-name">${primary}</div>
            ${secondaryHtml ? `<div class="bc-meta">${secondaryHtml}</div>` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="bc-tippers">${chips}</div>
      </div>`;
    }

    function bonusStatusBar(intro, statusLabel, statusClass, rightLabel){
      return `<div class="bonus-intro">${intro}</div>
        <div class="bonus-status">
          <span class="left">Current status</span>
          <span class="right">${rightLabel || ''}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="margin:-4px 4px 14px"><span class="bonus-pill ${statusClass}">${statusLabel}</span></div>`;
    }

    function tippersForField(data, field, value){
      return data.answers.filter(a => a[field] === value);
    }

    // ----- Most / Fewest goals: show actual leader(s) across ALL teams.
    // Tippers shown below each card — "No one tipped this one." if empty. -----
    function renderGoalsBonus(data, mode){
      const tab    = BONUS_TABS.find(t => t.key === mode);
      const field  = tab.field;
      const teams  = computeTeamGoalStats(data.matches);
      // ALL teams that appear in fixtures (skip TBD KO placeholders that haven't
      // had a real opponent assigned yet).
      const allTeams = Object.values(teams).filter(t => t.code !== 'TBD' && (t.played > 0 || t.gamesLeft > 0));
      if (!allTeams.length) return `<div class="bonus-empty">No team data yet.</div>`;

      let leaders = [];
      let lockedRule = false;
      let statusLabel, statusClass, rightLabel;
      if (mode === 'most_goals') {
        const top = Math.max(...allTeams.map(t => t.gf));
        leaders = allTeams.filter(t => t.gf === top);
        // Most goals locks only when the Final is finished — any team still
        // playing could put up a blowout score (e.g. 12:0) and overtake.
        const finalMatch = data.matches.find(m => m.phase === 'final');
        lockedRule = !!(finalMatch && finalMatch.played && finalMatch.goals_home != null);
        statusLabel = lockedRule ? `Resolved · ${leaders.map(t=>countryFlag(t.code)).join(' ')} ${top} goals`
                                 : `Live · ${top} goals lead`;
        statusClass = lockedRule ? 'locked' : 'live';
        rightLabel  = lockedRule ? 'final whistle blown' : `${leaders.length} team${leaders.length===1?'':'s'} tied for top`;
      } else {
        // fewest_goals — actual lowest scorer(s) tournament-wide.
        const low = Math.min(...allTeams.map(t => t.gf));
        leaders = allTeams.filter(t => t.gf === low);
        // Locked when every leader is eliminated AND no remaining team can
        // possibly drop to or below `low`. Since teams can only score MORE,
        // a still-active team with current gf <= low could stay ≤ low ⇒ unlocked.
        const anyContenderCanReach = allTeams.some(t => !leaders.includes(t) && t.gf <= low);
        lockedRule = leaders.length > 0 && leaders.every(t => t.eliminated) && !anyContenderCanReach;
        const groupTotal = data.matches.filter(m => m.phase === 'group').length;
        const groupPlayed = data.matches.filter(m => m.phase === 'group' && m.played).length;
        statusLabel = lockedRule ? `Resolved · ${leaders.map(t=>countryFlag(t.code)).join(' ')} ${low} goal${low===1?'':'s'}`
                                 : `Live · ${groupPlayed}/${groupTotal} group matches played`;
        statusClass = lockedRule ? 'locked' : 'live';
        rightLabel  = lockedRule ? 'mathematically locked' : 'updates after each match';
      }

      const ordered = leaders.slice().sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code));
      const head = bonusStatusBar(tab.intro, statusLabel, statusClass, rightLabel);
      const cards = ordered.map(t => {
        const tippers = tippersForField(data, field, t.code);
        const ava = window.WC_AVATAR
          ? window.WC_AVATAR.avatarHtml(t.code, 'sm')
          : `<span style="font-size:30px">${countryFlag(t.code)}</span>`;
        const elim = t.eliminated ? `<span class="elim">eliminated</span>` : '';
        const meta = `<b>${t.gf}</b> goal${t.gf===1?'':'s'} · ${t.played} played${t.gamesLeft?` · ${t.gamesLeft} left`:''}${elim?` · ${elim}`:''}`;
        return bonusCardHtml({
          avaHtml: ava,
          primary: `${countryName(t.code)}`,
          secondaryHtml: meta,
          eliminated: t.eliminated && !lockedRule,
          tipperRows: tippers,
          locked: lockedRule,
          isWinner: true,
        });
      }).join('');
      return head + (cards || `<div class="bonus-empty">No leader determined yet.</div>`);
    }

    // ----- Champion: tipped teams that are still in the tournament. Eliminated
    // teams disappear from the board. Once final is played, only the actual
    // winner stays. -----
    function renderChampion(data){
      const tab = BONUS_TABS.find(t => t.key === 'champion');
      const teams = computeTeamGoalStats(data.matches);
      const tippedCodes = Array.from(new Set(data.answers.map(a => a.champion).filter(Boolean)));
      if (!tippedCodes.length) {
        return bonusStatusBar(tab.intro, 'No picks yet', 'pending', '') +
          `<div class="bonus-empty">No one has tipped a champion yet.</div>`;
      }
      // Resolve only when the final is played.
      const finalMatch = data.matches.find(m => m.phase === 'final');
      const finalPlayed = !!(finalMatch && finalMatch.played && finalMatch.goals_home != null);
      let winnerCode = null;
      if (finalPlayed) {
        const s = matchWinnerSide(finalMatch);  // ET/penalty winner too
        if (s === 'home') winnerCode = finalMatch.team_home;
        else if (s === 'away') winnerCode = finalMatch.team_away;
      }
      // Only show tipped teams still in the tournament (or the winner once the
      // final has been played). The rule as stated: a team stays listed for as
      // long as it is still in the tournament.
      const candidates = tippedCodes
        .map(code => {
          const t = teams[code] || { code, gf: 0, played: 0, gamesLeft: 0, eliminated: false };
          return { code, ...t };
        })
        .filter(t => winnerCode ? t.code === winnerCode : !t.eliminated)
        .sort((a, b) => countryName(a.code).localeCompare(countryName(b.code)));
      const head = bonusStatusBar(
        tab.intro,
        winnerCode ? `Resolved · 🏆 ${countryName(winnerCode)} won the final`
                   : `Live · ${candidates.length} tipped team${candidates.length===1?'':'s'} still in`,
        winnerCode ? 'locked' : 'live',
        finalPlayed ? 'final played' : 'updates after each elimination'
      );
      if (!candidates.length) return head + `<div class="bonus-empty">All tipped champions are eliminated.</div>`;
      const cards = candidates.map(t => {
        const tippers = tippersForField(data, 'champion', t.code);
        const ava = window.WC_AVATAR
          ? window.WC_AVATAR.avatarHtml(t.code, 'sm')
          : `<span style="font-size:30px">${countryFlag(t.code)}</span>`;
        const isWinner = (t.code === winnerCode);
        const trophy = isWinner ? ' · <b>🏆 Champion</b>' : '';
        const meta = `${t.played} played${t.gamesLeft ? ` · ${t.gamesLeft} left` : ''}${trophy}`;
        return bonusCardHtml({
          avaHtml: ava,
          primary: countryName(t.code),
          secondaryHtml: meta,
          eliminated: false,
          tipperRows: tippers,
          locked: isWinner,
          isWinner: true,
        });
      }).join('');
      return head + cards;
    }

    // ----- Golden Boot: show actual top scorers from football-data.org,
    // regardless of who was tipped. Tippers shown below each country card. -----
    function renderGoldenBoot(data){
      const tab = BONUS_TABS.find(t => t.key === 'golden_boot');
      const scorers = data.scorers || [];
      if (!scorers.length) {
        return bonusStatusBar(tab.intro, 'Awaiting first goals', 'pending', 'no scorers yet') +
          `<div class="bonus-empty">Top-scorers feed will populate after the first goal of the tournament.</div>`;
      }
      const topGoals = scorers[0].goals;
      const leaders = scorers.filter(s => s.goals === topGoals && s.country_code);
      const useLeaders = leaders;
      const teamsByCountry = {};
      useLeaders.forEach(s => {
        if (!teamsByCountry[s.country_code]) teamsByCountry[s.country_code] = [];
        teamsByCountry[s.country_code].push(s);
      });
      const cardCountries = Object.keys(teamsByCountry);
      // Golden Boot locks when the Final is finished — no more goals can be scored.
      const finalMatch = data.matches.find(m => m.phase === 'final');
      const bootLocked = !!(finalMatch && finalMatch.played && finalMatch.goals_home != null);
      const head = bonusStatusBar(
        tab.intro,
        bootLocked
          ? `Resolved · ${cardCountries.map(countryFlag).join(' ')} ${topGoals} goal${topGoals===1?'':'s'}`
          : `Live · ${topGoals} goal${topGoals===1?'':'s'} lead${useLeaders.length>1?` (${useLeaders.length} tied)`:''}`,
        bootLocked ? 'locked' : 'live',
        bootLocked
          ? 'final whistle blown'
          : `updated ${scorers[0].updated_at ? new Date(scorers[0].updated_at).toLocaleString(undefined,{month:'short',day:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}) : 'recently'}`
      );
      const teams = computeTeamGoalStats(data.matches);
      const cards = cardCountries.map(code => {
        const players = teamsByCountry[code];
        const elimTeam = (teams[code] && teams[code].eliminated);
        const playerLines = players.map(p => `<b>${escapeHtml(p.player_name)}</b> · ${p.goals}g`).join(' · ');
        const ava = window.WC_AVATAR
          ? window.WC_AVATAR.avatarHtml(code, 'sm')
          : `<span style="font-size:30px">${countryFlag(code)}</span>`;
        return bonusCardHtml({
          avaHtml: ava,
          primary: countryName(code),
          secondaryHtml: playerLines + (elimTeam && !bootLocked ? ` · <span class="elim">team eliminated</span>` : ''),
          eliminated: elimTeam && !bootLocked,
          tipperRows: tippersForField(data, 'topscorer_country', code),
          locked: bootLocked,
          isWinner: true,
        });
      }).join('');
      return head + cards;
    }

    // ----- Red cards: show ONLY the bracket the live count falls in, plus
    // the tippers of that bracket. Per user spec: only the team/bucket
    // currently holding the 20 pts. -----
    function renderRedCards(data){
      const tab   = BONUS_TABS.find(t => t.key === 'red_cards');
      const total = Number(data.stats?.red_cards_total || 0);
      const current = RED_CARD_BRACKETS.find(b => total >= b.min && total <= b.max);
      // Locks when the Final is finished — the tournament total is final.
      const finalMatch = data.matches.find(m => m.phase === 'final');
      const rcLocked = !!(finalMatch && finalMatch.played && finalMatch.goals_home != null);
      const head = bonusStatusBar(
        tab.intro,
        rcLocked
          ? `Resolved · 🟥 ${total} red cards total`
          : `Live · ${total} red card${total===1?'':'s'} so far`,
        rcLocked ? 'locked' : 'live',
        rcLocked ? 'final whistle blown' : 'final resolution at end of Final'
      );
      if (!current) return head + `<div class="bonus-empty">No bracket matches the current red-card total.</div>`;
      const tippers = tippersForField(data, 'red_cards_bracket', current.key);
      return head + bonusCardHtml({
        avaHtml: `<span style="font-size:30px">🟥</span>`,
        primary: current.label,
        secondaryHtml: rcLocked
          ? `<b>Final bucket</b> · ${total} red cards in the tournament`
          : `<b>Current bucket</b> · ${total} red card${total===1?'':'s'} so far`,
        eliminated: false,
        tipperRows: tippers,
        locked: rcLocked,
        isWinner: true,
      });
    }

    async function renderBonus(){
      showBonusSelector();
      $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading bonus standings…</div>';
      const data = await loadBonusData();
      const mode = subView || 'champion';
      let html;
      if      (mode === 'champion')     html = renderChampion(data);
      else if (mode === 'most_goals')   html = renderGoalsBonus(data, 'most_goals');
      else if (mode === 'fewest_goals') html = renderGoalsBonus(data, 'fewest_goals');
      else if (mode === 'golden_boot')  html = renderGoldenBoot(data);
      else if (mode === 'red_cards')    html = renderRedCards(data);
      else html = '<div class="bonus-empty">Pick a bonus question above.</div>';
      $('#list').innerHTML = html;
      if (window.twemoji) try { window.twemoji.parse($('#list'), { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    // ============== CRYPTO ==============
    async function renderCrypto(coin){
      $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading…</div>';
      const [r, matches] = await Promise.all([ window.WC_PROFILE.loadCryptoLeaderboard(coin), loadAllMatches() ]);
      if (r.error) { $('#list').innerHTML = `<div class="empty">${r.error}</div>`; return; }
      if (!r.rows.length) { $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No predictions yet.</div>'; return; }

      const priceKey = coin + '_price';
      const livePx = FINAL_KICKOFF_PRICES[coin];
      const display = coinDisplay(coin);

      // Reveal crypto tips publicly at the final edit lock (BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC), same gate as profile page.
      const cryptoLockMs = new Date(window.WC_CONFIG.BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC).getTime();
      const cryptoRevealed = Date.now() >= cryptoLockMs;

      // Sort all by tip price ASC (apply wallet-only filter first if toggle is on — ranks
      // and the "closest" highlight then re-number against the linked-only set).
      const all = applyWalletFilter(r.rows
        .filter(row => row[priceKey] != null)
        .map(row => ({ ...row, _tip: parseFloat(row[priceKey]) }))
        .filter(row => isFinite(row._tip)))
        .sort((a,b) => a._tip - b._tip);

      if (!all.length) { $('#list').innerHTML = `<div class="empty">${walletOnly ? 'No wallet-linked ' + display + ' predictions yet — toggle "All players" to see all.' : 'No valid ' + display + ' predictions yet.'}</div>`; return; }

      // Skip+promote: the actual payout winner is the closest player WITH A LINKED WALLET.
      // When the toggle is off we still highlight the absolute closest visually (because the
      // leaderboard rank reflects the real game state), but the prize cell is attributed to
      // the eligible winner. When the toggle is on, `all` is already wallet-only so both
      // collapse to the same row.
      let closestIdx = 0;
      if (livePx != null && isFinite(livePx)) {
        let bestDiff = Infinity;
        all.forEach((row, i) => {
          const d = Math.abs(row._tip - livePx);
          if (d < bestDiff) { bestDiff = d; closestIdx = i; }
        });
      }
      // Payout winner — FINAL (2026-07-20): closest wallet-linked guess WITH BOBAI
      // holdings (26x cap: zero balance → cap 0 → next closest). BTC _PW · BNB prince27 ·
      // BOBAI Beso2025 (Havy was closest but holds 0 BOBAI → forfeits per cap rule).
      const PAYOUT_WINNERS = { btc: 12, bnb: 59, bobai: 40 };
      const CRYPTO_FORFEITS = { bobai: 46 };  // Havy — closest guess, 0 BOBAI → cap 0 → forfeited
      const payoutWinnerId = PAYOUT_WINNERS[coin] ?? null;
      // Rank by absolute distance to live (closest = #1)
      const rankByUserId = new Map();
      if (livePx != null && isFinite(livePx)) {
        all.map((row, i) => ({ row, dist: Math.abs(row._tip - livePx) }))
           .sort((a,b) => a.dist - b.dist)
           .forEach((x, rank) => rankByUserId.set(x.row.user_id, rank + 1));
      } else {
        all.forEach((row, i) => rankByUserId.set(row.user_id, i + 1));
      }

      const above = all.slice(closestIdx + 1, closestIdx + 4); // higher tips
      const below = all.slice(Math.max(0, closestIdx - 3), closestIdx); // lower tips
      const aboveDisplay = above.slice().reverse(); // highest tip first (top of display)
      const belowDisplay = below.slice().reverse(); // closest-of-below first (just under closest)
      const winnerPrize = computeCryptoWinnerShare();
      const closestRow = all[closestIdx];

      function buildRow(row, isClosest){
        const rank = rankByUserId.get(row.user_id) || '—';
        const isMe = !!(me && row.user_id === me.id);
        const reveal = cryptoRevealed || isMe;
        let ptsValue;
        if (reveal) {
          const diff = (livePx != null) ? Math.abs(row._tip - livePx) : null;
          const tipPx = fmtCryptoPrice(coin, row._tip);
          const diffHtml = diff != null
            ? `<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:${isClosest?'var(--gold)':'var(--muted)'};font-weight:${isClosest?700:500}">Δ ${fmtCryptoPrice(coin, diff)}</span>`
            : `<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">no live</span>`;
          ptsValue = tipPx + diffHtml;
        } else {
          ptsValue = `<span style="color:var(--muted)">🔒</span><br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">locked</span>`;
        }
        // Prize % (33% of crypto pot) is public — derives from rank, which is also public.
        // Only the actual tip price stays hidden until first-match kickoff.
        // Settled state: pot winner renders green "✓ paid"; a closer guess that
        // forfeited on the 26× cap (0 BOBAI holdings) renders the amber cap row —
        // same visual language as the settled groups.
        const isWinner = row.user_id === payoutWinnerId;
        const isForfeit = CRYPTO_FORFEITS[coin] === row.user_id;
        return userRow(row, rank, {
          ptsLabel: '',
          ptsValue,
          ptsClass: 'diff',
          prizeBobai: isWinner ? winnerPrize : 0,
          contextPot: pool.crypto,
          extraClass: isForfeit ? 'forfeit-inline' : (isWinner ? 'paid-inline' : (isClosest ? 'closest' : '')),
          forfeit: isForfeit,
          paidPill: isWinner,
        });
      }

      const coinImg = `<img class="coin-badge ${coin}" src="${coinIconPath(coin)}" alt="">`;
      const liveBadge = livePx != null
        ? `<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px;background:rgba(0,230,118,.04);border:1px solid rgba(0,230,118,.2);border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:14px;font-size:12px;color:#5fffa0">${coinImg}Final kickoff ${display}: <b>${fmtCryptoPrice(coin, livePx)}</b> · frozen 19 Jul 2026, 19:00 UTC · winner-take-all on the closest guess</div>`
        : `<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px;background:rgba(255,180,0,.04);border:1px solid rgba(255,180,0,.2);border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:14px;font-size:12px;color:#ffc066">${coinImg}${display} price unavailable — showing predictions only</div>`;

      const aboveSection = aboveDisplay.length
        ? `<div class="crypto-divider up">▲ ${display} wins if price rises</div>` +
          aboveDisplay.map(row => buildRow(row, false)).join('')
        : '';
      const closestHtml = buildRow(closestRow, true);
      const belowSection = belowDisplay.length
        ? belowDisplay.map(row => buildRow(row, false)).join('') +
          `<div class="crypto-divider down">▼ ${display} wins if price falls</div>`
        : '';

      // "Me" handling: if not in the shown set, append separately
      const shownIds = new Set([closestRow.user_id, ...aboveDisplay.map(r0=>r0.user_id), ...belowDisplay.map(r0=>r0.user_id)]);
      const meRowData = me ? all.find(r0 => r0.user_id === me.id) : null;
      const meExtraHtml = (me && meRowData && !shownIds.has(me.id))
        ? `<div class="rank-gap">···</div>` + buildRow(meRowData, false)
        : '';

      // Footer
      let footer = '';
      if (me) {
        if (meRowData) {
          const meRank = rankByUserId.get(meRowData.user_id);
          // Prices are frozen at the Final kickoff — the board is final, no flip math anymore.
          const status = meRank === 1
            ? `<span class="mf-status qualified">✓ Closest guess — wins the ${display} pot</span>`
            : `<span class="mf-status need">Δ ${fmtCryptoPrice(coin, Math.abs(meRowData._tip - livePx))} off the kickoff price</span>`;
          footer = `<div class="me-footer">
            <div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-rank">#${meRank}</span><span class="mf-status">of ${all.length} prediction${all.length===1?'':'s'}</span></div>
            <div>${status}</div></div>`;
        } else {
          footer = `<div class="me-footer"><div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-status">You haven't set a ${display} prediction yet.</span></div></div>`;
        }
      }

      $('#list').innerHTML = liveBadge + aboveSection + closestHtml + belowSection + meExtraHtml + footer;
    }

    // ============== PAYOUTS (total prize money per wallet-linked user) ==============
    // Global ranking by projected total payout across all 3 pots — only shows wallet-linked
    // users (skip+promote is already applied: non-eligible players don't get a slot, so they
    // simply don't appear here). Live, recomputes from the same data the rest of the
    // leaderboard uses.
    async function renderPayouts(){
      $('#sub-selector').classList.add('hidden');
      $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading projections…</div>';
      if (!window.WC_PROJECTION) {
        $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Projection helper not loaded.</div>';
        return;
      }
      window.WC_PROJECTION.injectStyles();
      const data = await window.WC_PROJECTION.loadAll();
      if (!data || data.error) {
        $('#list').innerHTML = `<div class="empty">${(data && data.error) || 'Could not load projections.'}</div>`;
        return;
      }
      const entries = window.WC_PROJECTION.computeAll(data);
      if (!entries.length) {
        $('#list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">No wallet-linked players yet — once players link a wallet they show up here with their projected payout.</div>';
        return;
      }
      const meId = me ? me.id : null;
      const intro = `<div class="section-h" style="margin-top:0"><span>Projected payouts — wallet-linked players only</span><span class="right">summed across End-Pool + Groups + Crypto</span></div>
        <div class="empty" style="text-align:left;font-size:12px;padding:10px 12px;background:rgba(240,185,11,.04);border:1px solid rgba(240,185,11,.18);border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:10px;color:var(--text)">Each row sums what that wallet would receive across all 3 pots if the current standings held to settlement. Chips show which pots contribute. Click a name to open their profile.</div>`;
      const rowsHtml = entries.map((e, i) =>
        window.WC_PROJECTION.renderPayoutListRowHtml(e, i + 1, data.pool, { meId })
      ).join('');
      const scrollCls = entries.length > 10 ? 'list scroll-cap' : 'list';
      const myIdx = meId ? entries.findIndex(e => e.user_id === meId) : -1;
      let footer = '';
      if (me) {
        if (myIdx >= 0) {
          const myEntry = entries[myIdx];
          const myPct = data.pool.total > 0 ? (myEntry.totalBobai / data.pool.total * 100) : 0;
          footer = `<div class="me-footer">
            <div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-rank">#${myIdx + 1}</span><span class="mf-status">of ${entries.length} wallet-linked player${entries.length===1?'':'s'}</span></div>
            <div><span class="mf-status qualified">You'd receive ${myPct.toFixed(myPct < 0.1 ? 2 : 1)}% of the full pool right now</span></div>
          </div>`;
        } else if (!me.wallet) {
          footer = `<div class="me-footer">
            <div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-status">You're not in this list — <a href="./dashboard.html" style="color:var(--gold);font-weight:700">link a wallet</a> to be eligible for payouts.</span></div>
          </div>`;
        } else {
          footer = `<div class="me-footer">
            <div class="mf-left"><span class="mf-status">Wallet linked, but at current standings you wouldn't receive a payout — keep tipping.</span></div>
          </div>`;
        }
      }
      $('#list').innerHTML = intro + `<div class="${scrollCls}">${rowsHtml}</div>` + footer;
      if (window.twemoji) try { window.twemoji.parse($('#list'), { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    // ============== SUB-SELECTORS ==============
    function showGroupSelector(){
      const sel = $('#sub-selector');
      sel.classList.remove('hidden');
      sel.innerHTML = GROUP_LETTERS.map(L =>
        `<button data-letter="${L}" class="${subView===L?'active':''}">Group ${L}</button>`
      ).join('');
      $$('#sub-selector button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        subView = b.dataset.letter; showGroupSelector(); renderGroup(subView);
      }));
    }
    function showPhaseSelector(){
      const sel = $('#sub-selector');
      sel.classList.remove('hidden');
      sel.innerHTML = KO_PHASES.map(p =>
        `<button data-phase="${p.key}" class="${subView===p.key?'active':''}">${p.label}</button>`
      ).join('');
      $$('#sub-selector button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        subView = b.dataset.phase; showPhaseSelector(); renderBracket();
      }));
    }
    function showBonusSelector(){
      const sel = $('#sub-selector');
      sel.classList.remove('hidden');
      sel.innerHTML = BONUS_TABS.map(t =>
        `<button data-bonus="${t.key}" class="${subView===t.key?'active':''}">${t.label}</button>`
      ).join('');
      $$('#sub-selector button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        subView = b.dataset.bonus; showBonusSelector(); renderBonus();
      }));
    }
    function showCoinSelector(){
      const sel = $('#sub-selector');
      sel.classList.remove('hidden');
      sel.innerHTML = COINS.map(c =>
        `<button data-coin="${c.code}" class="${subView===c.code?'active':''}"><img class="coin-ic ${c.code}" src="${c.icon}" alt="">${c.label}</button>`
      ).join('');
      $$('#sub-selector button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        subView = b.dataset.coin; showCoinSelector(); renderCrypto(subView);
      }));
    }

    function switchView(v){
      view = v;
      $$('#lbar button').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.view === v));
      // The wallet filter is meaningless in the payouts view (list is always wallet-linked
      // only by definition), so hide the toggle there.
      const wf = $('#wallet-filter');
      if (wf) wf.classList.toggle('hidden', v === 'payouts');
      if (v === 'overall') {
        subView = null; $('#sub-selector').classList.add('hidden'); renderOverall();
      } else if (v === 'groups') {
        if (!subView || !GROUP_LETTERS.includes(subView)) subView = 'A';
        showGroupSelector(); renderGroup(subView);
      } else if (v === 'bracket') {
        if (!subView || !KO_PHASES.find(p => p.key === subView)) subView = 'r32';
        showPhaseSelector(); renderBracket();
      } else if (v === 'bonus') {
        if (!subView || !BONUS_TABS.find(t => t.key === subView)) subView = 'champion';
        showBonusSelector(); renderBonus();
      } else if (v === 'payouts') {
        subView = null; $('#sub-selector').classList.add('hidden'); renderPayouts();
      } else {
        if (!subView || !['btc','bnb','bobai'].includes(subView)) subView = 'btc';
        showCoinSelector(); renderCrypto(subView);
      }
    }

    $$('#lbar button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => switchView(b.dataset.view)));

    // Wallet-only toggle — sync initial UI, then re-render current view on each click.
    function syncWalletFilterButtons(){
      $$('#wallet-filter button').forEach(b =>
        b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.filter === (walletOnly ? 'wallet' : 'all'))
      );
    }
    syncWalletFilterButtons();
    $$('#wallet-filter button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const next = b.dataset.filter === 'wallet';
      if (next === walletOnly) return;
      walletOnly = next;
      try { localStorage.setItem('wc_wallet_filter', walletOnly ? '1' : '0'); } catch(e){}
      syncWalletFilterButtons();
      switchView(view); // preserves subView for groups/bracket/crypto
    }));

    (async ()=>{
      me = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      // Public archive page — anonymous visitors see the full board, `me` stays null.
      await loadPool();
      switchView('overall');
      setInterval(loadPool, 30000);
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

    .pool-cell .pct{font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px}

    /* SECTION HEADER */
    .section-h{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;margin:0 4px 10px;font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:600;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--gold);letter-spacing:1.5px;text-transform:uppercase}

    .ptable-wrap{background:rgba(0,0,0,.3);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;overflow-x:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;margin-bottom:24px;scrollbar-width:thin;scrollbar-color:rgba(240,185,11,.3) transparent}

    .ptable-wrap::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:rgba(240,185,11,.25);border-radius:3px}
    .ptable{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px}
    .ptable thead{background:rgba(0,0,0,.4)}
    .ptable th{padding:9px 10px;text-align:left;font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:600;font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;white-space:nowrap;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04)}
    .ptable td{padding:9px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04);color:var(--text);vertical-align:middle}
    .ptable tbody tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none}
    .ptable tr.pot-h td{background:rgba(240,185,11,.05);color:var(--gold);font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:700;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase}
    .ptable td.rank-icon{width:34px;text-align:center;font-size:14px}
    .ptable td.tnum{font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:600;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}
    .ptable td.tusd{color:var(--gold);font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}
    .ptable .pct-cell{color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap}

    /* TX TABLE */
    .txwrap{background:rgba(0,0,0,.3);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:24px}
    .txscroll{max-height:400px;overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;scrollbar-width:thin;scrollbar-color:rgba(240,185,11,.3) transparent}
    .txscroll::-webkit-scrollbar{width:6px;height:6px}
    .txscroll::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:rgba(240,185,11,.25);border-radius:3px}
    .txfilter{display:flex;gap:4px;padding:8px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.4);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
    .txfilter button{padding:6px 11px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;border:1px solid var(--border);background:rgba(0,0,0,.3);color:var(--muted);border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .15s;font-family:inherit;letter-spacing:.3px}
    .txfilter button:hover{border-color:rgba(240,185,11,.3)}
    .txfilter button.active{background:var(--gold-glow);color:var(--gold);border-color:var(--gold)}
    .txfilter .right{margin-left:auto;display:flex;align-items:center;font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted);padding:0 6px}
    .txfilter .right a{color:var(--gold);text-decoration:none}
    .txfilter .right a:hover{text-decoration:underline}

    .txtable{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px}
    .txtable th,.txtable td{padding:9px 10px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04)}
    .txtable th{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:2;font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:600;font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;background:#0c0c1c;white-space:nowrap}
    .txtable td{color:var(--text);vertical-align:middle}
    .txtable tbody tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none}
    .txtable tbody tr:hover{background:rgba(240,185,11,.03)}

              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">2</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Read wallet eligibility</b> — only players with a linked BEP-20 wallet receive transfers.
                  <span class="muted">Unlinked players keep leaderboard position but their share redistributes to eligible winners.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">3</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Read on-chain BOBAI balance</b> of each eligible wallet (live from blockchain, not from a database — un-fakeable).
                  <span class="muted">Used for the 26× holder cap + tie-breaker.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">4</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Compute raw shares</b> per pot:
                  <span class="muted">Group: 55 % / 30 % / 15 % flat across 1st/2nd/best-3rd brackets. End pool: 50/25/15/10 % tiered for top 4. Crypto: 3.3 %/coin to closest guesses.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">5</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Apply 26× holder cap</b> per winner, <b>per pot independently</b>. Excess flows to remaining uncapped winners proportionally within the same pot — never crosses pot boundaries.
                  <span class="muted">Cascade repeats until every dollar lands within someone's cap. Any residual that no eligible winner can absorb is added as <b>permanent BOBAI/BNB liquidity</b> on PancakeSwap with LP burned to the dead address — never refunded, never kept.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">6</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Test payout: 1 % first</b>. Admin signs a tiny test batch to verify addresses + amounts on-chain before full execution.
                  <span class="muted">If anything looks off, full payout aborts. No 99 % surprise.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">7</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Full payout in randomized group order</b> (e.g. Group F before Group A) — so on-chain observers can't match wallets to group rank.
                  <span class="muted">Only End-Pool top 4 + 3 Crypto winners stay matchable, since those are unique single-winner positions already on the leaderboard.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flow-step">
                <div class="num">8</div>
                <div class="body">
                  <b>Audit PDF published</b> after each run.
                  <span class="muted">Lists every winner, raw share, cap applied, redistribution rounds, and final tx hash. Verifiable from this page.</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flow-note">
              💡 The same player can win in <b>multiple groups</b> (e.g. 1st in A + 2nd in D + best-3rd in F) — each placement is paid separately. Linked wallet is keyed to a username, not to a single group.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>

    <!-- ===== PAYOUT RECEIPT MODAL (group + final, content set per pot) ===== -->
    <div class="rc-modal" id="receipt-modal" aria-hidden="true" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="rc-title" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeReceipt()">
      <div class="rc-backdrop" onclick="closeReceipt()"></div>
      <div class="rc-box" role="document">
        <button class="rc-close" type="button" onclick="closeReceipt()" aria-label="Close">×</button>
        <div class="rc-header">
          <div class="illus">
            <img src="./illus/receipt-payout.webp" alt="" onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='./illus/receipt-payout.png'">
