  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/config.js?v=12"></script>
  <script src="./assets/countries.js?v=11"></script>
  <script src="./assets/auth.js?v=13"></script>
  <script src="./assets/profile.js?v=18"></script>
  <script src="./assets/avatar.js?v=7"></script>
  <script src="./assets/nav.js?v=6"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);

    function fmtBobai(n){
      if (n == null) return '0';
      const v = Number(n);
      if (v >= 1_000_000) return (v/1_000_000).toFixed(2) + 'M';
      if (v >= 1_000)     return (v/1_000).toFixed(1) + 'K';
      return v.toFixed(0);
    }
    // Returns formatted USD or 'n/a' if price feed unavailable. Use this anywhere
    // a USD value is derived from `bobai_price_usd` so an outage doesn't show $0.00.
    function fmtUsdOrNa(bobaiAmount, price){
      return price > 0 ? fmtUsd(bobaiAmount * price) : 'n/a';
    }
    function fmtUsd(n){
      if (n == null || !isFinite(n)) return '$0.00';
      if (n >= 1_000_000) return '$' + (n/1_000_000).toFixed(2) + 'M';
      if (n >= 1_000)     return '$' + (n/1_000).toFixed(1) + 'K';
      if (n >= 1)         return '$' + n.toFixed(2);
      if (n > 0)          return '$' + n.toFixed(4);
      return '$0.00';
    }
    function fmtAmountIn(token, n){
      const v = parseFloat(n) || 0;
      if (v >= 1) return v.toFixed(4) + ' ' + token;
      return v.toFixed(6) + ' ' + token;
    }
    function fmtTime(iso){
      if (!iso) return '—';
      const d = new Date(iso);
      const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2,'0');
      return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getUTCMonth()+1) + '-' + pad(d.getUTCDate()) + ' ' + pad(d.getUTCHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getUTCMinutes());
    }
    function txLink(h){
      if (!h) return '—';
      return `<a class="txl" href="https://bscscan.com/tx/${h}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${h.slice(0,6)}…${h.slice(-4)}</a>`;
    }

    // Latest pool state (for $ calculations on tx rows)
    let livePrice = 0;
    let liveTotalBobai = 0;

    let groupPaidAt = null;
    let finalPaidAt = null;      // derived from wc_payouts pot=end/crypto rows
    let payoutRowsCache = null;  // all wc_payouts rows, loaded once per refresh

    async function loadAllPayoutRows(){
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadPayouts();
      if (r.error) return payoutRowsCache || [];
      payoutRowsCache = r.rows || [];
      const finalRows = payoutRowsCache.filter(x => x.pot === 'end' || x.pot === 'crypto');
      const paidTs = finalRows.filter(x => x.tx_hash && x.paid_at).map(x => new Date(x.paid_at).getTime());
      finalPaidAt = paidTs.length ? new Date(Math.min(...paidTs)).toISOString() : null;
      return payoutRowsCache;
    }

    async function loadPool(){
      const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.loadPool();
      if (r.error) return;
      const p = r.pool || {};
      const total = parseFloat(p.total_bobai) || 0;
      const px    = parseFloat(p.bobai_price_usd) || 0;
      const group  = parseFloat(p.group_pot)  || 0;
      const end    = parseFloat(p.endpool)    || 0;
      const crypto = parseFloat(p.crypto_pot) || 0;
      groupPaidAt = p.group_paid_at || null;

      livePrice = px;
      liveTotalBobai = total;

      $('#pool-bobai').textContent  = fmtBobai(total) + ' BOBAI';
      $('#pool-usd').textContent    = '≈ ' + fmtUsdOrNa(total, px);
      $('#pool-group').textContent  = fmtBobai(group) + ' BOBAI';
      $('#pool-end').textContent    = fmtBobai(end) + ' BOBAI';
      $('#pool-crypto').textContent = fmtBobai(crypto) + ' BOBAI';
      $('#pool-group-usd').textContent  = '≈ ' + fmtUsdOrNa(group,  px);
      $('#pool-end-usd').textContent    = '≈ ' + fmtUsdOrNa(end,    px);
      $('#pool-crypto-usd').textContent = '≈ ' + fmtUsdOrNa(crypto, px);
      if (p.updated_at) {
        $('#pool-updated').textContent = 'updated ' + new Date(p.updated_at).toLocaleTimeString();
      }

      // Toggle paid-out visual state once the admin marks the group pot paid.
      const cell = $('#pool-group-cell');
      const pct  = $('#pool-group-pct');
      const lbl  = $('#pool-group-lbl');
      const lblEnd = $('#pool-end-lbl');
      const btn  = $('#receipt-btn');
      if (groupPaidAt) {
        cell.classList.add('paid');
        const d = new Date(groupPaidAt);
        const datePill = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        pct.innerHTML = `<span class="paid-pill">✅ Paid · ${datePill}</span>`;
        lbl.textContent = 'Group pot · 60 % (final snapshot)';
        lblEnd.textContent = 'End pool · 90 %';
        btn.style.display = '';
      } else {
        cell.classList.remove('paid');
        pct.textContent = 'paid 1 day after group stage';
        lbl.textContent = 'Group pot · 60 %';
        lblEnd.textContent = 'End pool · 30 %';
        btn.style.display = 'none';
      }

      // End-pool + Crypto cells flip to paid once wc_payouts carries confirmed
      // end/crypto rows (Final payout 2026-07-20).
      await loadAllPayoutRows();
      const finalPaid = !!finalPaidAt;
      const datePillF = finalPaid ? finalPaidAt.slice(0, 10) : null;
      for (const [cellId, pctId, btnId] of [
        ['pool-end-cell',    'pool-end-pct',    'receipt-btn-final'],
        ['pool-crypto-cell', 'pool-crypto-pct', 'receipt-btn-final2'],
      ]) {
        const c = $('#' + cellId), pc = $('#' + pctId), b = $('#' + btnId);
        if (finalPaid) {
          c.classList.add('paid');
          pc.innerHTML = `<span class="paid-pill">✅ Paid · ${datePillF}</span>`;
          b.style.display = '';
          $('#pool-end-lbl').textContent = 'End pool · 90 % (final snapshot)';
        } else {
          c.classList.remove('paid');
          pc.textContent = 'paid 1 day after Final';
          b.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }

      renderPayouts(group, end, crypto, px, !!groupPaidAt, finalPaid);
    }

    // Per-position expected payouts (kept in sync with the leaderboard's POOL_SPLIT)
    function renderPayouts(group, end, crypto, px, groupPaid, finalPaid){
      const tbody = $('#payout-body');
      const rows = [];

      // Pool share = % of the pot named in the section header (group/end/crypto).
      // Group brackets are FLAT within: 12 winners share the bracket pot equally.
      const groupSection = groupPaid
        ? 'Group pot · ✅ paid out'
        : 'Group pot · paid 1 day after group stage';
      rows.push({ section: groupSection, sectionPot: 'group' });
      rows.push({ pot:'group', icon:'🥇', label:'Group 1st', slots:12, share:0.55, bobai: (group*0.55)/12 });
      rows.push({ pot:'group', icon:'🥈', label:'Group 2nd', slots:12, share:0.30, bobai: (group*0.30)/12 });
      rows.push({ pot:'group', icon:'🥉', label:'Best 3rd',  slots:8,  share:0.15, bobai: (group*0.15)/8  });

      rows.push({ section: finalPaid ? 'End pool · ✅ paid out' : 'End pool · paid 1 day after Final', sectionPot: 'end' });
      rows.push({ pot:'end', icon:'🥇', label:'Overall 1st', slots:1, share:0.50, bobai: end*0.50 });
      rows.push({ pot:'end', icon:'🥈', label:'Overall 2nd', slots:1, share:0.25, bobai: end*0.25 });
      rows.push({ pot:'end', icon:'🥉', label:'Overall 3rd', slots:1, share:0.15, bobai: end*0.15 });
      rows.push({ pot:'end', icon:'4️⃣', label:'Overall 4th', slots:1, share:0.10, bobai: end*0.10 });

      rows.push({ section: finalPaid ? 'Crypto pot · ✅ paid out' : 'Crypto pot · paid 1 day after Final', sectionPot: 'crypto' });
      rows.push({ pot:'crypto', icon:'₿',  label:'Closest BTC guess',   slots:1, share:1/3, bobai: crypto/3 });
      rows.push({ pot:'crypto', icon:'🟡', label:'Closest BNB guess',   slots:1, share:1/3, bobai: crypto/3 });
      rows.push({ pot:'crypto', icon:'🧠', label:'Closest BOBAI guess', slots:1, share:1/3, bobai: crypto/3 });

      tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(r => {
        if (r.section) {
          const paidSection = (groupPaid && r.sectionPot === 'group') || (finalPaid && r.sectionPot !== 'group');
          return `<tr class="${paidSection ? 'pot-h paid' : 'pot-h'}"><td colspan="6">${r.section}</td></tr>`;
        }
        const slotsLabel = r.slots > 1 ? `× ${r.slots}` : '× 1';
        const pctLabel = (r.share * 100).toFixed(r.share < 0.1 ? 2 : (r.share < 1 ? 1 : 0)) + ' %';
        const paidRow = (groupPaid && r.pot === 'group') || (finalPaid && (r.pot === 'end' || r.pot === 'crypto'));
        return `<tr class="${paidRow ? 'row paid' : 'row'}">
          <td class="rank-icon">${r.icon}</td>
          <td>${r.label}</td>
          <td class="pct-cell">${slotsLabel}</td>
          <td class="pct-cell" style="text-align:right">${pctLabel}</td>
          <td class="tnum">${fmtBobai(r.bobai)}</td>
          <td class="tusd">${fmtUsdOrNa(r.bobai, px)}</td>
        </tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    // ─── Receipt Modal (generic: 'group' = group pot · 'final' = end-pool + crypto) ───
    let receiptLoadedKind = null;
    let receiptKind = 'group';

    async function loadReceipt(kind){
      const body = $('#rc-body');
      try {
        const rows = await loadAllPayoutRows();
        if (kind === 'final') {
          renderFinalReceipt(rows.filter(r => r.pot === 'end' || r.pot === 'crypto'));
        } else {
          renderReceipt(rows.filter(r => r.pot === 'group'));
        }
        receiptLoadedKind = kind;
      } catch (e) {
        body.innerHTML = `<div class="rc-empty">Could not load receipt: ${e.message}</div>`;
      }
    }

    function positionOrder(pos){
      const order = { '1st': 1, '2nd': 2, 'best_3rd': 3 };
      return order[pos] ?? 99;
    }
    function positionLabel(pos){
      if (pos === '1st') return '🥇 1st';
      if (pos === '2nd') return '🥈 2nd';
      if (pos === 'best_3rd') return '🥉 Best 3rd';
      return pos;
    }
    function countryFlag(code){
      if (!code || code.length !== 2) return '';
      const A = 0x1F1E6, base = 'A'.charCodeAt(0);
      return String.fromCodePoint(A + (code.toUpperCase().charCodeAt(0) - base))
           + String.fromCodePoint(A + (code.toUpperCase().charCodeAt(1) - base));
    }
    function countryName(code){
      // Optional: window.WC_COUNTRIES is the same object the rest of the app uses
      if (window.WC_COUNTRIES && code) {
        const c = window.WC_COUNTRIES.find?.(x => x.code === code);
        if (c?.name) return c.name;
      }
      return code || '';
    }

    function renderReceipt(rows){
      const body = $('#rc-body');
      if (!rows.length) {
        body.innerHTML = `<div class="rc-empty">
          <div style="font-size:32px;margin-bottom:8px">⏳</div>
          <div style="font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:700;font-size:14px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:6px">Payout in progress</div>
          <div>Transactions will appear here as they confirm on-chain.<br>Refresh to update.</div>
        </div>`;
        $('#rc-total-bobai').textContent = '—';
        $('#rc-total-usd').textContent = '—';
        $('#rc-total-wallets').textContent = '—';
        return;
      }

      // Group by group_letter (A..L)
      const groups = {};
      let totalBobai = 0;
      const wallets = new Set();
      for (const r of rows) {
        const key = r.group_letter || '?';
        (groups[key] ||= []).push(r);
        totalBobai += Number(r.bobai_amount) || 0;
        if (r.wallet) wallets.add(r.wallet.toLowerCase());
      }
      const letters = Object.keys(groups).sort();

      // Header totals
      $('#rc-total-bobai').textContent = fmtBobai(totalBobai) + ' BOBAI';
      $('#rc-total-usd').textContent   = fmtUsdOrNa(totalBobai, livePrice);
      $('#rc-total-wallets').textContent = wallets.size;
      const earliest = rows.reduce((a, r) => {
        const t = new Date(r.paid_at).getTime();
        return (!a || t < a) ? t : a;
      }, null);
      if (earliest) $('#rc-date').textContent = new Date(earliest).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      body.innerHTML = letters.map(letter => {
        const list = groups[letter].slice().sort((a, b) => positionOrder(a.position) - positionOrder(b.position));
        const sum = list.reduce((s, r) => s + (Number(r.bobai_amount) || 0), 0);
        return `<div class="rc-group">
          <div class="rc-group-h">
            <span>Group ${letter}</span>
            <span class="right">${fmtBobai(sum)} BOBAI · ${list.length} winner${list.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rc-rows">
            ${list.map(r => `
              <div class="rc-row">
                <div class="ic">${positionLabel(r.position).split(' ')[0]}</div>
                <div class="who">
                  <div class="name">${escapeHtml(r.username || '—')}</div>
                  <div class="country">${countryFlag(r.country_code)} ${escapeHtml(countryName(r.country_code))}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="amt">
                  <div class="b">${fmtBobai(Number(r.bobai_amount))} BOBAI</div>
                  <span class="u">${r.usd_at_payout != null ? fmtUsd(Number(r.usd_at_payout)) : fmtUsdOrNa(Number(r.bobai_amount), livePrice)}</span>
                </div>
                ${r.tx_hash
                  ? `<span class="tx tx-paid" title="On-chain confirmed. Verify aggregate outflows on the prize wallet (linked in footer).">✓ paid</span>`
                  : (Number(r.bobai_amount) === 0
                      ? `<span class="tx-pending" style="color:#ffc066;border-color:rgba(255,180,0,.32)">26× cap not reached</span>`
                      : `<span class="tx-pending">pending</span>`)}
              </div>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    // ─── Final receipt (End-Pool top-4 + 3 crypto pots, paid 2026-07-20) ──
    function finalSlotMeta(pos){
      const M = {
        end_1:        { ic: '🥇', label: 'Overall 1st · 50 %' },
        end_2:        { ic: '🥈', label: 'Overall 2nd · 25 %' },
        end_3:        { ic: '🥉', label: 'Overall 3rd · 15 %' },
        end_4:        { ic: '4️⃣', label: 'Overall 4th · 10 %' },
        crypto_btc:   { ic: '₿',  label: 'Closest BTC guess' },
        crypto_bnb:   { ic: '🟡', label: 'Closest BNB guess' },
        crypto_bobai: { ic: '🧠', label: 'Closest BOBAI guess' },
      };
      return M[pos] || { ic: '·', label: pos };
    }
    function renderFinalReceipt(rows){
      const body = $('#rc-body');
      if (!rows.length) {
        body.innerHTML = `<div class="rc-empty">
          <div style="font-size:32px;margin-bottom:8px">⏳</div>
          <div style="font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:700;font-size:14px;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:6px">Payout in progress</div>
          <div>Transactions will appear here as they confirm on-chain.<br>Refresh to update.</div>
        </div>`;
        $('#rc-total-bobai').textContent = '—';
        $('#rc-total-usd').textContent = '—';
        $('#rc-total-wallets').textContent = '—';
        return;
      }
      let totalBobai = 0;
      const wallets = new Set();
      rows.forEach(r => {
        totalBobai += Number(r.bobai_amount) || 0;
        if (r.wallet && Number(r.bobai_amount) > 0) wallets.add(r.wallet.toLowerCase());
      });
      $('#rc-total-bobai').textContent = fmtBobai(totalBobai) + ' BOBAI';
      $('#rc-total-usd').textContent   = fmtUsdOrNa(totalBobai, livePrice);
      $('#rc-total-wallets').textContent = wallets.size;
      const earliest = rows.reduce((a, r) => {
        if (!r.paid_at) return a;
        const t = new Date(r.paid_at).getTime();
        return (!a || t < a) ? t : a;
      }, null);
      if (earliest) $('#rc-date').textContent = new Date(earliest).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      const order = ['end_1','end_2','end_3','end_4','crypto_btc','crypto_bnb','crypto_bobai'];
      const byPos = new Map(rows.map(r => [r.position, r]));
      const sections = [
        { title: 'End Pool — Overall Top 4 (50 / 25 / 15 / 10 %)', slots: order.slice(0, 4) },
        { title: 'Crypto Pots — winner-take-all per coin (frozen at Final kickoff, 19 Jul 19:00 UTC)', slots: order.slice(4) },
      ];
      body.innerHTML = sections.map(sec => {
        const list = sec.slots.map(p => byPos.get(p)).filter(Boolean);
        const sum = list.reduce((s, r) => s + (Number(r.bobai_amount) || 0), 0);
        return `<div class="rc-group">
          <div class="rc-group-h">
            <span>${sec.title}</span>
            <span class="right">${fmtBobai(sum)} BOBAI</span>
          </div>
          <div class="rc-rows">
            ${list.map(r => {
              const meta = finalSlotMeta(r.position);
              const zero = Number(r.bobai_amount) === 0;
              const note = r.notes ? `<div class="country" style="color:#ffc066">${escapeHtml(r.notes)}</div>` : '';
              return `<div class="rc-row">
                <div class="ic">${meta.ic}</div>
                <div class="who">
                  <div class="name">${escapeHtml(r.username || '—')} <span style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:500;font-size:11px">· ${meta.label}</span></div>
                  <div class="country">${countryFlag(r.country_code)} ${escapeHtml(countryName(r.country_code))}</div>
                  ${note}
                </div>
                <div class="amt">
                  <div class="b">${fmtBobai(Number(r.bobai_amount))} BOBAI</div>
                  <span class="u">${r.usd_at_payout != null ? fmtUsd(Number(r.usd_at_payout)) : fmtUsdOrNa(Number(r.bobai_amount), livePrice)}</span>
                </div>
                ${r.tx_hash
                  ? `<span class="tx tx-paid" title="On-chain confirmed. Verify aggregate outflows on the prize wallet (linked in footer).">✓ paid</span>`
                  : (zero
                      ? `<span class="tx-pending" style="color:#ffc066;border-color:rgba(255,180,0,.32)">26× cap not reached</span>`
                      : `<span class="tx-pending">pending</span>`)}
              </div>`;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    function escapeHtml(s){
      return String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
    }

    async function openReceipt(kind){
      receiptKind = kind || 'group';
      // Header copy per pot
      if (receiptKind === 'final') {
        $('#rc-title').textContent = 'Final · Payout Receipt';
        $('#rc-sub').innerHTML = 'End-Pool top 4 + 3 crypto pots · paid <b id="rc-date">—</b>';
      } else {
        $('#rc-title').textContent = 'Group Pot · Payout Receipt';
        $('#rc-sub').innerHTML = '12 groups · 32 winners · paid <b id="rc-date">—</b>';
      }
      $('#receipt-modal').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      await loadReceipt(receiptKind);  // always fresh — #rc-date lives inside the rebuilt sub-line
      if (window.twemoji) window.twemoji.parse($('#receipt-modal'));
    }
    function closeReceipt(){
      $('#receipt-modal').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && $('#receipt-modal').getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'false') closeReceipt();
    });
    async function shareReceipt(){
      const url = location.origin + location.pathname + '?receipt=' + receiptKind;
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        const b = document.querySelector('.rc-footer .share');
        const orig = b.textContent;
        b.textContent = '✓ Copied';
        setTimeout(() => { b.textContent = orig; }, 1500);
      } catch { /* ignore */ }
    }
    // Make the modal-control functions reachable from inline onclick attributes
    window.openReceipt = openReceipt;
    window.closeReceipt = closeReceipt;
    window.shareReceipt = shareReceipt;

    // ─── Tx History ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    let currentFilter = 'all';
    let txCache = [];

    async function loadTxs(){
      try {
        const sb = supabase.createClient(window.WC_CONFIG.SUPABASE_URL, window.WC_CONFIG.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
        const { data, error } = await sb
          .from('wc_donations')
          .select('id, from_address, token, amount_in, amount_bobai, tx_hash, swap_tx_hash, created_at')
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
          .limit(100);
        if (error) { console.warn('tx fetch:', error.message); return; }
        txCache = data || [];
        renderTxs();
      } catch (e) { console.warn('tx fetch error:', e.message); }
    }

    function renderTxs(){
      const tbody = $('#tx-body');
      const rows = txCache.filter(row => {
        if (currentFilter === 'all')      return true;
        if (currentFilter === 'tax')      return row.token === 'TAX';
        if (currentFilter === 'donation') return row.token === 'BNB' || row.token === 'USDT';
        return true;
      });
      if (!rows.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="txempty">No ${currentFilter === 'all' ? '' : currentFilter + ' '}entries yet — waiting for the first swap.</td></tr>`;
        return;
      }
      tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(r => {
        const chipClass = r.token === 'TAX' ? 'tax' : (r.token === 'BNB' ? 'bnb' : 'usdt');
        const chipLabel = r.token === 'TAX' ? '⚙️ Tax' : (r.token === 'BNB' ? '🟡 BNB' : '💵 USDT');
        const bobai = parseFloat(r.amount_bobai) || 0;
        return `<tr>
          <td class="ttime">${fmtTime(r.created_at)}</td>
          <td><span class="tchip ${chipClass}">${chipLabel}</span></td>
          <td class="tnum">${fmtAmountIn(r.token === 'TAX' ? 'BNB' : r.token, r.amount_in)}</td>
          <td class="tnum">${fmtBobai(bobai)}</td>
          <td class="tusd">${fmtUsdOrNa(bobai, livePrice)}</td>
          <td>${txLink(r.swap_tx_hash || r.tx_hash)}</td>
        </tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    // Filter buttons
    $('#txfilter').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const btn = e.target.closest('button[data-filter]');
      if (!btn) return;
      document.querySelectorAll('#txfilter button').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      currentFilter = btn.dataset.filter;
      renderTxs();
    });

    // ─── Donation address copy + BscScan link ─────────────────────
    (() => {
      const addr = window.WC_CONFIG?.PRIZE_WALLET;
      if (!addr) return;
      const linkEl = $('#prize-addr');
      linkEl.textContent = addr;
      linkEl.href = `https://bscscan.com/address/${addr}`;
      $('#addr-link').href = `https://bscscan.com/address/${addr}`;
      $('#rc-wallet-link').href = `https://bscscan.com/address/${addr}`;
      $('#copy-addr').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        try {
          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(addr);
          const b = $('#copy-addr');
          const orig = b.textContent;
          b.textContent = '✓ Copied';
          setTimeout(() => { b.textContent = orig; }, 1500);
        } catch { /* ignore */ }
      });
    })();

    // ─── Init ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    (async ()=>{
      await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      await loadPool();
      await loadTxs();
      setInterval(loadPool, 30000);
      setInterval(loadTxs, 30000);

      // Auto-open the receipt modal if the link was shared with ?receipt=group|final
      const rq = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('receipt');
      if (rq === 'group' && groupPaidAt) openReceipt('group');
      if (rq === 'final' && finalPaidAt) openReceipt('final');
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

      <div class="nav">

          <span>←</span> Sign in

        <div class="brand">BOBAI Worldcup <span style="opacity:.55;margin:0 4px">·</span> '26</div>
        <span style="width:80px"></span>
      </div>
    </nav>

    <main>
      <div class="container">
        <div class="card">
          <h1><span class="accent">Reset password</span></h1>
          <p class="sub">Choose a new password for your account.</p>

          <div id="alert"></div>

          <form id="form-reset">
            <div class="field">
              <label>New password</label>
              <input type="password" name="password" autocomplete="new-password" minlength="6" required>
              <div class="hint">Min 6 characters.</div>
            </div>
            <div class="field">
              <label>Confirm new password</label>
              <input type="password" name="password_confirm" autocomplete="new-password" minlength="6" required>
            </div>
            <button type="submit" class="btn">Update password</button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/config.js?v=12"></script>
  <script src="./assets/auth.js?v=13"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = $('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }

    // Supabase parses the recovery token from the URL hash automatically
    // and emits a PASSWORD_RECOVERY event. We just need to wait for it (or
    // for an existing session) before allowing the form submit.
    let ready = false;
    window.WC_SB.auth.onAuthStateChange((event) => {
      if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY' || event === 'SIGNED_IN') {
        ready = true;
      }
    });

    // Also check on load — direct navigations may already have a session.
    (async () => {
      const { data } = await window.WC_SB.auth.getSession();
      if (data.session) ready = true;
      if (!ready) {
        // Give the SDK ~500ms to process the recovery hash
        setTimeout(async () => {
          const { data } = await window.WC_SB.auth.getSession();
          if (data.session) ready = true;
        }, 500);
      }
    })();

    $('#form-reset').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      if (!ready) {
        showAlert('error', 'Reset link expired or invalid. Request a new one from the sign-in page.');
        return;
      }
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Updating…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.updatePassword(
        fd.get('password'),
        fd.get('password_confirm'),
      );
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Update password';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); return; }
      showAlert('ok', 'Password updated — redirecting to sign in…');
      await window.WC_AUTH.logout();
      setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = './index.html'; }, 1000);
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

        <div class="card">
          <h1><span class="accent">Rules &amp; FAQ</span></h1>
          <p class="sub">Read this before you play. Especially if it's your first crypto-tipgame.</p>

          <nav class="toc">
            <a href="#disclaimer">⚠ Disclaimer</a>
            <a href="#register">Register</a>
            <a href="#bonus">Bonus</a>
            <a href="#tips">Tips</a>
            <a href="#crypto">Crypto</a>
            <a href="#scoring">Scoring</a>
            <a href="#pool">Prize pool</a>

          <!-- DISCLAIMER -->
          <h2 id="disclaimer">⚠ Disclaimer — read first</h2>
          <div class="callout danger">
            <b>BOBAI Worldcup '26 is a community fan project.</b> Not affiliated with FIFA, BNB Chain, Binance, or any of the listed teams or players. Names, flags and competition data are used for descriptive purposes only.
          </div>
          <div class="callout warn">
            <b>No responsibility, no guarantees.</b> Participation is entirely at your own risk. We do not provide financial, investment, or legal advice. Predictions are entertainment, not a strategy.
            <br><br>
            • If you don't know what you're doing — <b>don't do it.</b><br>
            • Never send funds you can't afford to lose.<br>
            • Smart contracts and wallets can have bugs. Networks can be congested. Transactions can fail.<br>
            • Prize-pool distribution depends on on-chain conditions (gas, slippage, network availability). Payout timing is a target, not a promise.<br>
            • You are responsible for your own taxes wherever you live.<br>
            • If gambling is restricted in your jurisdiction or you are under the local legal age — <b>do not participate.</b>
          </div>
          <p>By creating an account, sending donations or claiming payouts you accept these terms. If you don't agree — close this page and don't take part. The dev team reserves the right to fix bugs, correct match results, and adjust scoring rules transparently if data sources disagree.</p>

          <!-- REGISTER -->
          <h2 id="register">1. Register</h2>
          <p>Create an account with a <b>username + email + password</b>. Pick a country — you'll get a BOBAI in that national jersey as your avatar. Email is used only for confirmation + password reset — never shown publicly, never spammed.</p>
          <p>Before the account is created you'll see a short <b>security briefing</b> (anti-scam, anti-phishing, game disclaimer) that you have to acknowledge. After confirming, a confirmation link is sent to your inbox — click it once and you're in.</p>
          <p>Optionally link a <b>BEP-20 wallet</b> (MetaMask, Trust Wallet, Binance Web3, hardware — any wallet works). You just paste the address — no signing, no app install, no gas. The wallet is only needed if you want to be <b>eligible for prize-pool payouts</b>. You can play purely for fun without one — your username will still appear on the leaderboard, just without the ⚽ icon.</p>
          <div class="callout warn">
            A linked wallet is <b>permanent</b> and <b>unique</b>: it cannot be changed or removed after linking, and the same wallet cannot be used on two different accounts. Double-check the address before confirming.
          </div>

          <!-- BONUS QUESTIONS -->
          <h2 id="bonus">2. Bonus questions (one-time)</h2>
          <p>Five long-shot predictions, each worth <b>20 points</b>:</p>
          <ol>
            <li>Who wins the World Cup? (single team)</li>
            <li>Team with the most goals scored across the tournament</li>
            <li>Team with the fewest goals scored</li>
            <li>Top scorer's country (Golden Boot)</li>
            <li>Total red cards bracket: &lt;5 / 5–9 / 10–14 / 15–19 / &gt;19</li>
          </ol>
          <p><b>Submission deadline:</b> <b>18 June 2026, 15:45 UTC</b> — 15 min before the first matchday-2 match. One-time extension to give late joiners a fair shot. After the deadline: locked — no changes (DB-enforced).</p>

          <!-- TIPS -->
          <h2 id="tips">3. Match tips</h2>
          <p>For every match you tip the <b>exact score</b> (e.g. 2:1). All 72 group-stage matches plus 32 knockout matches.</p>
          <p>Each match locks <b>15 minutes before kickoff</b>. After that: no edits possible (enforced at database level — you literally can't get around it).</p>
          <p>Knockout matches start as <i>TBD vs TBD</i> in the UI and become tippable once the draw resolves them.</p>

          <!-- CRYPTO -->
          <h2 id="crypto">4. Crypto predictions</h2>
          <p>Predict the USD price of <b>BTC, BNB and BOBAI</b> at the exact moment of the World Cup <b>Final kickoff</b> (19 July 2026, 19:00 UTC). Live current prices are shown for reference; you can edit anytime until <b>18 June 2026, 15:45 UTC</b> — 15 min before the first matchday-2 match. After that: locked.</p>
          <p>Scoring is based on <b>absolute difference</b> to the actual price at the snapshot moment — the closest guess on each coin wins that coin's share of the crypto pot.</p>

          <!-- SCORING -->
          <h2 id="scoring">5. Scoring</h2>
          <h3>Match scoring (per match)</h3>
          <p>Raw points stack — first you get points for calling the <b>winner (1/X/2)</b>, then a bonus on top if you also got the <b>goal difference</b>, and another bonus on top if you nailed the <b>exact score</b>:</p>
          <table class="scoring-table">
            <tr><th>Outcome</th><th>Example (tipped 2:1)</th><th>Raw points</th></tr>
            <tr><td>Correct winner only (1 / X / 2)</td><td>Actual 4:0 — right side, wrong margin</td><td>2</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Correct winner <b>+ correct goal difference</b></td><td>Actual 3:2 — right side, 1-goal margin matches (+1)</td><td>3</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Correct winner + GD <b>+ exact result</b></td><td>Actual 2:1 — nailed it (+2)</td><td>5</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Wrong winner / wrong direction</td><td>Actual 0:1 or 1:1</td><td>0</td></tr>
          </table>
          <p style="font-size:12.5px">A correct draw tip (X) only counts if the actual result is also a draw — the score doesn't have to match exactly to get the 2 base points, but you need an actual draw for the <b>+1</b> GD bonus and an exact draw score for the <b>+2</b> exact bonus.</p>
          <h3>Phase multiplier</h3>
          <p>Later matches matter more. Raw points get multiplied by phase — and inside the group stage the multiplier climbs each matchday so totals spread out and fewer players land on the same number of points:</p>
          <table class="scoring-table">
            <tr><th>Phase</th><th>Multiplier</th></tr>
            <tr><td>Group stage — Matchday 1</td><td>×1</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Group stage — Matchday 2</td><td>×1.25</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Group stage — Matchday 3</td><td>×1.5</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Round of 32</td><td>×2</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Round of 16</td><td>×3</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Quarter-finals</td><td>×4</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Semi-finals</td><td>×5</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Third-place match</td><td>×6</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Final</td><td>×7</td></tr>
          </table>
          <p style="font-size:12.5px">A perfect exact-score tip in the Final is worth <b>5 × 7 = 35 points</b> — enough to flip the leaderboard on a single match, which is exactly the point.</p>
          <h3>Bonus &amp; Crypto</h3>
          <p>Bonus questions: <b>20 pts each</b> if correct. Crypto: <b>not counted in points</b> — winners are determined by closest absolute price difference (own pot).</p>
          <h3>Tie-breaker</h3>
          <p>If two players have the exact same point total in a payout group, the player with <b>more BOBAI in their linked wallet at payout time</b> wins. Players without a linked wallet automatically rank below players with one in case of a tie.</p>

          <!-- POOL -->
          <h2 id="pool">6. Prize pool</h2>
          <h3>Where the BOBAI comes from</h3>
          <p>Two sources feed the prize-pool wallet (<code id="addr-hint">0x5E41…D105</code>):</p>
          <ul>
            <li><b>Token tax auto-flow</b>: from <b>11 Jun 2026, 00:01 UTC</b> until the Final (<b>19 Jul 2026, 23:59 UTC</b>), <b>0.52 % of every BOBAI buy/sell tax</b> flows into the prize-pool wallet automatically — <b>0.26 % redirected from the BOB-burn slice</b> + <b>0.26 % from the creator slice</b>. The 3 % on-chain tax rate stays fixed; only the off-chain distribution changes for the WC26 window. After the Final, the split reverts to the standard 1 % BOBAI burn · 1 % BOB burn · 1 % creator.</li>
            <li><b>Donations</b>: anyone can send BNB or USDT (BEP-20) to the prize-pool wallet. Incoming funds are auto-swapped to BOBAI via PancakeSwap and added on-chain. Any amount welcome — see <a href="#donations" style="color:var(--gold)">below</a>.</li>
          </ul>
          <p>Everything is verifiable on BscScan. The pool figure displayed on the leaderboard reads the actual on-chain BOBAI balance — there's nothing in a back-office database we could fudge.</p>
          <h3>Pool split</h3>
          <p>How each incoming BOBAI gets allocated depends on the tournament phase at the moment it arrives:</p>
          <ul>
            <li><b>During group-stage matches</b>: 60 % → Group Pot · 30 % → End Pool · 10 % → Crypto Pot</li>
            <li><b>From R32 onwards</b>: 90 % → End Pool · 10 % → Crypto Pot (Group Pot stops growing — it's paid out before KO starts)</li>
          </ul>

          <!-- PAYOUTS -->
          <h2 id="payouts">7. Payouts</h2>
          <h3>Group Pot (60 % of group-stage collection)</h3>
          <p>Paid <b>one day after the last group-stage match</b>. Split across <b>32 winners</b> from three sub-brackets:</p>
          <table class="scoring-table">
            <tr><th>Sub-bracket</th><th>Who qualifies</th><th>Share of group pot</th></tr>
            <tr><td>Group 1st</td><td>1st-placed player in each of the 12 groups</td><td>55 %</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Group 2nd</td><td>2nd-placed player in each of the 12 groups</td><td>30 %</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Best 3rds</td><td>8 best 3rd-placed players across all groups</td><td>15 %</td></tr>
          </table>
          <p>Within each sub-bracket, the share is <b>split equally</b> among its members — every group 1st gets the same slice of the 55 %, every group 2nd gets the same slice of the 30 %, and every best-3rd gets the same slice of the 15 %. The bracket weights still guarantee that being 1st always pays more than being 2nd, and being 2nd always pays more than just sneaking into best-3rd.</p>
          <table class="scoring-table">
            <tr><th>Position</th><th>Share per player</th></tr>
            <tr><td>Group 1st (×12)</td><td>55 % ÷ 12 ≈ <b>4.58 %</b> of group pot each</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Group 2nd (×12)</td><td>30 % ÷ 12 ≈ <b>2.50 %</b> of group pot each</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Best 3rd (×8)</td><td>15 % ÷ 8 ≈ <b>1.88 %</b> of group pot each</td></tr>
          </table>
          <p style="font-size:12.5px">Flat split inside the bracket = no &ldquo;winner-take-most&rdquo; effect; finishing 1st of your group with 30 group_points pays the same as finishing 1st with 60. The only thing that matters is which position you finished.</p>
          <h3>End Pool (KO-phase pot)</h3>
          <p>Paid <b>one day after the Final</b>. Top 4 overall — based on total score across all phases <b>including bonus questions</b>. Within the top 4 the split is <b>tiered by position</b>:</p>
          <table class="scoring-table">
            <tr><th>Overall finish</th><th>Share of end pool</th></tr>
            <tr><td>🥇 1st</td><td><b>50 %</b></td></tr>
            <tr><td>🥈 2nd</td><td><b>25 %</b></td></tr>
            <tr><td>🥉 3rd</td><td><b>15 %</b></td></tr>
            <tr><td>4th</td><td><b>10 %</b></td></tr>
          </table>
          <p style="font-size:12.5px">Fixed percentages — climbing one position is always worth a meaningful jump. Bonus questions count in the total points that determine your rank, so don't skip them.</p>
          <h3>Crypto Pot (10 %)</h3>
          <p>Paid <b>one day after the Final</b>, alongside the End Pool. Split into 3 equal slots:</p>
          <ul>
            <li>3.3 % of total pool to the closest BTC guess</li>
            <li>3.3 % of total pool to the closest BNB guess</li>
            <li>3.3 % of total pool to the closest BOBAI guess</li>
          </ul>
          <p>The same player can win multiple Crypto Pot slots if they have the best guess on more than one coin.</p>
          <h3>Eligibility</h3>
          <div class="callout warn">
            Only players with a <b>linked wallet</b> at payout time are counted for payouts. Players without a linked wallet keep their leaderboard position for bragging rights, but are <b>skipped</b> when payout slots are filled — the next eligible player on the leaderboard takes the slot. Example: if leaderboard #1 has no wallet, leaderboard #2 (with wallet) becomes payout #1 and receives the 50 % End-Pool share; #3 (with wallet) becomes payout #2, and so on. On every leaderboard you can toggle <b>“💰 Wallet-linked only”</b> to view the exact payout ranking.
          </div>

          <h3>Holder-balance cap: max 26× your BOBAI</h3>
          <p>Each individual payout to a winner is capped at <b>26× the BOBAI balance held in their linked wallet at the moment of payout</b>. Examples:</p>
          <table class="scoring-table">
            <tr><th>Your wallet holds</th><th>Maximum single payout</th></tr>
            <tr><td>$1 in BOBAI</td><td>$26</td></tr>
            <tr><td>$10 in BOBAI</td><td>$260</td></tr>
            <tr><td>$100 in BOBAI</td><td>$2,600</td></tr>
            <tr><td>$1,000 in BOBAI</td><td>$26,000</td></tr>
            <tr><td>$10,000 in BOBAI</td><td>$260,000</td></tr>
          </table>
          <div class="callout info">
            <b>Why this exists.</b> This is an <b>anti-abuse rule</b> that protects the people who actually believe in <b>BOBAI / Brain On BNB AI</b> — the holders, supporters, and long-term believers. Without a cap, anyone could spin up an empty wallet, place a random tip, and try to siphon a pool that was built by real supporters. The 26× rule ties payouts to actual skin in the game.
          </div>
          <p><b>Don't worry about being "capped out".</b> Nobody knows in advance how big the prize pool will be — but if the pool grows, your <b>BOBAI balance grows with it</b> (same token, same chart). So the cap scales with you. Holding more BOBAI literally raises your ceiling.</p>
          <p>The balance is read <b>live from the blockchain</b> at payout time — there's no snapshot to game and no way to fake it.</p>

          <div class="cap-h3-row">
            <h3>Cap overflow — cascading redistribution</h3>
            <button type="button" class="cap-modal-btn" onclick="openCapModal()" aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="cap-modal">
              <span class="ic">⚽</span> See a visual walk-through
            </button>
          </div>
          <p>If a winner's computed share exceeds their personal 26× cap, the excess does <b>not</b> stay with that winner and is <b>not</b> kept by the team. It flows to the rest of the eligible winners <b>in the same pot</b>. Each pot (Group, End Pool, and the three Crypto pots) is evaluated <b>independently</b> — overflow never crosses pot boundaries.</p>
          <ol>
            <li>Compute every winner's raw share based on points / closest-guess logic.</li>
            <li>Apply each winner's 26× cap. Sum up the overflow from anyone who got capped.</li>
            <li>Redistribute that overflow <b>proportionally</b> to the remaining (uncapped) winners <b>within the same pot</b>.</li>
            <li>If that redistribution pushes a new winner over their cap, repeat step 2 — the cascade continues until either every dollar is paid out within someone's cap, or no eligible uncapped winner remains.</li>
            <li>If after the full cascade <b>residual still remains</b> (every winner in that pot is capped out), the leftover BOBAI is added as <b>permanent liquidity</b> to the BOBAI/BNB PancakeSwap pool, and the resulting LP tokens are <b>burned to the dead address</b>. It is never carried into another pot, never refunded, never kept by the team — the value flows back to every BOBAI holder through a deeper, tighter market.</li>
          </ol>
          <p style="font-size:12.5px">The cascading logic is implemented in the same payout-computation code that the admin runs before signing. The full split (raw share, cap applied, redistribution rounds) will be published on-chain alongside each payout batch so anyone can verify it.</p>

          <h3>How the payouts run</h3>
          <p>Payout transactions are <b>triggered manually</b> by the project admin (not fully autonomous) — the code computes the exact split (including cap and cascade), then the admin signs and broadcasts the batch. This is a deliberate safety design: an off-by-one bug should never auto-drain the pool.</p>

          <!-- DONATIONS -->
          <h2 id="donations">8. Donations</h2>
          <p>The prize-pool wallet accepts <b>BNB</b> and <b>USDT (BEP-20)</b> on BNB Smart Chain. Address is shown on the <a href="./leaderboard.html" style="color:var(--gold)">Leaderboard</a> page with a copy button.</p>
          <div class="callout danger">
            <b>BSC only.</b> If you send USDT on Ethereum, Tron, Solana or any other network — funds are <b>lost</b>. There's no human in the loop to recover them.
          </div>
          <p>How it works:</p>
          <ol>
            <li>You send X BNB or USDT to the prize wallet</li>
            <li>The pool sync worker detects the new balance</li>
            <li>BNB/USDT is auto-swapped to BOBAI via PancakeSwap</li>
            <li>The new BOBAI is added to the pool (verifiable on BscScan)</li>
            <li>Pool display updates within ~5 minutes</li>
          </ol>
          <p>Any amount welcome. For gas efficiency we suggest at least the equivalent of ~$5, but anything works.</p>

          <!-- SECURITY -->
          <h2 id="security">9. Security &amp; manipulation safety</h2>
          <ul>
            <li><b>Tip lock</b>: 15 minutes before kickoff, enforced at the database level — not just in the UI.</li>
            <li><b>Bonus &amp; crypto lock</b>: enforced at the database level when the relevant match kicks off.</li>
            <li><b>Match results</b>: only the sync worker (using a service-role key, never exposed to users) can write results. Players cannot edit goals.</li>
            <li><b>Scoring</b>: runs in a database trigger after match results are written. The trigger overwrites any user-side `points` value, so nobody can self-award points.</li>
            <li><b>Wallet linking</b>: you just paste your BEP-20 address — no signing, no seed phrase, no private key. We never ask for any of that, and the app would never need it.</li>
            <li><b>Wallet privacy</b>: your linked wallet is <b>never shown to other players in-app</b>. The only public reveal is the on-chain payout transaction itself — and Group-Pot payouts are sent in <b>randomized order across the 12 groups</b> (e.g. group F may receive before group A), so observers cannot match a wallet to a specific group placement. Only the <b>End-Pool top 4</b> (🥇/🥈/🥉/4th overall) and the <b>three closest crypto guesses</b> (BTC/BNB/BOBAI) are matchable to a wallet, because those are unique single-winner positions that are already public on the leaderboard.</li>
            <li><b>Tie-breaker by BOBAI balance</b>: read live from the blockchain at payout time, not from a database — un-fakeable.</li>
            <li><b>Payouts</b>: manually triggered by admin, not autonomous. An admin error or bug can be caught before signing.</li>
            <li><b>Prize wallet</b>: a fresh dedicated wallet. Private key is only stored in encrypted Cloudflare Worker secrets — never in code, never on a server you can SSH into.</li>
          </ul>
          <div class="callout danger">
            <b>Admins NEVER DM you first.</b> Anyone offering you to "double your winnings", "verify your account", "claim your payout faster" via Telegram/X DM is a scammer. Block + report.
          </div>

          <!-- CONTACT -->

            <li>Website: <a href="https://brainonbnb.com" style="color:var(--gold)">brainonbnb.com</a></li>
          </ul>

          </p>

      <div class="cap-modal-body">
        <div class="cap-example">
          <p class="lead">
            Imagine the <b>Group Pot</b> has grown to <b>$24,000</b>. The top-1 sub-bracket holds <b>55% = $13,200</b>, split equally among the <b>12 group winners</b> → each one is entitled to <b>$1,100</b>. Below are <b>4 representative winners</b> with very different BOBAI balances.
          </p>

          <div class="cap-case">
            <div class="cap-case-title ok">Case A — Everyone holds enough BOBAI</div>
            <div class="cap-row">
              <div class="cap-card">
                <div class="h">Winner 1</div>
                <div class="line"><span>Holds</span><b>$500</b></div>
                <div class="line"><span>26× cap</span><b>$13,000</b></div>
                <div class="line pay"><span>Paid</span><b>$1,100 ✓</b></div>
              </div>
              <div class="cap-card">
                <div class="h">Winner 2</div>
                <div class="line"><span>Holds</span><b>$1,000</b></div>
                <div class="line"><span>26× cap</span><b>$26,000</b></div>
                <div class="line pay"><span>Paid</span><b>$1,100 ✓</b></div>
              </div>

    function openCapModal(){

      if (!m) return;
      m.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
      document.body.classList.add('cap-modal-open');
      // Focus the close button for keyboard users

      if (close) setTimeout(()=>close.focus(), 50);

      const m = document.getElementById('cap-modal');
      if (!m) return;
      m.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      document.body.classList.remove('cap-modal-open');
    }

-- Phase C migration — bonus questions finalized, RLS hardened for empty-match-table case
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor after the base schema.sql.

-- 1) wc_bonus: q5 → topscorer_country (typed properly), drop q6 placeholder
ALTER TABLE wc_bonus RENAME COLUMN q5 TO topscorer_country;
ALTER TABLE wc_bonus DROP COLUMN q6;

-- 2) Harden RLS time-locks so they don't reject inserts when wc_matches is empty
--    (during beta build, matches aren't seeded yet — without COALESCE, NULL > NOW()
--     returns NULL which fails the WITH CHECK clause and blocks legitimate inserts)

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_bonus_insert_own ON wc_bonus;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_bonus_update_own ON wc_bonus;
CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_insert_own ON wc_bonus FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND COALESCE((SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = 1), '9999-12-31'::timestamptz) > NOW()
);
CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_update_own ON wc_bonus FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND COALESCE((SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = 1), '9999-12-31'::timestamptz) > NOW()
);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto;
CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND COALESCE(
        (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE phase = 'final' ORDER BY kickoff_utc DESC LIMIT 1),
        '9999-12-31'::timestamptz
      ) > NOW()
);
CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND COALESCE(
        (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE phase = 'final' ORDER BY kickoff_utc DESC LIMIT 1),
        '9999-12-31'::timestamptz
      ) > NOW()
);

-- Tips RLS already covers this implicitly: tip insert requires a wc_matches row with kickoff > NOW(),
-- so empty wc_matches naturally blocks tipping until Phase D seeds the matches. Keep as-is.

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_e.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase E migration — Scoring engine (DB trigger, auto-resolves tips when match.played=true)
-- Manipulation-safe: scoring runs ONLY via this trigger, never by user input.
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor after Phase D.

-- ============================================================
-- Compute one tip's raw score (pre-multiplier)
-- 5 = exact result · 3 = correct winner + correct goal diff · 1 = correct winner only · 0 otherwise
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_score_tip(
  m_h INT, m_a INT, t_h INT, t_a INT
) RETURNS INT AS $$
BEGIN
  IF m_h IS NULL OR m_a IS NULL OR t_h IS NULL OR t_a IS NULL THEN
    RETURN 0;
  END IF;
  -- Exact
  IF t_h = m_h AND t_a = m_a THEN
    RETURN 5;
  END IF;
  -- Correct winner direction?
  IF (m_h > m_a AND t_h > t_a)
     OR (m_h < m_a AND t_h < t_a)
     OR (m_h = m_a AND t_h = t_a) THEN
    -- Correct goal difference too?
    IF (m_h - m_a) = (t_h - t_a) THEN
      RETURN 3;
    END IF;
    RETURN 1;
  END IF;
  RETURN 0;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- ============================================================
-- Trigger: when match transitions to played (or score corrected),
-- recompute points for every tip on that match.
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_resolve_tips() RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  -- Trigger fires when match is marked played WITH valid goals, OR when goals are corrected
  IF NEW.played
     AND NEW.goals_home IS NOT NULL
     AND NEW.goals_away IS NOT NULL
     AND (
       OLD.played       IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.played
       OR OLD.goals_home IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.goals_home
       OR OLD.goals_away IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.goals_away
     ) THEN
    UPDATE wc_tips t
    SET
      points       = wc_score_tip(NEW.goals_home, NEW.goals_away, t.tip_home, t.tip_away),
      points_final = wc_score_tip(NEW.goals_home, NEW.goals_away, t.tip_home, t.tip_away) * NEW.multiplier,
      resolved     = TRUE,
      updated_at   = NOW()
    WHERE t.match_id = NEW.id;
  END IF;
  -- Unresolve if a match is reset (played=true → false)
  IF OLD.played = TRUE AND NEW.played = FALSE THEN
    UPDATE wc_tips t
    SET points = 0, points_final = 0, resolved = FALSE, updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE t.match_id = NEW.id;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS wc_resolve_tips_trigger ON wc_matches;
CREATE TRIGGER wc_resolve_tips_trigger
AFTER UPDATE ON wc_matches
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION wc_resolve_tips();

-- ============================================================
-- Hard guard: users can NEVER update wc_matches (results/goals)
-- Only service-role (Cloudflare Worker) is allowed to write.
-- This is implicit (no INSERT/UPDATE policy → blocked under RLS), but
-- we add a NEGATIVE policy explicitly for clarity.
-- ============================================================
-- (no-op — wc_matches has only SELECT policy; INSERT/UPDATE require service role)

-- ============================================================
-- Helper: resolve bonus questions in one call (run after tournament ends)
-- Takes the actual answers and writes points to wc_bonus rows.
-- Each correct answer = 10 pts.  (Superseded by migration_phase_k_bonus_20 → 20 pts.)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_resolve_bonus(
  p_champion           TEXT,
  p_most_goals_team    TEXT,
  p_fewest_goals_team  TEXT,
  p_red_cards_bracket  TEXT,
  p_topscorer_country  TEXT
) RETURNS INT AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_updated INT;
BEGIN
  UPDATE wc_bonus b
  SET points = (
    (CASE WHEN b.champion          = p_champion          THEN 10 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.most_goals_team   = p_most_goals_team   THEN 10 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.fewest_goals_team = p_fewest_goals_team THEN 10 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.red_cards_bracket = p_red_cards_bracket THEN 10 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.topscorer_country = p_topscorer_country THEN 10 ELSE 0 END)
  ),
  resolved = TRUE,
  updated_at = NOW();
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_updated = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN rows_updated;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================================
-- Helper: resolve crypto predictions
-- Stores abs-diff to actual prices; lowest diff wins the pool slot (computed at payout time).
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_resolve_crypto(
  p_btc   NUMERIC,
  p_bnb   NUMERIC,
  p_bobai NUMERIC
) RETURNS INT AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_updated INT;
BEGIN
  UPDATE wc_crypto c
  SET btc_diff   = ABS(c.btc_price   - p_btc),
      bnb_diff   = ABS(c.bnb_price   - p_bnb),
      bobai_diff = ABS(c.bobai_price - p_bobai),
      resolved   = TRUE,
      updated_at = NOW();
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_updated = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN rows_updated;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_e_grants.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase E hotfix — grant service_role explicit privileges on all wc_* tables
-- Required because new sb_secret_* API keys don't get implicit table privileges
-- like the legacy service_role JWT did.

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO service_role;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO service_role;
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA public TO service_role;

-- Auto-grant to service_role for any new tables/sequences/functions we add later
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLES TO service_role;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCES TO service_role;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTIONS TO service_role;

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_g.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase G migration — leaderboard views (overall, per-group, per-crypto)
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor after previous migrations.

-- ============================================================
-- Per-group leaderboard: points from group-stage matches only
-- ============================================================
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard_group;
CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard_group AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL AND u.wallet_verified) AS has_wallet,
  m.group_letter,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)         AS group_points,
  COUNT(t.id)                               AS tips_count,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)     AS tips_resolved
FROM wc_users u
JOIN wc_tips t ON t.user_id = u.id
JOIN wc_matches m ON m.id = t.match_id AND m.phase = 'group'
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, u.wallet_verified, m.group_letter;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard_group SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard_group TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- ============================================================
-- Per-crypto leaderboard view (one per coin, sorted by diff)
-- Returns NULL diff at the bottom (= not yet resolved or didn't predict)
-- ============================================================
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard_crypto;
CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard_crypto AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL AND u.wallet_verified) AS has_wallet,
  c.btc_price,    c.btc_diff,
  c.bnb_price,    c.bnb_diff,
  c.bobai_price,  c.bobai_diff,
  c.resolved      AS crypto_resolved
FROM wc_users u
JOIN wc_crypto c ON c.user_id = u.id;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard_crypto SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard_crypto TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- ============================================================
-- Refresh existing wc_leaderboard to include avatar + crypto resolved info
-- (the original view exists from base schema; we replace it to include
-- the crypto-prize-pot positions so the overall page can show them)
-- ============================================================
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard;
CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL AND u.wallet_verified) AS has_wallet,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0) + COALESCE(b.points, 0) AS total_points,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)                          AS match_points,
  COALESCE(b.points, 0)                                     AS bonus_points,
  COUNT(t.id)                                               AS tips_count,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)                     AS tips_resolved
FROM wc_users u
LEFT JOIN wc_tips  t ON t.user_id = u.id
LEFT JOIN wc_bonus b ON b.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, u.wallet_verified, b.points;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_h_auth.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase H Auth Migration
-- Switch from synthetic-email auth (<username>@worldcup.bobai.app)
-- to real-email auth (user provides their own email; can log in via
-- username OR email; password reset via email).
--
-- Run AFTER reset_test_data.sql (since old synthetic-email users won't
-- have a real email and can't be migrated).

-- ============================================================
-- RPC: wc_username_to_email(p_username)
-- Resolves a username to the auth.users.email so client can call
-- signInWithPassword({ email, password }) when the user typed only
-- a username. SECURITY DEFINER bypasses RLS so anonymous callers
-- (pre-login) can use it.
--
-- Trade-off accepted: this exposes the email of any username someone
-- queries. Username enumeration is already possible via the "Username
-- already taken" sign-up error, so this is an acceptable widening for
-- the login UX. Supabase auth rate limiting still applies on the
-- subsequent password attempt.
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.wc_username_to_email(p_username TEXT)
RETURNS TEXT
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT au.email
  FROM wc_users wu
  JOIN auth.users au ON au.id = wu.auth_id
  WHERE wu.username_lc = lower(p_username)
  LIMIT 1;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.wc_username_to_email(TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.wc_username_to_email(TEXT) TO anon, authenticated;

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_h_crypto_lock.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase H follow-up: lock crypto predictions at tournament start
--
-- Previously crypto predictions stayed editable until the FINAL kickoff, which
-- meant they could not be revealed on public profiles until after the Final.
-- New rule: crypto predictions lock when the FIRST match kicks off (same lock
-- moment as bonus). They then become publicly visible during the tournament,
-- and the leaderboard can show who's currently closest to the live price.

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto;

CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = 1) > NOW()
);

CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = 1) > NOW()
);

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_h_grants.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase H hotfix — grant anon + authenticated explicit privileges on wc_* tables.
-- Required because new sb_publishable_*/sb_secret_* API keys don't get implicit
-- table privileges like the legacy JWT keys did. Without this, every INSERT
-- into wc_users (during signup) fails with "permission denied for table".
--
-- RLS policies remain the gatekeeper — these GRANTs only allow the role to
-- attempt the operation; the policy WITH CHECK still has to pass.

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO anon, authenticated;

-- All current tables: authenticated does full CRUD (RLS scopes to owner),
-- anon is read-only (for public leaderboard / pool / matches).
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO anon;

-- Sequences (BIGSERIAL columns need USAGE on the underlying sequence for INSERT)
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO anon, authenticated;

-- Functions (RPCs like wc_username_to_email)
GRANT EXECUTE ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA public TO anon, authenticated;

-- Auto-apply same grants to any tables/sequences/functions added later
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public
  GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLES TO authenticated;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public
  GRANT SELECT ON TABLES TO anon;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public
  GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCES TO anon, authenticated;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public
  GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTIONS TO anon, authenticated;

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_h_signup_trigger.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase H — auto-create wc_users profile when an auth.users row is created.
-- Replaces the client-side insert (which fails when Confirm Email is on,
-- because the new user has no session yet → anon role → permission denied).
--
-- Username uniqueness is enforced by the existing unique index on
-- wc_users.username_lc — if it collides, the trigger raises an exception,
-- which rolls back the auth.users INSERT too. Net effect: signUp fails
-- with "Database error saving new user", which the client maps to
-- "Username already taken".
--
-- Signups WITHOUT a `username` in raw_user_meta_data are skipped, so this
-- trigger is safe even if other apps share this Supabase project.

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.wc_handle_new_user()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_username TEXT;
  v_country  TEXT;
BEGIN
  v_username := NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'username';
  v_country  := NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'country';

  -- Only auto-create for users that signed up via the worldcup app
  -- (they always carry a username in user_metadata).
  IF v_username IS NULL OR v_username = '' THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.wc_users (auth_id, username, avatar_country)
  VALUES (NEW.id, v_username, NULLIF(v_country, ''));

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- Replace any prior version of the trigger
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS wc_on_auth_user_created ON auth.users;

CREATE TRIGGER wc_on_auth_user_created
  AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW
  EXECUTE FUNCTION public.wc_handle_new_user();

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_h_wallet_signature.sql

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase H: wallet signature columns
-- Adds storage for ECDSA signature + signed message + timestamp on wc_users.
-- The user produces this signature in their own wallet (MetaMask, Trust, hardware, etc.);
-- client-side ethers.verifyMessage prevents bad data, but the authoritative check
-- happens server-side via a Cloudflare Worker before any payout.

ALTER TABLE wc_users
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS wallet_signature TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS wallet_message   TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS wallet_signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

-- Re-grant updates on the new columns to authenticated role (RLS still gates per-row).
GRANT UPDATE (wallet, wallet_signature, wallet_message, wallet_signed_at, avatar_country)
  ON wc_users TO authenticated;

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_i_wallet_simple.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase I: simple wallet linking
-- Removes the ECDSA-signature flow. New rules:
--   1. User just types their BEP-20 address — no signing, no gas.
--   2. Each wallet can be linked to AT MOST ONE account (UNIQUE).
--   3. Once set, the wallet is IMMUTABLE — to switch, the account is sunk.
--      Hard lock + UI confirm replaces signature verification as anti-fraud.
-- Idempotent: works whether or not the never-applied signature migration H
-- was ever run.

-- ============================================================
-- 1) Drop dependent views first (they reference wallet_verified)
-- ============================================================
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard_crypto;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard_group;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard;

-- ============================================================
-- 2) Drop signature columns + wallet_verified (no-op if absent)
-- ============================================================
ALTER TABLE wc_users
  DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS wallet_signature,
  DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS wallet_message,
  DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS wallet_signed_at,
  DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS wallet_verified;

-- ============================================================
-- 3) Wipe stale wallet rows (clean slate before adding UNIQUE)
-- ============================================================
UPDATE wc_users
   SET wallet = NULL
 WHERE wallet IS NOT NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- 4) UNIQUE wallet (case-insensitive, only when set)
--    Replaces the old non-unique partial index from schema.sql.
-- ============================================================
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS wc_users_wallet;
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS wc_users_wallet_unique;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX wc_users_wallet_unique
  ON wc_users (lower(wallet))
  WHERE wallet IS NOT NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- 5) Immutability: wallet may go NULL → set, never change after that
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_users_immutable_check() RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  -- Service role (Cloudflare Worker / admin SQL) bypasses these checks so we
  -- can still correct mistakes manually if a user reaches out.
  IF coalesce(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true)::json->>'role','') = 'service_role' THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;
  IF NEW.auth_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.auth_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'auth_id is immutable';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.username IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.username THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'username is immutable';
  END IF;
  -- Wallet: NULL → address is the ONLY allowed transition for a normal user.
  -- Changing an already-set wallet, or clearing it, requires service_role.
  IF OLD.wallet IS NOT NULL AND NEW.wallet IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.wallet THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'wallet is permanent once linked — contact support to correct it';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Trigger already exists from base schema; CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION above
-- is enough — no need to drop/recreate the trigger.

-- ============================================================
-- 6) Tighten column grant to the new minimal write set
--    REVOKE list only includes the column that definitely exists today.
--    Past grants on dropped columns vanished with the columns themselves.
-- ============================================================
REVOKE UPDATE (wallet, avatar_country) ON wc_users FROM authenticated;
GRANT  UPDATE (wallet, avatar_country) ON wc_users TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================
-- 7) Recreate leaderboard views without wallet_verified
--    has_wallet now == (wallet IS NOT NULL)
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL) AS has_wallet,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0) + COALESCE(b.points, 0) AS total_points,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)                          AS match_points,
  COALESCE(b.points, 0)                                     AS bonus_points,
  COUNT(t.id)                                               AS tips_count,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)                     AS tips_resolved
FROM wc_users u
LEFT JOIN wc_tips  t ON t.user_id = u.id
LEFT JOIN wc_bonus b ON b.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, b.points;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard_group AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL) AS has_wallet,
  m.group_letter,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)         AS group_points,
  COUNT(t.id)                               AS tips_count,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)     AS tips_resolved
FROM wc_users u
JOIN wc_tips t ON t.user_id = u.id
JOIN wc_matches m ON m.id = t.match_id AND m.phase = 'group'
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, m.group_letter;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard_group SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard_group TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard_crypto AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL) AS has_wallet,
  c.btc_price,    c.btc_diff,
  c.bnb_price,    c.bnb_diff,
  c.bobai_price,  c.bobai_diff,
  c.resolved      AS crypto_resolved
FROM wc_users u
JOIN wc_crypto c ON c.user_id = u.id;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard_crypto SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard_crypto TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_j_scoring_rebalance.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase J — Scoring rebalance (17 May 2026)
--
-- Two changes that the rules + UI already advertise:
--   1) Raw points for a winner-only tip: 1 → 2.   New ladder: 0 · 2 · 3 · 5.
--   2) Phase multipliers expanded from x1..x4 to x1..x7 so the late KO
--      matches truly swing the leaderboard.
--
-- Idempotent: safe to re-run.  Re-resolves any already-played matches under
-- the new scheme so historical points stay consistent (no-op before kickoff,
-- but correct if this is ever re-run mid-tournament).
--
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor after Phase I.

BEGIN;

-- ============================================================
-- 1) Updated raw score function: 5 / 3 / 2 / 0
--    (was: 5 / 3 / 1 / 0)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_score_tip(
  m_h INT, m_a INT, t_h INT, t_a INT
) RETURNS INT AS $$
BEGIN
  IF m_h IS NULL OR m_a IS NULL OR t_h IS NULL OR t_a IS NULL THEN
    RETURN 0;
  END IF;
  -- Exact result
  IF t_h = m_h AND t_a = m_a THEN
    RETURN 5;
  END IF;
  -- Correct winner direction?
  IF (m_h > m_a AND t_h > t_a)
     OR (m_h < m_a AND t_h < t_a)
     OR (m_h = m_a AND t_h = t_a) THEN
    -- Correct goal difference on top of correct winner?
    IF (m_h - m_a) = (t_h - t_a) THEN
      RETURN 3;
    END IF;
    RETURN 2;  -- was 1
  END IF;
  RETURN 0;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- ============================================================
-- 2) Phase multipliers
--    Was:  group=1, r32/r16=2, qf/sf=3, 3rd/final=4
--    Now:  group=1, r32=2, r16=3, qf=4, sf=5, 3rd=6, final=7
-- ============================================================
UPDATE wc_matches SET multiplier =
  CASE phase
    WHEN 'group' THEN 1
    WHEN 'r32'   THEN 2
    WHEN 'r16'   THEN 3
    WHEN 'qf'    THEN 4
    WHEN 'sf'    THEN 5
    WHEN '3rd'   THEN 6
    WHEN 'final' THEN 7
    ELSE multiplier
  END
WHERE phase IN ('group','r32','r16','qf','sf','3rd','final');

-- ============================================================
-- 3) Retro-resolve any already-played matches under the new scheme
--    so points + points_final stay in sync.  Pre-kickoff this hits
--    zero rows; mid-tournament it brings history up to date.
-- ============================================================
UPDATE wc_tips t
SET
  points       = wc_score_tip(m.goals_home, m.goals_away, t.tip_home, t.tip_away),
  points_final = wc_score_tip(m.goals_home, m.goals_away, t.tip_home, t.tip_away) * m.multiplier,
  updated_at   = NOW()
FROM wc_matches m
WHERE t.match_id = m.id
  AND m.played = TRUE
  AND m.goals_home IS NOT NULL
  AND m.goals_away IS NOT NULL;

COMMIT;

-- Verification queries (run manually if you want to double-check):
--   SELECT phase, multiplier, COUNT(*) FROM wc_matches GROUP BY phase, multiplier ORDER BY multiplier;
--   SELECT wc_score_tip(3,2,2,1);  -- expects 2 (correct winner only)
--   SELECT wc_score_tip(3,2,2,1);  -- ↑
--   SELECT wc_score_tip(3,2,3,2);  -- expects 5 (exact)
--   SELECT wc_score_tip(3,1,2,0);  -- expects 3 (winner + goal diff)
--   SELECT wc_score_tip(1,0,0,1);  -- expects 0 (wrong winner)

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_k_bonus_20.sql
==============================================================================

-- ============================================================
-- Phase K: bump bonus question points 10 → 20
-- Each correct bonus answer now scores 20 (was 10).
-- Idempotent: CREATE OR REPLACE — safe to re-run.
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_resolve_bonus(
  p_champion           TEXT,
  p_most_goals_team    TEXT,
  p_fewest_goals_team  TEXT,
  p_red_cards_bracket  TEXT,
  p_topscorer_country  TEXT
) RETURNS INT AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_updated INT;
BEGIN
  UPDATE wc_bonus b
  SET points = (
    (CASE WHEN b.champion          = p_champion          THEN 20 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.most_goals_team   = p_most_goals_team   THEN 20 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.fewest_goals_team = p_fewest_goals_team THEN 20 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.red_cards_bracket = p_red_cards_bracket THEN 20 ELSE 0 END) +
    (CASE WHEN b.topscorer_country = p_topscorer_country THEN 20 ELSE 0 END)
  ),
  resolved = TRUE,
  updated_at = NOW();
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_updated = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN rows_updated;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
