=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_l_md2_reopen.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase L: one-time reopen of Bonus + Crypto submissions
--
-- Original rule: bonus + crypto predictions locked at the first-match kickoff
-- (wc_matches id=1, 2026-06-11 19:00 UTC).
--
-- After MD1 we noticed late joiners had zero chance to play. Fair extension:
-- reopen Bonus + Crypto editing for everyone (existing + new users) and lock
-- finally 15 min before the first MD2 match (CZ–ZA, 2026-06-18 16:00 UTC),
-- i.e. 2026-06-18 15:45 UTC.
--
-- This is a ONE-TIME extension — the new lock is a hard-coded timestamp so it
-- isn't coupled to match IDs (cleaner; no risk of moving again if matches get
-- reshuffled).
--
-- Crypto: the scoring snapshot is still taken at the Final kickoff (19 Jul 2026
-- 19:00 UTC) — only the edit deadline moves, no re-snapshot needed.

-- BONUS
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_bonus_insert_own ON wc_bonus;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_bonus_update_own ON wc_bonus;

CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_insert_own ON wc_bonus FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND NOW() < '2026-06-18 15:45+00'::timestamptz
);

CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_update_own ON wc_bonus FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND NOW() < '2026-06-18 15:45+00'::timestamptz
);

-- CRYPTO
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto;

CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND NOW() < '2026-06-18 15:45+00'::timestamptz
);

CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND NOW() < '2026-06-18 15:45+00'::timestamptz
);

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_m_md_staircase.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase M: matchday staircase in the group phase
--
-- Why: with a flat ×1 over the 72 group matches, many players land on the
-- same total at the end of MD3 → too many ties per payout rank. Staggering
-- MD2 and MD3 produces more distinct totals while keeping the relative
-- value of each match close to the original (no late-round shock):
--
--   MD1  ×1     (matches 1–2 in every group)
--   MD2  ×1.25  (matches 3–4)
--   MD3  ×1.5   (matches 5–6)
--   KO   ×2 → ×7  (unchanged, see migration_phase_j)
--
-- Side-effect: points_final + multiplier are no longer integers
-- (e.g. exact-score in an MD3 match = 5 × 1.5 = 7.5), so both columns
-- are widened to NUMERIC. Sums / ordering on the leaderboard view keep
-- working unchanged.
--
-- Idempotent: safe to re-run. Re-resolves any already-played matches
-- under the new scheme so historical points stay in sync (no-op before
-- MD2 kickoff, but correct if this is ever re-run mid-tournament).
--
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor after Phase L.

BEGIN;

-- ============================================================
-- 1) Drop leaderboard views that depend on points_final
--    (Postgres blocks ALTER COLUMN TYPE on columns used by views.)
--    Views are recreated identically at the end of this migration.
-- ============================================================
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS wc_leaderboard_group;

-- ============================================================
-- 2) Widen multiplier + points_final from INT to NUMERIC
-- ============================================================
ALTER TABLE wc_matches
  ALTER COLUMN multiplier TYPE NUMERIC USING multiplier::NUMERIC;
ALTER TABLE wc_matches
  ALTER COLUMN multiplier SET DEFAULT 1;

ALTER TABLE wc_tips
  ALTER COLUMN points_final TYPE NUMERIC USING points_final::NUMERIC;
ALTER TABLE wc_tips
  ALTER COLUMN points_final SET DEFAULT 0;

-- ============================================================
-- 3) Group-phase staircase
--    Within each group, matches are ordered by kickoff time + id;
--    positions 1–2 = MD1, 3–4 = MD2, 5–6 = MD3.
--    KO phases are untouched (WHERE phase = 'group').
-- ============================================================
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT
    id,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
      PARTITION BY group_letter
      ORDER BY kickoff_utc, id
    ) AS pos
  FROM wc_matches
  WHERE phase = 'group'
)
UPDATE wc_matches m
SET multiplier = CASE
  WHEN r.pos <= 2 THEN 1
  WHEN r.pos <= 4 THEN 1.25
  ELSE                 1.5
END
FROM ranked r
WHERE m.id = r.id;

-- ============================================================
-- 4) Retro-resolve any already-played matches under the new scheme
--    so points_final stays in sync with the new multipliers.
--    Pre-MD2 this hits zero MD2/MD3 rows; safe.
-- ============================================================
UPDATE wc_tips t
SET
  points_final = wc_score_tip(m.goals_home, m.goals_away, t.tip_home, t.tip_away) * m.multiplier,
  updated_at   = NOW()
FROM wc_matches m
WHERE t.match_id = m.id
  AND m.played = TRUE
  AND m.goals_home IS NOT NULL
  AND m.goals_away IS NOT NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- 5) Recreate leaderboard views (identical to migration_phase_i)
--    Now reading the widened NUMERIC points_final column.
-- ============================================================
CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL) AS has_wallet,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0) + COALESCE(b.points, 0) AS total_points,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)                          AS match_points,
  COALESCE(b.points, 0)                                     AS bonus_points,
  COUNT(t.id)                                               AS tips_count,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)                     AS tips_resolved
FROM wc_users u
LEFT JOIN wc_tips  t ON t.user_id = u.id
LEFT JOIN wc_bonus b ON b.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, b.points;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

CREATE VIEW wc_leaderboard_group AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL) AS has_wallet,
  m.group_letter,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)         AS group_points,
  COUNT(t.id)                               AS tips_count,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)     AS tips_resolved
FROM wc_users u
JOIN wc_tips t ON t.user_id = u.id
JOIN wc_matches m ON m.id = t.match_id AND m.phase = 'group'
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, m.group_letter;

ALTER VIEW wc_leaderboard_group SET (security_invoker = on);
GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard_group TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

COMMIT;

-- Verification (run manually):
--   SELECT group_letter, multiplier, COUNT(*)
--   FROM wc_matches WHERE phase='group'
--   GROUP BY group_letter, multiplier ORDER BY group_letter, multiplier;
--   -- expect 2 rows at 1, 2 at 1.25, 2 at 1.5 per group
--
--   SELECT phase, multiplier, COUNT(*)
--   FROM wc_matches GROUP BY phase, multiplier ORDER BY multiplier;
--   -- group: 24 @ 1, 24 @ 1.25, 24 @ 1.5
--   -- r32 24 @ 2 → final 1 @ 7 (unchanged)

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_n_bonus_live.sql

-- ============================================================
-- Phase N: Live bonus-resolution data
--
-- Adds two tables driven by the football-data.org sync worker so the
-- leaderboard "Bonus" view can render live winners without manual entry:
--
--   wc_scorers           — top scorers (for Golden Boot question)
--   wc_tournament_stats  — single-row counter for red cards (Q5 bracket)
--
-- Both are read-only for the public; writes go through the service-role
-- worker. Idempotent.
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wc_scorers (
  rank          INT  PRIMARY KEY,
  player_name   TEXT NOT NULL,
  country_code  TEXT,                       -- ISO code; null if unmappable
  goals         INT  NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  assists       INT,
  updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wc_tournament_stats (
  id              INT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT 1 CHECK (id = 1),
  red_cards_total INT DEFAULT 0,
  updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
INSERT INTO wc_tournament_stats (id) VALUES (1) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

-- Per-match red-card counter so the worker can hit /matches/{id} once per
-- finished match (saving the count) and aggregate totals from cached values.
-- NULL = not yet fetched, integer = swept and counted.
ALTER TABLE wc_matches ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS red_cards INT;

ALTER TABLE wc_scorers          ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_tournament_stats ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_scorers_read           ON wc_scorers;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS wc_tournament_stats_read  ON wc_tournament_stats;
CREATE POLICY wc_scorers_read          ON wc_scorers          FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY wc_tournament_stats_read ON wc_tournament_stats FOR SELECT USING (true);

GRANT SELECT ON wc_scorers, wc_tournament_stats TO anon, authenticated;

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_o_bobai_snapshot.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Phase O: BOBAI balance snapshot for tie-breaker.
-- Adds a periodically-refreshed on-chain BOBAI balance to wc_users so the
-- leaderboard can apply the published tie-breaker rule (rules.html §7):
--   1. group_points DESC                  (the score itself)
--   2. on-chain BOBAI balance DESC        (skin-in-the-game tie-breaker)
--   3. has_wallet DESC                    (linked > non-linked)
--   4. user_id ASC                        (deterministic last-resort fallback)
--
-- PRIVACY: bobai_balance MUST NEVER be exposed to anon/authenticated. It's
-- consumed strictly inside SECURITY DEFINER RPCs which return only the
-- pre-computed `rank` + `tied_above`/`tied_below` flags (so the UI can render
-- a "holds more $BOBAI" badge without ever revealing the actual amounts).
--
-- Also used at payout time (Phase H) for the 26× holder cap and the audit PDF
-- — but the payout calculator reads a FRESH on-chain balance at lock time;
-- this column is for live display only.

BEGIN;

-- ============================================================
-- 1) New columns on wc_users
-- ============================================================
ALTER TABLE wc_users
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS bobai_balance     NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS bobai_snapshot_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

-- ============================================================
-- 2) Privacy lockdown — anon/authenticated must NOT see balances
--    (existing RLS policy `wc_users_read FOR SELECT USING (true)` would
--     otherwise expose any column the role has GRANT on)
-- ============================================================
REVOKE SELECT (bobai_balance, bobai_snapshot_at) ON wc_users FROM anon, authenticated;

-- ============================================================
-- 3) Group leaderboard with tie-breaker resolved server-side
--    Returns same shape as wc_leaderboard_group + rank + tied flags.
--    bobai_balance is consumed inside this function but NEVER returned.
-- ============================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS wc_group_leaderboard_ranked(text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_group_leaderboard_ranked(p_letter text)
RETURNS TABLE (
  user_id            bigint,
  username           text,
  avatar_country     text,
  has_wallet         boolean,
  group_letter       text,
  group_points       numeric,
  tips_count         bigint,
  tips_resolved      bigint,
  rank               int,
  tied_above         boolean,
  tied_below         boolean,
  bobai_beats_below  boolean,   -- this row beat the next-ranked row on BOBAI
  bobai_loses_above  boolean    -- this row lost to the prior-ranked row on BOBAI
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  WITH base AS (
    SELECT
      u.id                                                       AS user_id,
      u.username,
      u.avatar_country,
      (u.wallet IS NOT NULL)                                     AS has_wallet,
      m.group_letter::text                                       AS group_letter,
      COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)::numeric                  AS group_points,
      COUNT(t.id)::bigint                                        AS tips_count,
      COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)::bigint              AS tips_resolved,
      COALESCE(u.bobai_balance, 0)::numeric                      AS bobai_balance
    FROM wc_users u
    JOIN wc_tips    t ON t.user_id = u.id
    JOIN wc_matches m ON m.id = t.match_id AND m.phase = 'group'
    WHERE m.group_letter = p_letter
    GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, m.group_letter, u.bobai_balance
  ),
  ordered AS (
    SELECT
      *,
      ROW_NUMBER() OVER w AS rk,
      LAG(group_points)   OVER w AS prev_pts,
      LEAD(group_points)  OVER w AS next_pts,
      LAG(bobai_balance)  OVER w AS prev_bobai,
      LEAD(bobai_balance) OVER w AS next_bobai
    FROM base
    WINDOW w AS (ORDER BY group_points DESC, bobai_balance DESC, has_wallet DESC, user_id ASC)
  )
  SELECT
    user_id, username, avatar_country, has_wallet, group_letter,
    group_points, tips_count, tips_resolved,
    rk::int AS rank,
    (prev_pts IS NOT NULL AND prev_pts = group_points) AS tied_above,
    (next_pts IS NOT NULL AND next_pts = group_points) AS tied_below,
    (next_pts IS NOT NULL AND next_pts = group_points AND bobai_balance > next_bobai) AS bobai_beats_below,
    (prev_pts IS NOT NULL AND prev_pts = group_points AND bobai_balance < prev_bobai) AS bobai_loses_above
  FROM ordered
  ORDER BY rk;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION wc_group_leaderboard_ranked(text) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION wc_group_leaderboard_ranked(text) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- ============================================================
-- 4) Overall leaderboard with tie-breaker resolved server-side
--    Mirrors wc_leaderboard shape + rank + tied flags.
-- ============================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS wc_overall_leaderboard_ranked();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_overall_leaderboard_ranked()
RETURNS TABLE (
  user_id            bigint,
  username           text,
  avatar_country     text,
  has_wallet         boolean,
  total_points       numeric,
  match_points       numeric,
  bonus_points       int,
  tips_count         bigint,
  tips_resolved      bigint,
  rank               int,
  tied_above         boolean,
  tied_below         boolean,
  bobai_beats_below  boolean,
  bobai_loses_above  boolean
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  WITH base AS (
    SELECT
      u.id                                                                       AS user_id,
      u.username,
      u.avatar_country,
      (u.wallet IS NOT NULL)                                                     AS has_wallet,
      (COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0) + COALESCE(b.points, 0))::numeric        AS total_points,
      COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0)::numeric                                  AS match_points,
      COALESCE(b.points, 0)::int                                                 AS bonus_points,
      COUNT(t.id)::bigint                                                        AS tips_count,
      COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved)::bigint                              AS tips_resolved,
      COALESCE(u.bobai_balance, 0)::numeric                                      AS bobai_balance
    FROM wc_users u
    LEFT JOIN wc_tips  t ON t.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN wc_bonus b ON b.user_id = u.id
    GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, b.points, u.bobai_balance
  ),
  ordered AS (
    SELECT
      *,
      ROW_NUMBER() OVER w AS rk,
      LAG(total_points)   OVER w AS prev_pts,
      LEAD(total_points)  OVER w AS next_pts,
      LAG(bobai_balance)  OVER w AS prev_bobai,
      LEAD(bobai_balance) OVER w AS next_bobai
    FROM base
    WINDOW w AS (ORDER BY total_points DESC, bobai_balance DESC, has_wallet DESC, user_id ASC)
  )
  SELECT
    user_id, username, avatar_country, has_wallet,
    total_points, match_points, bonus_points,
    tips_count, tips_resolved,
    rk::int AS rank,
    (prev_pts IS NOT NULL AND prev_pts = total_points) AS tied_above,
    (next_pts IS NOT NULL AND next_pts = total_points) AS tied_below,
    (next_pts IS NOT NULL AND next_pts = total_points AND bobai_balance > next_bobai) AS bobai_beats_below,
    (prev_pts IS NOT NULL AND prev_pts = total_points AND bobai_balance < prev_bobai) AS bobai_loses_above
  FROM ordered
  ORDER BY rk;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION wc_overall_leaderboard_ranked()  FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION wc_overall_leaderboard_ranked() TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- ============================================================
-- 5) Bulk-update RPC for the hourly cron snapshot
--    Worker posts a JSON array of {id, balance} and updates in one round-trip
--    (keeps Cloudflare Workers free-tier subrequest count down — single REST
--    call instead of N PATCHes).
-- ============================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS wc_update_bobai_snapshots(jsonb);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_update_bobai_snapshots(payload jsonb)
RETURNS int
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_updated int;
BEGIN
  WITH src AS (
    SELECT
      (e->>'id')::bigint       AS id,
      (e->>'balance')::numeric AS balance
    FROM jsonb_array_elements(payload) e
  )
  UPDATE wc_users u
     SET bobai_balance     = src.balance,
         bobai_snapshot_at = NOW()
    FROM src
   WHERE u.id = src.id;
  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_updated = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN v_updated;
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION wc_update_bobai_snapshots(jsonb) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION wc_update_bobai_snapshots(jsonb) TO service_role;

COMMIT;

-- Verification (run manually):
--   SELECT column_name, data_type FROM information_schema.columns
--   WHERE table_name='wc_users' AND column_name IN ('bobai_balance','bobai_snapshot_at');
--
--   -- Anon should NOT be able to read bobai_balance directly:
--   SET ROLE anon;
--   SELECT bobai_balance FROM wc_users LIMIT 1;   -- expect: permission denied
--   RESET ROLE;
--
--   -- RPC should return ranked rows with tied flags but no bobai column:
--   SELECT * FROM wc_group_leaderboard_ranked('A') LIMIT 5;
--   SELECT * FROM wc_overall_leaderboard_ranked() LIMIT 5;

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_p_group_payouts.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase P: Group pot payout tracking
--
-- 1. wc_payouts: per-transfer row written by the payout CLI script
--    (one row per winner per pot). End-pool/Crypto payouts use the same table.
-- 2. wc_pool.group_paid_at: single timestamp that gates the "paid out" UI
--    state across the worldcup app. Set by /admin/mark-group-paid after the
--    payout script finishes successfully.

-- =====================================================================
-- 1) wc_payouts — receipt rows
-- =====================================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wc_payouts (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  pot             TEXT        NOT NULL CHECK (pot IN ('group', 'end', 'crypto')),
  group_letter    TEXT        CHECK (group_letter IS NULL OR group_letter ~ '^[A-L]$'),
  position        TEXT        NOT NULL,        -- '1st' | '2nd' | 'best_3rd' | 'overall_1st' | 'overall_2nd' | 'overall_3rd' | 'overall_4th' | 'btc' | 'bnb' | 'bobai'
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES wc_users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  username        TEXT        NOT NULL,        -- snapshot at payout time
  country_code    TEXT,                        -- snapshot at payout time
  wallet          TEXT        NOT NULL,        -- BEP-20 address (lowercased)
  bobai_amount    NUMERIC(38, 8) NOT NULL,
  usd_at_payout   NUMERIC(14, 2),
  tx_hash         TEXT,                        -- 0x… BscScan tx
  paid_at         TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  notes           TEXT                         -- e.g. "26x cap applied", "redistributed share"
);

-- Common read patterns:
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS wc_payouts_pot_idx          ON wc_payouts (pot);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS wc_payouts_group_letter_idx ON wc_payouts (group_letter) WHERE group_letter IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS wc_payouts_user_idx         ON wc_payouts (user_id)      WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS wc_payouts_paid_at_idx      ON wc_payouts (paid_at DESC);

-- Public read access (TX hashes + amounts are on-chain anyway, no PII beyond
-- wallet which is also on-chain). Insert is service-role only.
ALTER TABLE wc_payouts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE POLICY "public read wc_payouts" ON wc_payouts FOR SELECT USING (true);
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- 2) wc_pool.group_paid_at — the global "paid" gate
-- =====================================================================
ALTER TABLE wc_pool
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS group_paid_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/migration_phase_q_penalties.sql
==============================================================================

-- Phase Q — Knockout: extra time & penalty shootouts
-- ====================================================
-- KO matches can be decided after 90 min (extra time or a penalty shootout).
-- The tip game scores the PLAYED-90-MIN result only: a 1:1 that goes to pens
-- still scores everyone's draw-tips. Extra time / penalties only decide who
-- advances and feed the bonus questions (champion, etc.) — they NEVER change
-- the match-tip points.
--
-- Data model:
--   goals_home / goals_away  = 90-minute (regular) result  -> tip scoring
--   final_home / final_away  = full result incl. ET + pens -> winner & display
--                              (NULL when the match was decided inside 90 min)
--   decided_by               = 'aet' | 'pens' | NULL        -> display label
--
-- Winner everywhere (bracket advance / elimination / champion / bonus) =
--   direction of final_* when present, else direction of goals_*.
-- Penalty tally shown in the UI = final_* - goals_* (e.g. 4:5 - 1:1 = 3:4).
--
-- The scoring trigger (wc_resolve_tips / wc_score_tip) is intentionally NOT
-- touched — it keeps scoring on goals_home/goals_away.

ALTER TABLE wc_matches ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS final_home INT;
ALTER TABLE wc_matches ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS final_away INT;
ALTER TABLE wc_matches ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS decided_by TEXT
  CHECK (decided_by IN ('aet','pens'));

-- Backfill of the two already-played R32 shootouts (DE-PY id 73, NL-MA id 87)
-- is done out-of-band via service-role PATCH so the wc_resolve_tips trigger
-- re-scores their tips from the corrected 90-min goals. See deploy notes.

==============================================================================
=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/reset_test_data.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Reset all user/test data
-- Run BEFORE public registration opens (04.06) if you want a clean slate.
-- Keeps wc_matches + wc_pool (reference data); wipes all user-created rows.
--
-- ⚠ DESTRUCTIVE: deletes ALL users including yours.
--   You'll need to re-register after running this.

BEGIN;

-- 1) Drop donation log (no FK to wc_users)
DELETE FROM wc_donations;

-- 2) Delete every Supabase auth user that has a wc_users profile.
--    FK chain: auth.users → wc_users (CASCADE) → wc_tips/wc_bonus/wc_crypto (CASCADE)
--    so this one DELETE wipes everything user-related in one shot.
DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id IN (SELECT auth_id FROM wc_users);

-- Safety net for the historical synthetic-email accounts (pre-Phase-H).
-- These had no matching wc_users row in some failed-registration cases.
DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE email LIKE '%@worldcup.bobai.app';

-- Belt-and-suspenders: if any orphan game rows survived the cascade, kill them.
DELETE FROM wc_tips;
DELETE FROM wc_bonus;
DELETE FROM wc_crypto;
DELETE FROM wc_users;

-- 3) Reset pool counters (kept for now; will start filling from kickoff)
UPDATE wc_pool SET
  total_bobai = 0,
  group_pot   = 0,
  endpool     = 0,
  crypto_pot  = 0,
  updated_at  = NOW()
WHERE id = 1;

COMMIT;

=== FILE: dashboard/worldcup/app/sql/schema.sql
==============================================================================

-- BOBAI Worldcup '26 — Beta Schema
-- Run in Supabase SQL Editor (same project as buildonbnbgame)
-- All tables prefixed wc_ to keep app data isolated

-- ============================================================
-- 1) PROFILES (linked to auth.users, username-based)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_users (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  auth_id UUID UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(username) BETWEEN 3 AND 20),
  username_lc TEXT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (lower(username)) STORED,
  wallet TEXT,                        -- lowercase BEP-20 address, NULL if not connected
  wallet_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  avatar_country TEXT,                -- ISO country code (e.g. 'BR','FR','USA')
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX wc_users_username_lc ON wc_users (username_lc);
CREATE INDEX wc_users_wallet ON wc_users (wallet) WHERE wallet IS NOT NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- 2) MATCHES (static reference data, 104 entries: 72 group + 32 KO)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_matches (
  id INT PRIMARY KEY,                 -- 1-72 group, 73-104 KO
  phase TEXT NOT NULL,                -- 'group','r32','r16','qf','sf','3rd','final'
  group_letter CHAR(1),               -- 'A'-'L' for group, NULL for KO
  team_home TEXT,                     -- ISO or 'TBD'
  team_away TEXT,
  kickoff_utc TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  goals_home INT,                     -- NULL until played; 90-min (regular) result -> tip scoring
  goals_away INT,
  final_home INT,                     -- KO full result incl ET+pens; NULL if decided in 90 (phase_q)
  final_away INT,
  decided_by TEXT,                    -- 'aet' | 'pens' | NULL — how a KO match was decided (phase_q)
  played BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  multiplier NUMERIC DEFAULT 1        -- group MD1=1, MD2=1.25, MD3=1.5; r32=2, r16=3, qf=4, sf=5, 3rd=6, final=7 (rebalanced phase_j, MD staircase phase_m)
);
CREATE INDEX wc_matches_kickoff ON wc_matches (kickoff_utc);
CREATE INDEX wc_matches_phase ON wc_matches (phase);

-- ============================================================
-- 3) MATCH TIPS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_tips (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT REFERENCES wc_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  match_id INT REFERENCES wc_matches(id),
  tip_home INT NOT NULL CHECK (tip_home >= 0 AND tip_home <= 30),
  tip_away INT NOT NULL CHECK (tip_away >= 0 AND tip_away <= 30),
  points INT DEFAULT 0,               -- computed after match (raw, pre-multiplier; 0/2/3/5)
  points_final NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,     -- post-multiplier (can be fractional, e.g. 5 × 1.5 = 7.5)
  resolved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, match_id)
);
CREATE INDEX wc_tips_user ON wc_tips (user_id);
CREATE INDEX wc_tips_match ON wc_tips (match_id);

-- ============================================================
-- 4) BONUS ANSWERS (pre-WC, 20pts each)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_bonus (
  user_id BIGINT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES wc_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  champion TEXT,                      -- predicted winner (ISO code)
  most_goals_team TEXT,
  fewest_goals_team TEXT,
  red_cards_bracket TEXT,             -- '<5','5-9','10-14','15-19','>19'
  q5 TEXT,                            -- placeholder
  q6 TEXT,                            -- placeholder
  points INT DEFAULT 0,
  resolved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- 5) CRYPTO PREDICTIONS (at final kickoff)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_crypto (
  user_id BIGINT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES wc_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  btc_price NUMERIC(18,2),
  bnb_price NUMERIC(18,4),
  bobai_price NUMERIC(20,10),
  btc_diff NUMERIC,                   -- abs diff to actual, set at final-kickoff
  bnb_diff NUMERIC,
  bobai_diff NUMERIC,
  resolved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- ============================================================
-- 6) POOL STATE (single row)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_pool (
  id INT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT 1,
  total_bobai NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  group_pot NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,        -- 60% during group phase
  endpool NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,          -- 30% group / 90% KO
  crypto_pot NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,       -- 10% always
  bobai_price_usd NUMERIC,            -- cached for display
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  CHECK (id = 1)                      -- enforce single row
);
INSERT INTO wc_pool (id) VALUES (1) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

-- ============================================================
-- 7) DONATIONS LOG
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE wc_donations (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  from_address TEXT NOT NULL,
  token TEXT NOT NULL,                -- 'BNB','USDT','TAX'
  amount_in NUMERIC NOT NULL,
  amount_bobai NUMERIC,
  tx_hash TEXT UNIQUE,
  swap_tx_hash TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX wc_donations_from ON wc_donations (from_address);
CREATE INDEX wc_donations_created ON wc_donations (created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY
-- ============================================================
ALTER TABLE wc_users     ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_matches   ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_tips      ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_bonus     ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_crypto    ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_pool      ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE wc_donations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- USERS: public can read leaderboard fields, only owner writes own row
CREATE POLICY wc_users_read       ON wc_users FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY wc_users_insert_own ON wc_users FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = auth_id);
CREATE POLICY wc_users_update_own ON wc_users FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = auth_id);

-- IMMUTABILITY: username + auth_id can NEVER change after creation
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wc_users_immutable_check() RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  -- Service role (Cloudflare Worker) bypasses all immutability checks
  IF coalesce(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true)::json->>'role','') = 'service_role' THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;
  IF NEW.auth_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.auth_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'auth_id is immutable';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.username IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.username THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'username is immutable';
  END IF;
  -- Users cannot self-verify their wallet; flip happens via service-role flow
  IF NEW.wallet_verified IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.wallet_verified AND OLD.wallet_verified = FALSE THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'wallet_verified can only be set via signature verification flow';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER wc_users_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON wc_users
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION wc_users_immutable_check();

-- MATCHES: public read-only (writes via service role)
CREATE POLICY wc_matches_read ON wc_matches FOR SELECT USING (true);

-- TIPS: public can read all (for leaderboard transparency), owner writes own
-- DB-enforced lock: tips can only be inserted/updated BEFORE match kickoff
CREATE POLICY wc_tips_read       ON wc_tips FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY wc_tips_insert_own ON wc_tips FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = match_id) > NOW()
);
CREATE POLICY wc_tips_update_own ON wc_tips FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = match_id) > NOW()
);

-- BONUS: public can read (for transparency), owner writes own
-- DB-enforced lock: locked once the FIRST match (id=1) has kicked off
CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_read       ON wc_bonus FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_insert_own ON wc_bonus FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = 1) > NOW()
);
CREATE POLICY wc_bonus_update_own ON wc_bonus FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE id = 1) > NOW()
);

-- CRYPTO: public can read, owner writes own
-- DB-enforced lock: locked once the FINAL match has kicked off
CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_read       ON wc_crypto FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_insert_own ON wc_crypto FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE phase = 'final' ORDER BY kickoff_utc DESC LIMIT 1) > NOW()
);
CREATE POLICY wc_crypto_update_own ON wc_crypto FOR UPDATE USING (
  user_id IN (SELECT id FROM wc_users WHERE auth_id = auth.uid())
  AND (SELECT kickoff_utc FROM wc_matches WHERE phase = 'final' ORDER BY kickoff_utc DESC LIMIT 1) > NOW()
);

-- POOL & DONATIONS: public read-only
CREATE POLICY wc_pool_read      ON wc_pool      FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY wc_donations_read ON wc_donations FOR SELECT USING (true);

-- ============================================================
-- LEADERBOARD VIEW (computed aggregate)
-- ============================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW wc_leaderboard AS
SELECT
  u.id            AS user_id,
  u.username,
  u.avatar_country,
  (u.wallet IS NOT NULL AND u.wallet_verified) AS has_wallet,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0) + COALESCE(b.points, 0) AS total_points,
  COALESCE(SUM(t.points_final), 0) AS match_points,
  COALESCE(b.points, 0) AS bonus_points,
  COUNT(t.id) FILTER (WHERE t.resolved) AS matches_resolved
FROM wc_users u
LEFT JOIN wc_tips  t ON t.user_id = u.id
LEFT JOIN wc_bonus b ON b.user_id = u.id
GROUP BY u.id, u.username, u.avatar_country, u.wallet, u.wallet_verified, b.points;

GRANT SELECT ON wc_leaderboard TO anon, authenticated;

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/config.js?v=12"></script>
  <script src="./assets/countries.js?v=11"></script>
  <script src="./assets/auth.js?v=13"></script>
  <script src="./assets/profile.js?v=18"></script>
  <script src="./assets/avatar.js?v=7"></script>
  <script src="./assets/nav.js?v=6"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

    const LOCK_MIN = 15;

    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = $('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }

    function countryLookup(code){
      return window.WC_COUNTRIES.find(c => c.code === code);
    }

    function teamCell(code, alignRight, goals){
      if (!code || code === 'TBD') {
        return `<div class="team ${alignRight?'away':''}">
          <span class="fl">⚪</span><div class="nm-block"><span class="nm" style="color:var(--muted)">TBD</span></div>
        </div>`;
      }
      const c = countryLookup(code);
      const flag = c ? c.flag : '🏳';
      const name = c ? c.name : code;
      const scoreLine = (goals !== null && goals !== undefined) ? `<span class="gscore">${goals}</span>` : '';
      if (alignRight) {
        return `<div class="team away"><div class="nm-block"><span class="nm">${name}</span>${scoreLine}</div><span class="fl">${flag}</span></div>`;
      }
      return `<div class="team"><span class="fl">${flag}</span><div class="nm-block"><span class="nm">${name}</span>${scoreLine}</div></div>`;
    }

    function fmtKickoff(iso){
      const d = new Date(iso);
      // Local TZ for display
      const opts = { weekday:'short', month:'short', day:'numeric', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' };
      return d.toLocaleString(undefined, opts);
    }

    function isLocked(iso){
      const kickoff = new Date(iso).getTime();
      return Date.now() >= kickoff - LOCK_MIN * 60 * 1000;
    }

    function fmtPts(p){
      if (p == null) return '0';
      const n = Number(p);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0';
      return Number.isInteger(n) ? String(n) : (+n.toFixed(2)).toString();
    }
    function multiplierCls(m){
      // Class-safe token: '1.25' → 'x125', '1.5' → 'x15', integer → 'x<n>'
      const n = Number(m);
      if (n === 1.25) return 'x125';
      if (n === 1.5)  return 'x15';
      return 'x' + n;
    }
    function multiplierLabel(m){
      return `<span class="multi ${multiplierCls(m)}">×${fmtPts(m)}</span>`;
    }

    let allMatches = [];
    let myTips = {};
    let currentView = 'next48';
    let currentGroupLetter = 'all';
    const GROUP_LETTERS = ['A','B','C','D','E','F','G','H','I','J','K','L'];

    function hasTip(m){
      const t = myTips[m.id];
      return !!(t && Number.isInteger(t.tip_home) && Number.isInteger(t.tip_away));
    }

    function isNext48(m){
      if (m.team_home === 'TBD' || m.team_away === 'TBD') return false;
      if (isLocked(m.kickoff_utc)) return false;
      const t = new Date(m.kickoff_utc).getTime();
      const now = Date.now();
      return t > now - 60*1000 && t <= now + 48*60*60*1000; // ~now to +48h
    }

    function next48Untipped(){
      return allMatches
        .filter(m => isNext48(m) && !hasTip(m))
        .sort((a,b)=> new Date(a.kickoff_utc) - new Date(b.kickoff_utc));
    }

    function refreshBadge(){
      const b = $('#badge-next48');
      const n = next48Untipped().length;
      b.textContent = n ? String(n) : '';
    }

    function parseEmoji(){
      if (!window.twemoji) return;
      try { window.twemoji.parse($('#list'), { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    function render(){
      const list = $('#list');
      list.innerHTML = '';
      refreshBadge();
      // Re-parse twemoji after DOM is built — covers every early-return path.
      queueMicrotask(parseEmoji);

      if (currentView === 'next48') {
        const items = next48Untipped();
        if (!items.length) {
          // Differentiate: nothing scheduled vs. everything already tipped
          const anyIn48 = allMatches.some(isNext48);
          if (anyIn48) {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="alert ok" style="text-align:center;line-height:1.6">🎉 <b>You\'re up to date.</b><br><span style="opacity:.85">All matches in the next 48 hours are tipped. Switch to <b>Groups</b> / <b>R32</b> / … to plan further ahead.</span></div>';
          } else {
            list.innerHTML = '<div class="alert info" style="text-align:center;line-height:1.6">⏳ <b>No matches in the next 48 hours.</b><br><span style="opacity:.85">Check back closer to kickoff, or browse <b>Groups</b> / <b>R32</b> / … to place your tips early.</span></div>';
          }
          return;
        }
        const card = document.createElement('div');
        card.className = 'group-card';
        card.innerHTML = `<div class="group-head"><div class="group-title">⏰ Next 48 hours</div><div class="group-meta">${items.length} ${items.length===1?'match':'matches'} to tip</div></div><div class="rows"></div>`;
        const rows = card.querySelector('.rows');
        items.forEach((m, idx) => rows.appendChild(buildMatchRow(m, idx===0)));
        list.appendChild(card);
        return;
      }

      let filtered;
      if (currentView === 'group') {
        filtered = allMatches.filter(m => m.phase === 'group');
        // group by group_letter
        const byG = {};
        filtered.forEach(m => {
          byG[m.group_letter] = byG[m.group_letter] || [];
          byG[m.group_letter].push(m);
        });
        const allLetters = Object.keys(byG).sort();
        if (!allLetters.length) {
          list.innerHTML = '<div class="alert info">No matches loaded yet. Run the Phase D seed in Supabase.</div>';
          return;
        }
        const letters = currentGroupLetter === 'all'
          ? allLetters
          : allLetters.filter(L => L === currentGroupLetter);
        letters.forEach(letter => {
          const card = document.createElement('div');
          card.className = 'group-card';
          card.innerHTML = `
            <div class="group-head">
              <div class="group-title">Group ${letter}</div>
              <div class="group-meta">${byG[letter].length} matches</div>
            </div>
            <div class="rows"></div>`;
          const rows = card.querySelector('.rows');
          byG[letter].forEach((m, idx) => {
            rows.appendChild(buildMatchRow(m, idx === 0));
          });
          list.appendChild(card);
        });
      } else {
        filtered = allMatches.filter(m => m.phase === currentView);
        if (!filtered.length) {
          list.innerHTML = '<div class="alert info">No matches in this phase yet.</div>';
          return;
        }
        const card = document.createElement('div');
        card.className = 'group-card';
        const phaseName = ({ r32:'Round of 32', r16:'Round of 16', qf:'Quarter-Finals', sf:'Semi-Finals', '3rd':'Third Place', final:'Final' })[currentView] || currentView.toUpperCase();
        card.innerHTML = `<div class="group-head"><div class="group-title">${phaseName}</div><div class="group-meta">${filtered.length} matches</div></div><div class="rows"></div>`;
        const rows = card.querySelector('.rows');
        filtered.forEach((m, idx) => rows.appendChild(buildMatchRow(m, idx === 0)));
        list.appendChild(card);
      }
    }

    // KO matches decided after 90 min: tips score on the 90-min goals, this chip
    // just surfaces the decider (penalties / extra time) next to the result.
    function deciderChip(m){
      if (m.final_home == null || m.final_away == null) return '';
      if (m.decided_by === 'pens') return `<span class="decider">🥅 ${m.final_home - m.goals_home}:${m.final_away - m.goals_away} pen</span>`;
      if (m.decided_by === 'aet')  return `<span class="decider">⏱ ${m.final_home}:${m.final_away} a.e.t.</span>`;
      return '';
    }

    function buildMatchRow(m, isFirst){
      const wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.className = 'match-row' + (isFirst ? ' first' : '');
      const tip = myTips[m.id];
      const locked = isLocked(m.kickoff_utc);
      const resolved = !!(tip && tip.resolved);
      const played = m.played && m.goals_home != null && m.goals_away != null;

      const status = resolved
        ? `<span class="resolved">✓ resolved <span class="points-pill">+${fmtPts(tip.points_final)}</span></span>`
        : locked
          ? `<span class="lockicon">🔒 locked</span>`
          : `<span class="open">⏱ open</span>`;

      wrap.innerHTML = `
        <div class="match-meta">
          <span>${fmtKickoff(m.kickoff_utc)}</span>
          ${multiplierLabel(m.multiplier)}
          ${status}
          ${played ? deciderChip(m) : ''}
        </div>
        ${teamCell(m.team_home, false, played ? m.goals_home : null)}
        <div class="score-cell">
          <input class="score-input s-home" type="number" min="0" max="30" inputmode="numeric"
                 value="${tip ? tip.tip_home : ''}" ${locked ? 'disabled' : ''} placeholder="–">
          <span class="score-dash">:</span>
          <input class="score-input s-away" type="number" min="0" max="30" inputmode="numeric"
                 value="${tip ? tip.tip_away : ''}" ${locked ? 'disabled' : ''} placeholder="–">
        </div>
        ${teamCell(m.team_away, true, played ? m.goals_away : null)}
        <div class="match-meta" style="padding-top:2px"><span class="save-tag ${locked?'locked':''}" data-mid="${m.id}">${locked?'locked':''}</span></div>
      `;

      // disable inputs if either team is TBD
      if (m.team_home === 'TBD' || m.team_away === 'TBD') {
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.score-input').forEach(i => { i.disabled = true; i.placeholder = '–'; });
        wrap.querySelector('.save-tag').textContent = 'awaiting draw';
      }

      const inH = wrap.querySelector('.s-home');
      const inA = wrap.querySelector('.s-away');
      const tag = wrap.querySelector('.save-tag');

      const trySave = async () => {
        const vH = inH.value.trim(), vA = inA.value.trim();
        if (vH === '' || vA === '') return;
        const nh = parseInt(vH, 10), na = parseInt(vA, 10);
        if (!Number.isInteger(nh) || !Number.isInteger(na)) return;
        tag.className = 'save-tag dirty';
        tag.textContent = 'saving…';
        const r = await window.WC_PROFILE.saveTip(m.id, nh, na);
        if (r.error) {
          tag.className = 'save-tag err';
          tag.textContent = 'failed';
          showAlert('error', `Match ${m.id}: ${r.error}`);
          return;
        }
        myTips[m.id] = { match_id: m.id, tip_home: nh, tip_away: na, points_final: 0, resolved: false };
        tag.className = 'save-tag ok';
        tag.textContent = '✓ saved';
        refreshBadge();
        setTimeout(()=>{
          if (tag.textContent === '✓ saved') tag.textContent = '';
          tag.className = 'save-tag';
          // On the Next-48h view, the just-tipped match should drop out of the list
          if (currentView === 'next48') render();
        }, 1800);
      };

      inH.addEventListener('change', trySave);
      inA.addEventListener('change', trySave);
      inH.addEventListener('blur', trySave);
      inA.addEventListener('blur', trySave);

      return wrap;
    }

    function updateGroupSubBarVisibility(){
      $('#group-sub').classList.toggle('hidden', currentView !== 'group');
    }

    function buildGroupSubBar(){
      const bar = $('#group-sub');
      if (!bar.querySelector('[data-letter="A"]')) {
        GROUP_LETTERS.forEach(L => {
          const btn = document.createElement('button');
          btn.dataset.letter = L;
          btn.textContent = L;
          bar.appendChild(btn);
        });
      }
      bar.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.letter === currentGroupLetter);
      });
      bar.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
        b.onclick = () => {
          currentGroupLetter = b.dataset.letter;
          buildGroupSubBar();
          render();
        };
      });
    }

    $$('#phase-bar button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      $$('#phase-bar button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      b.classList.add('active');
      currentView = b.dataset.view;
      updateGroupSubBarVisibility();
      render();
    }));

    (async ()=>{
      buildGroupSubBar();
      updateGroupSubBarVisibility();
      const me = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      if (!me) { window.location.href = '/worldcup/'; return; }
      const [mr, tr] = await Promise.all([
        window.WC_PROFILE.loadMatches(),
        window.WC_PROFILE.loadMyTips(),
      ]);
      if (mr.error) { showAlert('error', mr.error); return; }
      if (tr.error) { showAlert('error', tr.error); return; }
      allMatches = mr.matches;
      myTips = tr.tips;
      render();
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2/dist/umd/supabase.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/config.js?v=12"></script>
  <script src="./assets/countries.js?v=11"></script>
  <script src="./assets/auth.js?v=13"></script>
  <script src="./assets/profile.js?v=18"></script>
  <script src="./assets/projection.js?v=5"></script>
  <script src="./assets/avatar.js?v=7"></script>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/twemoji@14.0.2/dist/twemoji.min.js"></script>
  <script src="./assets/twemojify.js?v=2"></script>
  <script>
    const $ = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = $('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }
    function findCountry(code){ return window.WC_COUNTRIES.find(c => c.code === code) || null; }
    function fmtKickoff(iso){
      return new Date(iso).toLocaleString(undefined, { weekday:'short', month:'short', day:'numeric', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' });
    }
    function fmtPts(p){
      if (p == null) return '0';
      const n = Number(p);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0';
      return Number.isInteger(n) ? String(n) : (+n.toFixed(2)).toString();
    }
    // KO decider suffix (penalties / extra time). Tips score on the 90-min goals.
    function koDecider(m){
      if (m.final_home == null || m.final_away == null) return '';
      if (m.decided_by === 'pens') return ` (${m.final_home - m.goals_home}:${m.final_away - m.goals_away} pen)`;
      if (m.decided_by === 'aet')  return ` (${m.final_home}:${m.final_away} a.e.t.)`;
      return '';
    }
    function teamCell(code, alignRight){
      if (!code || code === 'TBD') {
        return `<div class="team ${alignRight?'away':''}"><span class="fl">⚪</span><span class="nm" style="color:var(--muted)">TBD</span></div>`;
      }
      const c = findCountry(code);
      const flag = c ? c.flag : '🏳';
      const name = c ? c.name : code;
      if (alignRight) return `<div class="team away"><span class="nm">${name}</span><span class="fl">${flag}</span></div>`;
      return `<div class="team"><span class="fl">${flag}</span><span class="nm">${name}</span></div>`;
    }

    /* Parse ?u= */
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const targetUsername = (params.get('u') || '').trim();

    let myProfile = null;        // current logged-in user
    let target = null;           // wc_users row of the user we're viewing
    let isSelf = false;

    /* Match-tip + bonus + crypto reveal rules:
       - Own picks: always visible
       - Others' match tips: only after each match kickoff (= 15min-locked)
       - Others' bonus picks: only after BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC (final edit lock)
       - Others' crypto picks: only after BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC (final edit lock)
       Even though the DB currently exposes them publicly, we honour these
       rules in the UI so visitors can't copy-tip before lock. */
    let firstMatchKickoff = null;
    let finalKickoff = null;
    const BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_MS = new Date(window.WC_CONFIG.BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC).getTime();

    function canSeeTipFor(match){
      if (isSelf) return true;
      return Date.now() >= new Date(match.kickoff_utc).getTime();
    }
    function canSeeBonus(){
      if (isSelf) return true;
      return Date.now() >= BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_MS;
    }
    function canSeeCrypto(){
      if (isSelf) return true;
      // Crypto predictions lock at BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_UTC (final edit deadline),
      // so reveal them publicly at that moment.
      return Date.now() >= BONUS_CRYPTO_LOCK_MS;
    }

    async function loadTarget(){
      const sb = window.WC_SB;
      if (!targetUsername) { showAlert('error', 'No user specified. Add ?u=USERNAME to the URL.'); return false; }
      const { data, error } = await sb.from('wc_users')
        .select('id, username, avatar_country, wallet')
        .ilike('username', targetUsername)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (error || !data) { showAlert('error', 'Player "' + targetUsername + '" not found.'); return false; }
      target = data;
      return true;
    }

    function renderHeader(){
      const c = findCountry(target.avatar_country);
      $('#t-ava').innerHTML = window.WC_AVATAR.avatarHtml(target.avatar_country, 'lg');
      $('#t-uname').textContent = target.username;
      $('#t-country').textContent = c ? c.name : 'no country';
      const wb = $('#t-wallet-badge');
      if (target.wallet) wb.outerHTML = '<span id="t-wallet-badge" class="badge gold">⚽ wallet linked</span>';
      else               wb.outerHTML = '<span id="t-wallet-badge" class="badge muted">no wallet</span>';
      $('#t-self-badge').classList.toggle('hidden', !isSelf);
      document.title = 'BOBAI Worldcup \'26 — ' + target.username;
    }

    async function loadStats(){
      const sb = window.WC_SB;
      const [{ data: lb }, { count: totalUsers }, { count: tipCount }, { data: bonusRow }] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('wc_leaderboard').select('user_id,total_points').order('total_points', { ascending: false }).limit(500),
        sb.from('wc_users').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
        sb.from('wc_tips').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('user_id', target.id),
        sb.from('wc_bonus').select('champion,most_goals_team,fewest_goals_team,red_cards_bracket,topscorer_country').eq('user_id', target.id).maybeSingle(),
      ]);
      let pts = 0, rank = '—';
      if (lb && lb.length) {
        const idx = lb.findIndex(r => r.user_id === target.id);
        if (idx >= 0) { pts = lb[idx].total_points || 0; rank = idx + 1; }
      }
      $('#s-total').textContent = fmtPts(pts);
      $('#s-rank').textContent = rank;
      $('#s-rank-sub').textContent = 'of ' + (totalUsers || 1) + ' player' + ((totalUsers || 1) === 1 ? '' : 's');
      $('#s-tips').textContent = tipCount || 0;
      const VALID_BRACKETS = new Set(['<5','5-9','10-14','15-19','>19']);
      const isValidBonus = (k, v) => v && (k !== 'red_cards_bracket' || VALID_BRACKETS.has(v));
      const bonusFilled = bonusRow
        ? ['champion','most_goals_team','fewest_goals_team','red_cards_bracket','topscorer_country']
            .filter(k => isValidBonus(k, bonusRow[k])).length
        : 0;
      $('#s-bonus').textContent = bonusFilled;
    }

    async function loadProjection(){
      if (!target || !window.WC_PROJECTION) return;
      window.WC_PROJECTION.injectStyles();
      const box = $('#proj-content');
      try {
        const proj = await window.WC_PROJECTION.loadAndCompute(target.id, target.wallet);
        box.innerHTML = window.WC_PROJECTION.renderHtml(proj);
      } catch (e) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="proj-empty">Projection unavailable.</div>';
      }
    }

    /* ============== TIPS ============== */
    async function renderTipsPane(){
      const sb = window.WC_SB;
      const [{ data: matches }, { data: tips }] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('wc_matches').select('id, phase, group_letter, team_home, team_away, kickoff_utc, goals_home, goals_away, final_home, final_away, decided_by, played').order('kickoff_utc', { ascending: true }),
        sb.from('wc_tips').select('match_id, tip_home, tip_away, points_final, resolved').eq('user_id', target.id),
      ]);
      // pick first kickoff + final kickoff (used by reveal helpers)
      if (matches && matches.length) {
        const first = matches.find(m => m.id === 1);
        firstMatchKickoff = first ? new Date(first.kickoff_utc).getTime() : null;
        const finals = matches.filter(m => m.phase === 'final').sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.kickoff_utc)-new Date(a.kickoff_utc));
        finalKickoff = finals[0] ? new Date(finals[0].kickoff_utc).getTime() : null;
      }
      const byMatch = {};
      (tips || []).forEach(t => { byMatch[t.match_id] = t; });

      const pane = $('#pane-tips');
      if (!matches || !matches.length) { pane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state">No matches loaded yet.</div>'; return; }

      let visibleTipCount = 0;
      let html = '';
      let lastPhase = null;
      matches.forEach((m, i) => {
        const t = byMatch[m.id];
        if (!t) return;
        const visible = canSeeTipFor(m);
        if (visible) visibleTipCount++;
        const phaseLabel = m.phase === 'group' ? ('Group ' + m.group_letter) : m.phase.toUpperCase();
        if (m.phase !== lastPhase) {
          html += `<div style="margin:14px 0 6px;font-family:'Space Grotesk';font-weight:600;font-size:12px;color:var(--gold);letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase">${m.phase.toUpperCase()}</div>`;
          lastPhase = m.phase;
        }
        let scoreCell;
        if (!visible) {
          scoreCell = `<span class="score-pill locked">🔒 reveals at kickoff</span>`;
        } else {
          const points = m.played && t.points_final != null ? `<span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--gold);margin-left:6px">+${fmtPts(t.points_final)}p</span>` : '';
          scoreCell = `<span class="score-pill">${t.tip_home} – ${t.tip_away}${points}</span>`;
        }
        html += `<div class="tip-row">
          <div class="tip-meta">${phaseLabel} · ${fmtKickoff(m.kickoff_utc)}${m.played ? ' · played '+m.goals_home+'–'+m.goals_away+koDecider(m) : ''}</div>
          ${teamCell(m.team_home, false)}
          <div>${scoreCell}</div>
          ${teamCell(m.team_away, true)}
        </div>`;
      });
      if (!html) {
        pane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state">No match tips placed yet.</div>';
      } else {
        if (!isSelf) {
          const total = (tips || []).length;
          const hidden = total - visibleTipCount;
          if (hidden > 0) {
            html = `<div class="locked-cell" style="margin-bottom:12px">${hidden} of ${total} tips are still hidden — they reveal one by one as each match kicks off.</div>` + html;
          }
        }
        pane.innerHTML = html;
      }
      if (window.twemoji) try { window.twemoji.parse(pane, { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    /* ============== BONUS ============== */
    async function renderBonusPane(){
      const pane = $('#pane-bonus');
      if (!canSeeBonus()) {
        pane.innerHTML = '<div class="locked-cell">🔒 Bonus picks reveal after the final lock (18 June 2026, 15:45 UTC).</div>';
        return;
      }
      const sb = window.WC_SB;
      const { data, error } = await sb.from('wc_bonus')
        .select('champion,most_goals_team,fewest_goals_team,red_cards_bracket,topscorer_country,points,resolved')
        .eq('user_id', target.id).maybeSingle();
      if (error) { pane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state">Could not load bonus picks.</div>'; return; }
      if (!data) { pane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state">No bonus picks yet.</div>'; return; }
      const cn = code => { const c = findCountry(code); return c ? `${c.flag} ${c.name}` : (code || '—'); };
      // Final results (resolved 2026-07-20): Champion ES · Most goals FR+ENG (tie, 20 each) ·
      // Fewest goals PA (0) · Red cards 15-19 · Top scorer FR (Mbappé). Correct picks render green +20.
      const BONUS_CORRECT = { champion:['ES'], most_goals_team:['FR','ENG'], fewest_goals_team:['PA'], red_cards_bracket:['15-19'], topscorer_country:['FR'] };
      const hit  = f => !!(data.resolved && BONUS_CORRECT[f].includes(data[f]));
      const vst  = f => hit(f) ? ' style="color:#5fffa0;font-weight:700"' : '';
      const chip = f => hit(f) ? ' <span style="color:#5fffa0;font-size:10px;font-weight:700">+20</span>' : '';
      pane.innerHTML = `
        <div class="pick-list">
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Champion</span><span class="val"${vst('champion')}>${cn(data.champion)}${chip('champion')}</span></div>
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Most goals (team)</span><span class="val"${vst('most_goals_team')}>${cn(data.most_goals_team)}${chip('most_goals_team')}</span></div>
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Fewest goals (team)</span><span class="val"${vst('fewest_goals_team')}>${cn(data.fewest_goals_team)}${chip('fewest_goals_team')}</span></div>
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Red-cards bracket</span><span class="val"${vst('red_cards_bracket')}>${(['<5','5-9','10-14','15-19','>19'].includes(data.red_cards_bracket)) ? data.red_cards_bracket : '—'}${chip('red_cards_bracket')}</span></div>
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Top scorer (country)</span><span class="val"${vst('topscorer_country')}>${cn(data.topscorer_country)}${chip('topscorer_country')}</span></div>
          ${data.resolved ? `<div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Bonus points</span><span class="val" style="color:var(--gold)">${data.points || 0}</span></div>` : ''}
        </div>`;
      if (window.twemoji) try { window.twemoji.parse(pane, { folder:'svg', ext:'.svg', base:'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@14.0.2/assets/' }); } catch(e){}
    }

    /* ============== CRYPTO ============== */
    async function renderCryptoPane(){
      const pane = $('#pane-crypto');
      if (!canSeeCrypto()) {
        pane.innerHTML = '<div class="locked-cell">🔒 Crypto predictions reveal after the final lock (18 June 2026, 15:45 UTC).</div>';
        return;
      }
      const sb = window.WC_SB;
      const { data, error } = await sb.from('wc_crypto')
        .select('btc_price,bnb_price,bobai_price,btc_diff,bnb_diff,bobai_diff,resolved')
        .eq('user_id', target.id).maybeSingle();
      if (error) { pane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state">Could not load crypto picks.</div>'; return; }
      if (!data) { pane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state">No crypto picks yet.</div>'; return; }
      const fmt = (v, dp) => v == null ? '—' : '$' + Number(v).toFixed(dp);
      // Crypto pots resolved 2026-07-20 against the frozen Final-kickoff prices
      // (BTC 64420.00 · BNB 567.47 · BOBAI 0.0000553232). Winner-take-all per coin,
      // closest wallet-linked guess with BOBAI holdings wins (26x cap): BTC _PW (12) ·
      // BNB prince27 (59) · BOBAI Beso2025 (40) — Havy was closer but holds 0 BOBAI → cap 0 → next closest.
      const CRYPTO_WINNERS = { btc: 12, bnb: 59, bobai: 40 };
      const cw   = coin => !!(data.resolved && CRYPTO_WINNERS[coin] === target.id);
      const cst  = coin => cw(coin) ? ' style="color:#5fffa0;font-weight:700"' : '';
      const ctag = coin => cw(coin) ? ' <span style="color:#5fffa0;font-size:10px;font-weight:700">🏆 closest — wins the pot</span>' : '';
      pane.innerHTML = `
        <div class="pick-list">
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Bitcoin (BTC)</span><span class="val"${cst('btc')}>${fmt(data.btc_price, 2)}${ctag('btc')}</span></div>
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">BNB</span><span class="val"${cst('bnb')}>${fmt(data.bnb_price, 4)}${ctag('bnb')}</span></div>
          <div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">BOBAI</span><span class="val"${cst('bobai')}>${fmt(data.bobai_price, 10)}${ctag('bobai')}</span></div>
          ${data.resolved ? `<div class="pick-item"><span class="lbl">Absolute diffs</span><span class="val">${fmt(data.btc_diff,2)} · ${fmt(data.bnb_diff,4)} · ${fmt(data.bobai_diff,10)}</span></div>` : ''}
        </div>`;
    }

    /* Tab switching */
    let loadedPanes = { tips: false, bonus: false, crypto: false };
    $$('#pick-tabs button').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const v = btn.dataset.view;
        $$('#pick-tabs button').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('active', x === btn));
        $('#pane-tips').classList.toggle('hidden', v !== 'tips');
        $('#pane-bonus').classList.toggle('hidden', v !== 'bonus');
        $('#pane-crypto').classList.toggle('hidden', v !== 'crypto');
        if (!loadedPanes[v]) {
          loadedPanes[v] = true;
          if (v === 'tips') await renderTipsPane();
          if (v === 'bonus') await renderBonusPane();
          if (v === 'crypto') await renderCryptoPane();
        }
      });
    });

    (async () => {
      myProfile = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      // Public archive page — anonymous visitors can view any profile, myProfile stays null.

      const ok = await loadTarget();
      if (!ok) return;

      isSelf = !!(myProfile && myProfile.id === target.id);
      renderHeader();
      await loadStats();
      loadProjection();  // async, populates the projected-payout card
      // load default (tips) pane
      loadedPanes.tips = true;
      await renderTipsPane();
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

    /* On the landing page itself, "Sign in · Register" should scroll to the auth
       form below instead of reloading. nav.js writes those links after fetching
       auth state, so we re-target them once they exist. */
    (function retargetAuthLinks(){
      const tryFix = () => {
        const userEl = document.querySelector('.user');
        if (!userEl) return false;
        const links = userEl.querySelectorAll('a[href="/worldcup/"]');
        if (!links.length) return false;
        links.forEach(a => { a.setAttribute('href', '#auth'); });
        return true;
      };
      if (tryFix()) return;
      // nav.js runs an async profile-fetch; poll briefly until it has rewritten the slot.
      let tries = 0;
      const t = setInterval(() => {
        if (tryFix() || ++tries > 40) clearInterval(t);
      }, 100);
    })();

  <script>
    /* Countdown */
    const KICKOFF = new Date('2026-07-19T19:00:00Z').getTime();
    function $(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
    function renderCountdown(){
      const diff = KICKOFF - Date.now();
      if(diff <= 0){
        $('k-d').textContent = $('k-h').textContent = $('k-m').textContent = $('k-s').textContent = '00';
        return;
      }
      const d = Math.floor(diff / 86400000);
      const h = Math.floor((diff % 86400000) / 3600000);
      const m = Math.floor((diff % 3600000) / 60000);
      const s = Math.floor((diff % 60000) / 1000);
      $('k-d').textContent = String(d).padStart(2,'0');
      $('k-h').textContent = String(h).padStart(2,'0');
      $('k-m').textContent = String(m).padStart(2,'0');
      $('k-s').textContent = String(s).padStart(2,'0');
    }
    renderCountdown();
    setInterval(renderCountdown, 1000);

    /* Live prize-pool ticker — public anon read on wc_pool */
    function fmtBobai(n){
      if (n >= 1e9) return (n/1e9).toFixed(2)+'B';
      if (n >= 1e6) return (n/1e6).toFixed(2)+'M';
      if (n >= 1e3) return (n/1e3).toFixed(1)+'K';
      return Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US');
    }
    function fmtUsd(n){
      if (n >= 1000) return '$'+Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US');
      return '$'+n.toFixed(2);
    }
    // wc_pool is a frozen WC26 archive snapshot — the BOBAI amount never changes,
    // but the USD equivalent keeps tracking the live GeckoTerminal price (the
    // stored snapshot price is only the fallback when the feed is down).
    let livePx = 0;
    async function fetchLivePx(){
      try {
        const r = await fetch('https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/bsc/pools/0x6eadd4cb786898b34929444988380ed0cc6fd9a6');
        const j = await r.json();
        const v = parseFloat(j?.data?.attributes?.base_token_price_usd) || 0;
        if (v > 0) livePx = v;
      } catch (_) {/* keep last good value */}
      return livePx;
    }
    async function loadPool(){
      try {
        const cfg = window.WC_CONFIG;
        const url = `${cfg.SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/wc_pool?id=eq.1&select=total_bobai,bobai_price_usd`;
        const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { apikey: cfg.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, Accept: 'application/json' } });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const rows = await res.json();
        const p = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : null;
        if (!p) return;
        const total = parseFloat(p.total_bobai) || 0;
        const live = await fetchLivePx();
        const price = live > 0 ? live : (parseFloat(p.bobai_price_usd) || 0);
        $('pool-bobai').textContent = fmtBobai(total);
        $('pool-usd').textContent = price > 0 ? fmtUsd(total * price) : 'n/a';
        document.getElementById('pool-block').classList.remove('loading');
      } catch (_) {/* silent — block stays hidden in loading state */}
    }
    loadPool();
    setInterval(loadPool, 30000);

    /* Auth form (mirrors /worldcup/app/index.html) */
    const $$ = (s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];
    const qs = (s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);

    function showAlert(type, msg){
      const el = qs('#alert');
      el.innerHTML = msg ? `<div class="alert ${type}">${msg}</div>` : '';
    }

    /* Render country grid */
    (function renderCountries(){
      const grid = qs('#country-grid');
      const cInput = qs('#country-input');
      if (!grid || !window.WC_COUNTRIES) return;
      window.WC_COUNTRIES.forEach(c => {
        const b = document.createElement('button');
        b.type = 'button';
        b.className = 'country-cell';
        b.dataset.code = c.code;
        b.innerHTML = `<span class="fl">${c.flag}</span><span class="nm">${c.name}</span>`;
        b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
          $$('.country-cell').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));
          b.classList.add('sel');
          cInput.value = c.code;
        });
        grid.appendChild(b);
      });
    })();

    /* Tab switching */
    function showPane(which){
      $$('.tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active', x.dataset.tab===which));
      qs('#form-login').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='login');
      qs('#form-register').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='register');
      qs('#form-forgot').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='forgot');
      showAlert();
    }
    $$('.tab').forEach(t=>t.addEventListener('click',()=>showPane(t.dataset.tab)));
    qs('#link-forgot').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ e.preventDefault(); showPane('forgot'); });
    qs('#link-back-login').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ e.preventDefault(); showPane('login'); });

    /* Login */
    qs('#form-login').addEventListener('submit', async (e)=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Signing in…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.login({
        identifier: fd.get('identifier'),
        password: fd.get('password'),
      });
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Sign in';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); return; }
      window.location.href = './app/dashboard.html';
    });

    /* Register — open the security-briefing modal first, then actually register */
    const regModal = qs('#register-modal');
    const regAck = qs('#reg-ack');
    const regGo = qs('#reg-go');
    const regCancel = qs('#reg-cancel');
    let pendingRegister = null; // { username, email, password, passwordConfirm, country }

    function openRegModal(){
      regAck.checked = false;
      regGo.disabled = true;
      regGo.textContent = 'Create my account';
      regModal.classList.add('open');
      regModal.querySelector('.modal-body').scrollTop = 0;
    }
    function closeRegModal(){
      regModal.classList.remove('open');
    }

    regAck.addEventListener('change', ()=>{ regGo.disabled = !regAck.checked; });
    regCancel.addEventListener('click', ()=>{ closeRegModal(); pendingRegister = null; });
    regModal.addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ if (e.target === regModal) { closeRegModal(); pendingRegister = null; } });

    qs('#form-register').addEventListener('submit', (e)=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      if (!fd.get('country')) {
        showAlert('error', 'Please pick your country flag below.');
        document.getElementById('country-grid').scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' });
        return;
      }
      pendingRegister = {
        username: fd.get('username'),
        email: fd.get('email'),
        password: fd.get('password'),
        passwordConfirm: fd.get('password_confirm'),
        country: fd.get('country'),
      };
      openRegModal();
    });

    regGo.addEventListener('click', async ()=>{
      if (!regAck.checked || !pendingRegister) return;
      regGo.disabled = true; regGo.textContent = 'Creating…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.register(pendingRegister);
      regGo.disabled = false; regGo.textContent = 'Create my account';
      if (r.error) {
        closeRegModal();
        showAlert('error', r.error);
        return;
      }
      const email = pendingRegister.email;
      pendingRegister = null;
      closeRegModal();
      if (r.needsConfirmation) {
        const safeEmail = email.replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
        showAlert('ok', `📧 <b>Check your email</b> — we sent a confirmation link to <b style="word-break:break-all">${safeEmail}</b>. Click the link to activate your account, then sign in below.<br><br>⚠ <b>Don't see it?</b> Check your <b>spam / junk</b> folder — confirmation mails sometimes land there.`);
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          const alertEl = document.getElementById('alert');
          if (!alertEl) return;
          // Measure entire <nav> (includes .nav 58px + .tabbar 37px on landing page)
          const navEl = document.querySelector('nav');
          const navH = navEl ? navEl.offsetHeight : 58;
          const top = alertEl.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - navH - 16;
          window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, top), behavior: 'smooth' });
        });
        return;
      }
      showAlert('ok', 'Account created — redirecting…');
      setTimeout(()=>{ window.location.href = './app/dashboard.html'; }, 700);
    });

    /* Forgot password */
    qs('#form-forgot').addEventListener('submit', async (e)=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      showAlert();
      const fd = new FormData(e.target);
      const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending…';
      const r = await window.WC_AUTH.resetPasswordRequest(fd.get('email'));
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Send reset link';
      if (r.error) { showAlert('error', r.error); return; }
      showAlert('ok', 'If an account exists for that email, a reset link has been sent. Check your inbox.');
    });

    /* If already logged in, jump straight to the app dashboard */
    (async ()=>{
      const p = await window.WC_AUTH.currentProfile();
      if (p) window.location.href = './app/dashboard.html';
    })();
  </script>
